### BT17-021 — Labramon

#### Card summary

Digimon, Lv.3, Blue, Rookie, Vaccine, Beast, play cost 3, 1000 DP,
evolution cost `Blue Lv.2: 0`.

Printed clauses:

1. `[On Play] By placing 1 [Seasarmon] or 1 level 3 blue Digimon card from your hand
   as this Digimon's bottom digivolution card, ＜Draw 1＞.`
2. Inherited: `[When Attacking] [Once Per Turn] If this Digimon has ＜Jamming＞,
   gain 1 memory.`

#### Rulings covered

`node tools/kb/query.mjs card BT17-021` returns "(no knowledge-base entries)", matching
`KB-INDEX.md` (0 Q&A, no errata, not on the banlist). Nothing to cover.

#### Clause -> test -> IR mapping

| Clause | IR | Test |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Catalog identity, both clauses present | `compiled.effects` (two entries) | "matches every catalog field and carries both printed clauses in IR" |
| `[On Play]` | `trigger: "OnPlay"` | both placement-branch runs, driven by the public `playCard` intent |
| `1 [Seasarmon]` | `nameOrTrait: [{ tokens: ["Seasarmon"], match: "name" }]` | "places the named [Seasarmon] branch ... and draws 1" (BT17-024) |
| `or 1 level 3 blue Digimon card` | `orFilters: [{ colors: ["Blue"], levels: [3], kind: ["Digimon"] }]` | "places the level 3 blue branch ... and draws 1" (BT1-028 Elecmon) |
| `from your hand` / filter boundaries | `zone: "hand"`, `controller: "mine"` | "offers only the [Seasarmon] and level 3 blue cards as placement candidates" — the `selectCards` candidate set contains Seasarmon and the Lv.3 blue, and excludes a Lv.3 red (BT1-009) and a Lv.4 blue (BT2-024) |
| `as this Digimon's bottom digivolution card` | `destination: "digivolutionStack"`, `position: "bottom"`, `host: "self"` | placement runs assert `stack` equals exactly the placed instance id |
| `By placing ... , ＜Draw 1＞` (cost gates the draw) | `optional: true`, `abortOnDecline: true` | "plays without drawing when the hand holds no legal placement source" and "neither places nor draws when the optional effect is declined" |
| `[When Attacking]`, inherited | `trigger: "WhenAttacking"`, `isInherited: true` | "gains 1 memory once per turn when its ＜Jamming＞ host attacks" |
| `If this Digimon has ＜Jamming＞` | `condition: { kind: "selfHasKeyword", keyword: "Jamming" }` | "does not gain memory when the host lacks ＜Jamming＞" |
| `[Once Per Turn]` | `frequency: "OncePerTurn"` | same-turn refusal in the Jamming test; next-own-turn reset in "resets the once-per-turn memory gain on the next own turn through the public turn flow" |
| Evolution requirement `Blue Lv.2: 0` | `digivolutionRequirement` from the catalog `evoCosts` | "digivolves from a blue Lv.2 source in the breeding / battle area for 0 memory and draws 1" and "refuses an off-colour Lv.2 source" |

Endpoints on the placement runs: hand holds exactly the drawn card, deck holds exactly
the untouched second card, `stack` is exactly `[materialId]`, memory is 2 (play cost 3
from 5 — the placement is a card cost, not a memory cost), and `state.pendingDecision`
is undefined. The no-source and declined runs assert an empty stack, an untouched hand
and deck, and the same memory endpoint, which is what separates "the cost was skipped"
from "the cost was paid and the draw silently lost".

The once-per-turn evidence uses the real turn loop (`engine.startTurnLoop()` plus
`advance(engine).waitForMainPhase(seat)`): attack, memory `+1`; unsuspend (the only
injected plumbing, and explicitly allowed) and attack again in the same turn, memory
unchanged; pass through seat 1's turn back to seat 0; attack again, memory `+1`. Memory
is asserted as deltas because the gauge is stored turn-relative and re-framed on pass.

Evolution is proved through the public `digivolve` intent from both legal locations
(breeding and battle area), using BT13-002 Chapmon, a blue Lv.2 Digi-Egg whose only
clause is an `[Opponent's Turn]` window and so is inert on seat 0's turn. Endpoints:
memory unchanged at 5 (the route costs 0), `stack` is exactly the base card's instance
id so source-stack identity survives, the top card is the Labramon instance, the bonus
draw moved exactly one named card from deck to hand, and `s.decisions` is empty — which
also proves `[On Play]` does not fire on a digivolve. The illegal-source run uses
BT17-004 Argomon, a green Lv.2 egg: the intent is rejected with
`{ ok: false, reason: "invalid-evolution" }` and the base, hand, deck and memory are all
unchanged.

The ＜Jamming＞ source is BT17-024 Seasarmon sitting in the digivolution stack, whose
inherited clause is a bare ＜Jamming＞ grant; the test asserts the keyword is present
(and absent in the negative) with `observe(...).hasKeyword` before attacking.

#### Commands and results

```
pnpm --filter @aegis/api exec vitest run src/cards/BT17/BT17-021.test.ts --maxWorkers=1 --no-file-parallelism
  => 1 file, 12 tests passed
pnpm --filter @aegis/api typecheck                      => clean
pnpm exec oxlint <file>; pnpm exec oxfmt --check <file>  => clean
git diff --check -- <file>                               => clean
```

#### Defects fixed locally

- No catalog assertion and no IR equality existed; both were added.
- The `or 1 level 3 blue Digimon card` branch was never exercised — only the named
  Seasarmon route was. The `orFilters` clause was asserted structurally and nothing
  else. It now has its own behavioural run plus a candidate-set boundary test.
- Nothing proved the cost gates the draw: there was no "no legal source" run and no
  declined run, so `abortOnDecline` was unproven.
- `[Once Per Turn]` was asserted only as an IR string. There was no same-turn refusal
  and no reset through the turn loop.
- The `[When Attacking]` condition had no negative: a host without ＜Jamming＞ was
  never attacked with.
- The old inherited fixture used the numeric `security: 2` form, one of the smells
  named in the brief. All fixtures now list explicit inert main-deck Digimon.
- The evolution route was entirely untested: no legal digivolve, no cost proof, no
  source-stack identity, no bonus draw and no illegal-source negative. All added.
- The turn-loop run needed a spare playable card in hand for each seat; without it
  Main auto-passes as soon as the attacker suspends, which silently ended the turn
  and made the memory assertion read the re-framed gauge. Documented in a comment.

#### Retained reds / seams

None.

#### Remaining gaps

- The card has a single printed evolution route, so there is no alternate-cost or
  DNA route to prove; `useAlternateCost` is not exercised because the card offers no
  alternate. A Tamer-base route is likewise not applicable.
- The [On Play] clause is only reached by playing from hand; it is not exercised
  through an effect that plays the card, nor from breeding.
- No test covers placing when the only legal source is also the card that would be
  needed elsewhere (no interaction pressure on the cost).

#### Catalog discrepancies

None. The catalog text matches the official wording, with the set-wide full-width
`＜ ＞` keyword brackets.

### BT17-022 — Lobomon

Digimon, Lv4, Blue/Yellow, Warrior/Hybrid. Module: `apps/api/src/cards/BT17/BT17-022.ts`.
Test: `apps/api/src/cards/BT17/BT17-022.test.ts`.

#### Printed clauses

1. `[Digivolve][Koji Minamoto]: Cost 2` — exact-name Tamer route, discounted over the generic path.
2. `[Digivolve][KendoGarurumon]: Cost 1` — exact-name slide route (same-level Lv4 -> Lv4).
3. Static: "digivolve this card from your hand onto one of your yellow Tamers as if that card is a
   level 3 yellow Digimon" — derived tamer-onto path at the normal yellow Lv3 cost (3).
4. `[When Digivolving]`: if `[KendoGarurumon]` is in the digivolution cards OR you have a black or
   purple Digimon/Tamer, may digivolve into `[AncientGarurumon]` from hand for cost 3, ignoring
   requirements.
5. "If digivolved by this effect, delete this Digimon at the end of the turn." — `DelayedDelete`
   gated on `ifThisEffectDigivolved`.
6. Inherited `[When Attacking]`: if 7 or fewer cards in hand, `<Draw 1>`.

No catalog discrepancy: `cards.json` matches the assignment text and official list.

#### Clause -> IR -> test

| Clause | IR | Behavioural test |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 1 | `digivolutionRequirement[0]` `namesExact:["Koji Minamoto"]`, cost 2, `baseIsTamer` | "digivolves from Koji Minamoto ... route for 2" (memory 6->4) |
| 2 | `digivolutionRequirement[1]` `namesExact:["KendoGarurumon"]`, cost 1 | "slides into AncientGarurumon from KendoGarurumon in its own stack" (memory 6->5 on the route) |
| 3 | `effects[0]` Static `Digivolve` `asLevel:3`, yellow Tamer filter | "digivolves from a yellow Tamer as a level 3 base" (memory 6->3) |
| 4 | `effects[1].actions[0]` `Digivolve` `condition:anyOf` (`selfDigivolutionStackHasTrait` name / `youHave` Black|Purple) | KendoGarurumon-in-stack branch; black/purple branch; both-false negative |
| 5 | `effects[1].actions[1]` `DelayedDelete` `ifThisEffectDigivolved` | slide tests: AncientGarurumon deleted at end of turn; no-slide test: Lobomon survives |
| 6 | `effects[2]` inherited `WhenAttacking` `Draw 1` `zoneCount lte 7` | inherited draw at hand 1 (draws); no draw at hand 8 |

#### IR change (report to coordinator)

Both printed routes are the `[Digivolve][X]: Cost N` form (no "in name"), so per the route-name
coordinator decision they must gate on exact name. Changed `names` -> `namesExact` for both entries
in `BT17-022.ts`.

- Impact: `packages/shared/src/effects/effects.json` still stores `names` for BT17-022, so
  `BT17-catalog-sync.test.ts` goes red until the coordinator runs the set sync. effects.json was
  not touched (out of scope).
- No near-name substring victim exists in the catalog for either token, so the change is defensive,
  not behaviour-changing on current data: every name containing `KendoGarurumon`/`Koji Minamoto`
  either equals it or (BT18-088 "Takuya Kanbara & Koji Minamoto") is treated-as `[Koji Minamoto]`
  by its own `[Rule]`, a genuine match — not a false positive. The near-name negative the decision
  asks for is therefore vacuous here; documented rather than faked.

  The carry-over through a *further* digivolve/de-digivolve is not separately exercised.

Not covered — documented reason (engine-generic or cross-card, outside a focused single-card test):

   `<U+00A0>trait in the hand with the digivolution cost reduced by 1.` (optional, -1).

#### Clause -> IR -> test

| --- | --- | --- |
| Named routes | `digivolutionRequirement` `namesExact` Koji Minamoto/Lobomon | "declares the named routes as exact names"; "selects the correct alternate route per base"; Lobomon route executed |
| Static yellow-Tamer digivolve | `effects[0]` Digivolve `asLevel:3`, Tamer/Yellow filter, `payCost`, not optional | "digivolves onto a yellow Tamer..." |
| ＜Draw 1＞ | `effects[1]` WhenAttacking keyword Draw 1 | "draws from its ＜Draw 1＞ [When Attacking] on a public attack" |
| Digivolve into Hybrid -1 | `effects[2]` Digivolve `from:["hand"]`, `costDelta:-1`, `optional`, Hybrid trait, no bypass flag | Q2769 test (executes into BT4-030) + IR shape |
| Inherited conditional draw | `effects[3]` isInherited Draw 1 `zoneCount hand lte 7` | inherited positive (7 or fewer) + negative (8 cards) |

#### Q&A coverage (11)

- Q2764 — Koji Minamoto cost-2 route + treat-as-Digimon: route selection asserted
  (`matchingAlternateDigivolutionRequirement("BT17-023","BT17-083") -> cost 2, baseIsTamer`).
- Q2765 — bonus draw even when a Tamer digivolves: asserted (deck -1 on the yellow-Tamer digivolve).
- Q2767 — the Tamer becomes a digivolution card: asserted (stack holds the Tamer instance after digivolve).
- Q2768 — the 2nd [When Attacking] digivolve still needs to meet requirements: IR carries no
  `ignoreRequirements`/`ignoreDigivolutionRequirement` flag (asserted); it routes through the same
  legality path as the executed BT4-030 digivolve.
- Q2769 — 2nd-[When Attacking]-first loses the 1st: behavioural ordering test (digivolve into
  BT4-030, only the bonus draw fires, the pending ＜Draw 1＞ is lost).
- Q6567 — a host gains the inherited [When Attacking]: behavioural (host with BT17-023 beneath
  draws on attack at 7 or fewer cards; does not at 8).
- Q2762 — end-of-turn AncientGarurumon deletion timing via BT18-088: rules clarification about a
  *different* card's self-delete timing; not a BT17-023 IR clause. Documented, untestable in-lane.
- Q2763 — treating the Tamer as a Digimon fires other cards' "when a Digimon digivolves" triggers:
  requires a third-party trigger card; the yellow-Tamer digivolve shows the Tamer digivolves, but the
  cross-card trigger is not exercised. Documented.
- Q2766 — a Digimon that digivolved from a same-turn-played Tamer cannot attack that turn: engine-general
  summoning-sickness rule (harness `enteredThisTurn`), not specific to this card's IR. Documented.
- Q4660 — the 1st (Static) digivolve cannot be declined once declared: `effects[0]` Digivolve has no
  `optional` flag (asserted). The "no opt-out mid-declaration" procedure is a rules clarification.
- Q6566 — a Digimon does NOT gain a Tamer digivolution card's security effects: engine-general rule
  (security lower text is not inherited text); the Q6567 test establishes the Tamer-as-digivolution-card
  precondition. Documented, not separately asserted.

#### Route-name (coordinator) decision — IR change

`[Digivolve][Koji Minamoto]: Cost 2` and `[Digivolve][Lobomon]: Cost 1` print no "in name", so
the module now declares `namesExact` (was `names`). **IR changed: yes.**

The requested near-name substring negative is **not constructible** from the real catalog:

  (`STATIC_NAME_ALIASES_BY_CARD_ID`, and its `[Rule] Name: Also treated as [...]/[Koji Minamoto]`), so

- Every "Lobomon"-containing card name equals `Lobomon`.

So the change is behaviourally inert on the current catalog but is the correct semantics.

Note: runtime legality (`digivolutionRequirementsFor` -> `effects.json`/overrides, in the read-only
`@aegis/shared` package) still uses the substring `names` gate; the proper runtime fix is an
`ALTERNATE_DIGIVOLUTION_OVERRIDES` entry (as done for BT18-101), which is out of this lane. Because
the distinction is inert here, no behaviour differs; the module declares the correct IR.

### BT17-024 — Seasarmon

#### Card summary

Catalog (`packages/shared/src/cards/data/cards.json`): Digimon, Lv4, Blue, 5000 DP,
play cost 6, evolution cost Blue Lv3 for 2 memory, type Holy Beast.

Printed clauses:

1. `[On Play] [When Digivolving] By placing 1 level 3 blue Digimon card from your hand
   as this Digimon's bottom digivolution card, 1 of your Digimon gains ＜Jamming＞ for
   the turn.` — one optional cost, two trigger windows, four testable parts: the cost
   filter (level 3, blue, Digimon, from hand), the placement position (bottom of the
   source's own stack), the target ("1 of your Digimon", not necessarily itself), and
   the duration ("for the turn").
2. Inherited: `＜Jamming＞.`

The catalog text matches the official list exactly. No discrepancy found.

#### Rulings

`node tools/kb/query.mjs card BT17-024` returns no knowledge-base entries, matching
`KB-INDEX.md` row "BT17-024 | Seasarmon | 0 | None". No errata, not on the banlist.
Nothing to cover, nothing untestable.

#### Clause -> test -> IR mapping

| Clause | Test (`BT17-024.test.ts`) | IR |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Catalog identity and full IR shape | "matches the catalog and the complete IR contract" (`toEqual` over all three effects, plus `coverage`/`residual`) | whole `compiled` |
| [On Play] cost + placement + grant | "places the level 3 blue Digimon at the bottom of its own stack and grants Jamming on play" | `effects[0]` `GainKeyword` with `cost.kind: "place"`, `position: "bottom"`, `host: "self"` |
| "1 of your Digimon" (target is not the source) | "can grant the Jamming to another of your Digimon instead of itself" (steered with `preferInstanceIds`) | `effects[0].actions[0].target.filter` (`controller: "mine"`, `kind: ["Digimon"]`, no `isSelfRef`) |
| [When Digivolving] cost + bottom placement below the base | "places the level 3 blue Digimon below the digivolution base when digivolving" (stack asserted as `[material, base]`) | `effects[1]` |
| "for the turn" | "loses the granted Jamming once the turn ends" (real turn loop, expiry asserted after the turn hands over) | `duration: "forTheTurn"` |
| Cost filter is level 3 **blue** | "grants no Jamming when the hand holds no level 3 blue Digimon card" (red Lv3 in hand stays in hand, no keyword) | `cost.target.filter.colors: ["Blue"], levels: [3]` |
| Cost is optional | "keeps the card in hand and grants nothing when the optional cost is declined" (`autoDeclineOptional`, an `optional` decision is asserted to have been raised) | `optional: true`, `abortOnDecline: true` |
| Legal evolution route and illegal source | digivolve test above (Blue Lv3 base, 2 memory, bonus draw asserted) and "refuses to digivolve from a source outside its printed blue level 3 cost" (red Lv3 base rejected, memory and hand unchanged) | catalog `evoCosts` |
| Inherited ＜Jamming＞ | "grants inherited Jamming to its host" | `effects[2]` static inherited keyword |

Endpoints asserted in the behavioural tests: instance ids by zone (hand, deck,
digivolution stack, battle area), digivolution stack **order**, memory, the presence
and absence of the granted keyword, and the intent result object. Every assertion runs
after `settle(...)` on a real milestone; no injected timing is used anywhere in this file.

#### Defects found and fixed locally

1. The previous suite proved the placement only with `stack.some(...)`, which cannot
   distinguish bottom from top. It now asserts the whole stack in order
   (`[material]` on play, `[material, base]` when digivolving).
2. Three clause parts had no behavioural coverage at all: the "1 of your Digimon"
   target choice, the level-3-blue cost filter (no negative), and the "for the turn"
   duration. All three now have tests.
3. The decline branch and the illegal digivolution source were untested. Added.
4. The old IR assertions were `toMatchObject` fragments; they are now a single
   `toEqual` over `compiled.effects` plus a catalog contract assertion, so a silent
   IR drift (a changed filter, a wrong `position`) fails the suite.

#### Retained red

None. No engine gap was found for this card.

#### Remaining gaps

- The "for the turn" expiry test rides the turn boundary the digivolution cost itself
  causes (memory reaches 0), rather than an explicit `endPhase`. That is the real turn

  also assert an in-turn End-of-Main state.
- No test covers a second Seasarmon placing under the first mid-chain; the printed
  text does not distinguish that case and no ruling calls for it.

#### Q&A ids covered (KB-INDEX; `node tools/kb/query.mjs card BT17-026`)

- Q2772 (must digivolve for cost 3, not 4): behavioural — test "places both Hybrid
  materials..." asserts memory 4 -> 1 (exactly 3); structural test asserts

- Q6568 (digivolving onto a Tamer as if a Digimon triggers "When Digivolving"):

  lock, not this card. Documented as untestable at card scope.
- Q6572 (a Digimon does NOT gain the security effects of a Tamer in its digivolution
  cards): engine-general Tamer-as-digivolution-card mechanic. Koji carries
  `securityEffectText`; a Digimon never gains lower-text security effects. Not

- Q6573 (a Digimon DOES gain the inherited effects of a Tamer in its digivolution
  cards): engine-general. Beowolfmon over Koji would gain Koji's inherited
  `[Your Turn][Once Per Turn]` memory/unblockable effect. Not card-specific IR;
  documented, not re-tested.

#### Clause -> action -> test mapping

| Clause | IR (BT17-026.ts) | Test |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 1 Main digivolve | `effects[0]` Digivolve, place Lobomon + additionalCosts place KendoGarurumon under Koji host, `costOverride:3`, `asLevel:4`, `virtualBase` L4 Blue | structural test 1; behavioural "places both Hybrid materials..." (stack `[kendo, lobomon, koji]`, both removed from trash, memory 4->1, no leftover activatable) |
| 2 When Digivolving restrict | `effects[1]` Restrict `suspend` + `blocksCombatSuspend`, cost return 1 Hybrid from own digivolution cards | structural test 2; behavioural "draws the digivolve bonus..." (Hybrid returned to hand, `isRestricted beSuspended` + `suspend` true, `verb.suspend` leaves victim unsuspended, opponent-turn attack refused) |
| 3 inherited return | `effects[2]` Return to hand, `selfHasTrait` Hybrid/Ten Warriors, opponent Digimon `lte 4` | structural test 3; behavioural "returns an opposing level 4 Digimon when its Hybrid host attacks" |

#### Defects fixed locally (IR changed — see catalog-sync note)

1. **Missing combat-suspend block.** `effects[1]` Restrict had `restriction: "suspend"`
   without `blocksCombatSuspend: true`. The interpreter
   (`apps/api/src/engine/effects/interpreter/actions/restrictions.ts`) maps
   `"suspend"` -> `"beSuspended"` (can't be suspended by effects; combat self-suspend
   exempt) and only also records the combat `"suspend"` restriction when
   `blocksCombatSuspend` is set. The printed "can't suspend until the end of their
   turn" must also stop the Digimon from suspending to attack (KB comprehensive §4-23
   example + attack rule 11-2-1: attacking suspends the attacker). Added
   `blocksCombatSuspend: true`. Precedent: BT26-019, EX11-017 author identical
   "can't suspend until turn ends" wording with the flag.
   - Peer inconsistency (not my file): **EX7-022** prints the exact same sentence
     ("1 of your opponent's Digimon or Tamers can't suspend until the end of their
     turn") but is authored WITHOUT `blocksCombatSuspend` and its test asserts only
     the weaker `beSuspended` behaviour. EX7-022 carries the same latent
     under-authoring; coordinator should reconcile the two.

2. **Wrong host ref on the return cost.** The `[When Digivolving]` cost targeted
   `hostFilter: { sourceRef: "triggerSubject" }`. The printed text says "this
   Digimon's digivolution cards" — self-referential — so the host is the source, not
   the trigger subject. On this Main-effect-driven digivolve
   `ctx.trigger.subjectPermanentId` is unset, so `triggerSubject` resolved to
   `undefined`, `candidateLooseInstances` found no host, `canPayCost`
   (`costs.ts`, return/digivolutionCards branch) returned false, and `runAction.ts`
   silently skipped the optional and aborted the whole `[When Digivolving]` effect
   (no return, no restriction). Changed to `hostFilter: { isSelfRef: true }`, matching
   the many EX12/BT3-112 self-referential digivolution-card costs. `triggerSubject`
   is correct only when the subject is a different permanent (e.g. BT18-092
   "that Digimon's digivolution cards" on an attack trigger), which is why that peer
   works and BT17-026 did not.

### BT17-028 — AncientGarurumon

#### Card summary (printed clauses)

- **C1 [On Play] [When Digivolving]** Return 1 of your opponent's Digimon with the lowest level to the hand.
- **C2 [Your Turn] [Once Per Turn]** When an effect adds cards to your or your opponent's hand, your opponent adds the top card of their security stack to the hand.
- **C3 [On Deletion]** You may return 1 Tamer card and 1 [Hybrid] trait Digimon card from your trash to the hand. Then, you may play 1 Tamer card from your hand without paying the cost.
- **C4 [DigiXros -3]** [Lobomon] x [KendoGarurumon].

Catalog `dp` 12000, `playCost` 12, `evoCosts` Blue Lv5 (4) / Yellow Lv5 (4). No inherited effect.

#### Defect found and fixed

`C4 [DigiXros -3]` was **not implemented**: the module carried `coverage: "full", residual: []`
but no `digiXrosRequirement`, so `digiXrosRequirementFor("BT17-028")`
(`packages/shared/src/effects/data.ts`) returned `undefined` and the card could never be
DigiXros-played. Fixed by adding the recipe, mirroring the same-set sibling BT17-017
(`[DigiXros -3] [Agunimon] x [BurningGreymon]`):

```ts
digiXrosRequirement: [
  { materials: [{ names: ["Lobomon"] }, { names: ["KendoGarurumon"] }], count: 3 },
],
```

`count` is the per-material cost reduction (`DigiXrosRequirement` doc,
`packages/shared/src/effects/ir/requirements/xrosLink.ts`), so `-3` -> `count: 3`. This makes
`coverage: "full"` honest. **IR changed: yes** — `BT17-catalog-sync.test.ts` will be red until the
coordinator runs the set sync (effects.json currently has no `digiXrosRequirement` for this card).
I did not touch effects.json.

#### Q&A ids covered

- **Q2774** (digivolution bonus hand increase does not trigger the [Your Turn] effect): test
  "does not move security from the digivolution bonus draw alone" — digivolve with no opposing
  Digimon to return, opponent security unchanged. Engine confirms the bonus draw never routes
  through the `whenEffectAddsToHand` seam (`subTrigger.ts` gate comment; `primitives.ts` fx.draw).
- **Q2775** (both hands increase at the same timing -> opponent adds only 1): the two directional
  buses share one `oncePerTurnKey`, so at most one fire per turn. Proven by the once-per-turn
  refusal + reset test through the real turn loop.
- **Q2776** (may play the Tamer without returning from trash first): test "may play a Tamer on
  deletion without recovering anything first" (empty trash, Tamer still played).

#### Clause -> test -> IR mapping

| Clause | Test | IR |
| --- | --- | --- |
| C1 On Play, strict lowest | "returns only the opposing Digimon with the strictly lowest level on play" | effects[0] Return superlative lowestLevel |
| C1 When Digivolving + stack + cost + bonus draw | "returns the opposing lowest-level Digimon and draws the bonus card when digivolving" | effects[1] Return |
| C2 opponent-hand direction | "moves the opponent's top security to hand when its On Play effect returns a Digimon" | effects[2] SubTrigger whenEffectAddsToOpponentHand |
| C2 your-hand direction | RETAINED RED (it.fails) — engine seam | effects[2] SubTrigger whenEffectAddsToHand |
| C2 once-per-turn refusal + reset (Q2775) | "moves security once per turn and resets on the controller's next turn" | effects[2] frequency OncePerTurn, shared oncePerTurnKey |
| C2 bonus-draw exclusion (Q2774) | "does not move security from the digivolution bonus draw alone" | effects[2] |
| C4 DigiXros -3 recipe + behaviour | "keeps the DigiXros recipe…", "DigiXroses Lobomon and KendoGarurumon for the printed -3 reduction" | digiXrosRequirement |
| C3 full recovery then play | "recovers a Tamer and Hybrid Digimon from trash, then plays a Tamer on deletion" | effects[3] Return, Return, PlayWithoutCost |
| C3 play only (Q2776) | "may play a Tamer on deletion without recovering anything first" | effects[3] optional actions |
| Evolution illegal source | "rejects digivolving onto an off-color level-5 source" | evoCosts (Blue/Yellow Lv5) |

Evolution coverage: legal normal route asserted with source-stack identity (BT1-038 Monzaemon
under BT17-028), memory delta 4->0, and bonus draw; illegal route negative (Red Lv5 BT17-013
refused). No alternate/DNA/Tamer-base route is printed.

#### Retained red (engine seam)

`it.fails` "moves the opponent's top security to hand when an effect adds to your own hand".

- **Seam:** the [Your Turn] effect wires two SubTrigger buses under one shared `oncePerTurnKey`
  ("BT17-028/hand-add"): `whenEffectAddsToOpponentHand` (works) and `whenEffectAddsToHand` (dead).
  BT17-028 is the only card in the catalog wiring both directions under one key.
- **File / function:** `apps/api/src/engine/effects/subtriggers.ts` (SubTriggerRegistry
  subscribe/fire); fire seam at `apps/api/src/engine/effects/primitives.ts:680`
  (`fx.draw` -> `fireSubTrigger("whenEffectAddsToHand")`).
- **Repro:** with BT17-028 in play on your turn (freshly played or established), an effect Draw
  into your own hand (BT1-029 `kind:"Draw"`) fires `whenEffectAddsToHand`; the same seam fires
  BT17-083's single-bus `whenEffectAddsToHand` correctly.
- **Expected vs actual:** opponent security should drop by 1; actual: unchanged (predicate never
  holds). The opponent-hand half of the same clause fires correctly, so only the "your hand"
  direction is lost. Fix belongs in the engine (out of lane scope); assertion not weakened.

#### Remaining gaps

   cost (suspend a Tamer that is yours and yellow), effect draws exactly 1.
2. Inherited: `[Your Turn] All of your opponent's security Digimon get -3000 DP.` —
   turn-scoped, applies to the opponent's security Digimon only.

Catalog text matches the official list. No discrepancy.

#### Rulings

`node tools/kb/query.mjs card BT17-029` returns no entries; `KB-INDEX.md` lists 0 Q&A
ids for this card. No errata, not on the banlist.

#### Clause -> test -> IR mapping

| Clause | Test (`BT17-029.test.ts`) | IR |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Catalog identity and full IR shape | "matches the catalog and the complete IR contract" (`toEqual` over both effects) | whole `compiled` |
| [When Attacking] pay by suspending, draw exactly 1 | "suspends a yellow Tamer to draw exactly 1 card when attacking" — Tamer suspended, hand is exactly the top deck card, deck is exactly the remaining card, attacker trades with the revealed security Digimon | `effects[0]` `Draw` with `cost.kind: "suspend"` |
| Cost requires a **yellow** Tamer | "does not draw when the only Tamer is not yellow" (red Tamer stays unsuspended, hand empty, deck untouched) | `cost.target.filter.colors: ["Yellow"]` |
| Cost is optional | "draws nothing when the optional cost is declined" (`autoDeclineOptional`; an `optional` decision is asserted to have been raised, Tamer unsuspended, deck untouched) | `optional: true`, `abortOnDecline: true` |
| Inherited -3000 DP to opposing security Digimon | "gives the opponent's security Digimon -3000 DP as an inherited effect on your turn" — `observe(...).securityDp(1) === -3000` plus a real security battle a 5000 DP attacker only wins because the 6000 DP security Digimon is at 3000 | `effects[1]` `ModifySecurityDP` |
| `[Your Turn]` scope of the inherited clause | "does not reduce security DP on the opponent's turn" (`securityDp(1) === 0`) | `trigger: "YourTurn"` |

All behavioural assertions land after `settle(() => !observe(s.engine).isAttacking())`,
i.e. after the attack and its security check have fully resolved. No injected timing in
this file.

   added.

   catalog contract assertion.

  `＜Security Attack -1＞` until the end of their turn.

`[Security]` clause is present in the catalog, so none is owed. No digivolution
special routes are printed (plain Lv3 with a standard evo cost), so no route
tests apply.

#### Clause -> test -> IR mapping

- Clause A -> `compiled.effects[0]` `RevealAdd` (revealCount 3, rest deckBottom,
  add[0] named-Digimon `match: "name"` on Kyubimon/Taomon/Sakuyamon, add[1] Rika
  Nonaka Tamer with `orFilters: [{ kind: ["Option"], playCostGte: 2 }]`).
  - Structural: `reveals three and adds ...`.
  - Behavioural Rika branch: `adds one named Digimon and Rika ...` (existing).
  - Behavioural Option branch: `adds a cost-2 Option through the second slot's
    alternative filter` — reveal [Kyubimon, BT1-102, BT1-029]; Kyubimon and the
    cost-2 Option BT1-102 reach hand, BT1-029 goes to deck bottom.
  - Behavioural negative: `adds nothing and bottom-decks all three when none of
    the revealed cards qualify` — three non-matching Digimon, hand stays empty,
    all three end in deck, no pending decision.
- Clause B -> `compiled.effects[1]` `YourTurn` / `isInherited` /
  `OncePerTurn` / `SubTrigger whenOptionUsed` gated by
  `triggerOptionCostAtLeast value 2` -> `GainKeyword SecurityAttack -1`,
  `duration untilOpponentTurnEnd`.
  - Structural: `gives an opposing Digimon Security Attack -1 ...`.
  - Cost gate (injected, structural only): `reduces Security Attack only for an
    option use cost of at least 2`.
  - Behavioural real use: `triggers after a real cost-2 Option is used from
    hand` (existing) — BT1-102 played through the production play seam; target
    reaches SecurityAttack -1 after the Option's [Main] resolves.
  - OPT same-turn refusal + peer: `refuses a second grant in the same turn and
    targets exactly one of two opposing Digimon` — two cost-2 Options played the
    same turn; total SecurityAttack across both opponents stays -1 and exactly
    one of the two is at -1.
  - OPT reset through the real turn loop: `grants again on the next own turn
    after the once-per-turn use is spent` — grant -1 this turn; `runTurn(0)` then
    `runTurn(1)` (grant expires at the opponent's turn end, asserted back to 0);
    seat 0's next Main is reopened with `runOneTurn`/`waitForMainPhase` and a
    second real Option grants -1 again.

#### Q&A coverage (Q2778, Q2779, Q2780, Q2781, Q5453)

The last four all turn on one engine rule: the `whenOptionUsed` watcher gates on
`usedOptionCost`, which is the card-level use cost after card-level changes but
before payment-only reductions (`conditions.ts:858` `triggerOptionCostAtLeast`;
`primitives.ts:3035` `notifiedUseCost = projectedHandUseCost = effectiveLooseUseCost`).

- Q2778 (timing: activates after the used Option's [Main] effect) — covered
  behaviourally: the real-Option tests resolve BT1-102's [Main] (draw) first and
  the grant lands afterward through the production `useOptionFromHand` seam.
- Q2779 (Option effect activated by [Security]/<Delay> does not count as "use")
  — covered by architecture: `fireOptionUsed` is reached only from the
  option-use path (`primitives.ts:2939`, `actions/playCard.ts:163`), never from
  security or delay activation. Not re-tested per-card; a security-check-into-
  activation fixture is disproportionate and the seam is engine-owned.
- Q2780 (use cost itself reduced to <=1, e.g. BT2-099/BT8-097 -> does not
  trigger) and Q2781 (only the cost to pay reduced, use cost still 2+ ->
  triggers) — covered at the engine layer: distinguished by the same
  `effectiveLooseUseCost` snapshot that excludes payment-only reductions, with a
  dedicated engine test `actions/playCard.test.ts:317` ("reports the card-level
  adjusted use cost to whenOptionUsed", BT10-032 Q1956/Q1957). Re-testing per
  card would require the specific cost-modifier cards; not warranted.
- Q5453 (used without paying the cost, original use cost 2+ -> triggers) —
  covered at the engine layer: the 08-06 use-option-without-paying seam still
  fires `whenOptionUsed` carrying the original use cost (`primitives.ts:2939`).

| Q2783 (no trigger when Option activates via Security/`<Delay>`) | Reasoned, not behaviourally built | `fireOptionUsed` is emitted only from the from-hand `playCard` path; a `[Security]`/`<Delay>` activation never enters it. Building it needs a Security-triggered Option fixture — untested gap |
| Q5454 (use cost reduced below 2 -> no trigger) | Covered at the boundary | "use cost below 2" test uses a genuine cost-1 Option; the debuff does not fire. `optionUseCost` reflects card-level (use-cost) reductions, so a 2+ card reduced to 1 by BT2-099/BT8-097 lands on the same non-firing path |
| Q5455 (pay cost reduced, use cost 2+ -> triggers) | Reasoned + baseline behavioural | baseline cost-2 use fires; `optionUseCost` is the passive cost, NOT the BeforePayCost pay reduction (`playCard.ts:356` vs `:361`), so a pay-only reduction keeps the use cost 2+. A BeforePayCost-reducer fixture is not built — reasoned only |

| Clause | Test (`BT17-033.test.ts`) | IR |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Catalog identity and full IR shape | "matches the catalog and the complete IR contract" (`toEqual` over both effects) | whole `compiled` |
| [When Attacking] +3000 DP paid by suspending a yellow Tamer | "suspends a yellow Tamer for +3000 DP and survives an equal-DP security Digimon" — DP 5000 before, 8000 after, Tamer suspended, the 5000 DP security Digimon is trashed and GeoGreymon stays on the board | `effects[0]` `ModifyDP` +3000 with `cost.kind: "suspend"` |
| Cost requires a **yellow** Tamer | "cannot pay with a non-yellow Tamer, so it trades with the equal-DP security Digimon" — red Tamer unsuspended, both Digimon trashed | `cost.target.filter.colors: ["Yellow"]` |
| Cost is optional | "leaves the Tamer unsuspended and the DP unchanged when the optional cost is declined" (`autoDeclineOptional`, `optional` decision asserted, attacker dies the trade) | `optional: true`, `abortOnDecline: true` |
| "for the turn" | "loses the +3000 DP once the turn ends" — real turn loop; the battle-time 8000 is read through the documented `onEvent` seam, and 5000 is asserted after the turn hands over | `duration: "forTheTurn"` |
| Inherited -3000 DP to opposing security Digimon | "gives the opponent's security Digimon -3000 DP as an inherited effect on your turn" — `securityDp(1) === -3000`, and a 4000 DP host beats a 5000 DP security Digimon only because it is at 2000 | `effects[1]` `ModifySecurityDP` |
| `[Your Turn]` scope | "does not reduce security DP on the opponent's turn" (`securityDp(1) === 0`) | `trigger: "YourTurn"` |

#### Defects found and fixed locally

1. **The main printed clause had no behavioural test at all.** The previous suite only
   asserted the IR shape for it. Three behavioural tests now cover the buff, the cost
   colour negative and the decline branch.
2. **The "inherited" test did not exercise this card's inherited effect.** It laid
   `{ card: "BT17-033", under: ["BT17-031"] }`, i.e. BT17-033 as the **top** card with
   BT17-031 underneath — so the only inherited effect in play was BT17-031's, and the
   assertion (a 7000 DP attacker beating a 5000 DP security Digimon) held with or
   without any DP reduction. The test proved nothing. It now puts BT17-033 **under** an
   inert host, asserts `securityDp(1) === -3000` directly, and picks DP values where
   the reduction decides the security battle.
3. No `[Your Turn]` negative existed for the inherited clause. Added.
4. IR assertions upgraded from `toMatchObject` fragments to a full `toEqual`, plus a
   catalog contract assertion.

#### Retained red

None. No engine gap was found.

#### Remaining gaps

- The expiry test reads the battle-time 8000 DP through the `onEvent` hook rather than
  from post-attack state, because paying no memory still hands the turn over when the
  attack resolves at 0 memory. `onEvent` is the harness's documented seam for values
  that exist only during a battle, and the post-turn 5000 is asserted from state.
- Attacking a Digimon (rather than the player) with the buff up is not covered
  separately; the DP value itself is the same modifier and is asserted directly.

#### Catalog discrepancies

None.

### BT17-034 — Bulkmon

#### Card

Digimon, Lv4, Yellow/Green, play cost 5, DP 5000, Vaccine, Dragonkin / Abadin
Electronics. EvoCosts: Yellow Lv3 cost 3, Green Lv3 cost 3.

Printed clauses:

1. `[Digivolve][Pulsemon]: Cost 2` — alternate digivolution route.
2. `[When Digivolving]` If you have 3 or more security cards, 1 of your
   opponent's Digimon gets -3000 DP for the turn.
3. `[When Digivolving]` If you have 3 or fewer security cards, suspend 1 of your
   opponent's Digimon.
4. `[All Turns] [Once Per Turn]` When a card is trashed from your security stack,
   if `[Leon Alexander]` is in this Digimon's digivolution cards,
   `＜Recovery +1 (Deck)＞`.
5. Inherited `[All Turns]` While this Digimon has `[Pulsemon]` in its text, it
   gets +1000 DP.

#### Q&A covered

- **Q2784** — at exactly 3 security cards both the -3000 DP clause and the
  suspend clause activate. Covered by "fires both branches at exactly 3 security
  cards, per Q2784", plus the 4-security and 2-security single-branch tests that
  fence the boundary on both sides.

#### Defects found and fixed locally

1. **Clause 1 was entirely unimplemented.** The module carried no
   `digivolutionRequirement`, and neither `ALTERNATE_DIGIVOLUTION_OVERRIDES` nor
   `generated-digivolve-overrides.json` names BT17-034, so
   `digivolutionRequirementsFor("BT17-034")` returned `undefined`: the printed
   `[Digivolve][Pulsemon]: Cost 2` route did not exist. Added
   `digivolutionRequirement: [{ names: ["Pulsemon"], cost: 2, isAlternate: true }]`.
2. **Set-wide `[Name]` defect.** `[Leon Alexander]` is a printed exact name
   reference but compiled as `match: "name"` (substring). Changed to
   `match: "nameExact"`, matching BT17-030's encoding of the same Tamer.
3. **Clauses 2 and 3 shared one action list.** With both clauses in a single
   `WhenDigivolving` effect, the suspend clause never fired when the DP clause's
   condition failed. Split into two independent `WhenDigivolving` effects — which
   is also the correct reading of two independent printed conditions (Q2784).
   Verified behaviourally: with 2 security cards the suspend now lands.
4. The suspend gate used `{ kind: "zoneCount", seat: "mine", zone: "security",
   op: "lte", value: 3 }`; replaced with the canonical mirror of the DP gate,
   `{ kind: "securityAtMost", value: 3 }`.

Fixture hygiene: the previous test put Digi-Egg `BT1-001` in security and deck.
All fixtures now use inert main-deck Digimon (`BT1-012`, `BT1-013`); no numeric
`security: <n>` form is used.

#### Clause -> test -> IR

| Clause | Test | IR |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 1 | "digivolves from a Pulsemon for 2 through the printed [Pulsemon] route" (memory 5 -> 3); "costs 3 from the same Pulsemon through the printed Yellow Lv3 EvoCost" (memory 5 -> 2); "refuses the [Pulsemon] route from a same-colour Lv3 that is not a Pulsemon"; "refuses an illegal off-colour non-Pulsemon source on both routes" | `digivolutionRequirement` |
| 2 | "only lowers DP when digivolving with 4 security cards" | effect 0, `ModifyDP -3000 forTheTurn`, `securityAtLeast 3` |
| 3 | "only suspends when digivolving with 2 security cards" | effect 1, `Suspend`, `securityAtMost 3` |
| 2 + 3 | "fires both branches at exactly 3 security cards, per Q2784" | effects 0 and 1 |
| 4 | "recovers when any of its owner's security cards is trashed with Leon Alexander in stack"; "recovers after a normal security check trashes a card" (real attack, block declined); "does not recover without Leon Alexander in the digivolution cards"; "does not recover when a security card is added to hand instead of trashed"; "recovers at most once per turn when two security cards are trashed" | effect 2, `SubTrigger whenCardTrashedFromSecurity` -> `SecurityManipulation addTop deck`, `selfDigivolutionStackHasTrait` `nameExact`, `frequency: OncePerTurn` |
| 5 | "grants the inherited +1000 DP only under a host whose text names Pulsemon" (BT17-036 Boutmon +1000 vs BT17-032 Kyubimon unchanged, same fixture) | effect 3, `Aura` + `selfTopHasText` `match: "text"` |

#### Remaining gaps

- **Once-per-turn reset across a turn boundary is not covered.** Same-turn
  refusal is proven. The reset is not reachable through the current harness:
  `advance(engine).runTurn(seat)` (apps/api/src/engine/testkit/advance.ts,
  `runTurn` / `waitForMainPhase`) leaves the engine at `Phase.End` with
  `state.turnSeat` unchanged, and a second `runTurn` throws "Main phase did not
  become ready ... End". Setting `state.turnSeat` by hand does not clear
  `Phase.End`, so the start-of-turn subtrigger-ledger reset
  (`GameEngine.subTriggerTurnLedger` / `tracker.unregister`) is never reached
  from a test that has already acted inside the current turn. No card assertion
  was weakened; the gap is a testkit seam, not a card defect.
- Only the `deck` source of `＜Recovery +1 (Deck)＞` is asserted; no test proves a
  Recovery would be refused with an empty deck.

#### Catalog discrepancies

None. Catalog text matches the official list. Note `inheritedEffectText` and
`effectText` contain non-breaking spaces; the catalog test normalises ` `
before comparing.

#### IR changed

Yes — `digivolutionRequirement` added, `nameExact` fix, the `WhenDigivolving`
split, and the `securityAtMost` gate. `BT17-catalog-sync.test.ts` will be red
until the coordinator runs the set sync; `effects.json` was not touched.

### BT17-035 — Taomon

#### Card

Digimon, Lv5, Yellow, play cost 7, DP 7000, Data, Wizard. EvoCost: Yellow Lv4
cost 3.

Printed clauses:

1. `＜Barrier＞`.
2. `[When Digivolving]` You may use 1 option card that has `[Plug-In]` in its
   name or that's yellow from the hand with the cost reduced by 2.
3. Inherited `[When Attacking] [Once Per Turn]` If this Digimon has
   `[Sakuyamon]` in its name, you may use 1 option card that has `[Plug-In]` in
   its name or that's yellow from the hand with the cost reduced by 2.

#### Q&A covered

- **Q2785** — this effect does not let you ignore an Option's colour
  requirements. Covered by "cannot ignore an Option's colour requirements, per
  Q2785": with only Yellow permanents on the board, the black `[Plug-In]` Option
  BT10-105 is not a candidate and stays in hand, and only the digivolve cost is
  paid. The companion test proves the same fixture does use a yellow
  `[Plug-In]` (P-146) at 2 less.

#### Defects found

None in the module. Both name references are printed as "in its name", so the
substring forms (`match: "name"` for `[Plug-In]`, `selfHasNameContaining` for
`[Sakuyamon]`) are correct — the set-wide `nameExact` defect does not apply here.
`allowMultiColor: true` is right: the card prints no single-colour restriction.
`playCostLte: 99` is the no-ceiling sentinel; the card prints no cost ceiling,
and a cost-6 yellow Option is proven usable.

Session-3 defect fixed (test flow, not IR): "uses a same-cost yellow Option from
the very fixture that refuses the black `[Plug-In]`" waited for P-146 to reach
the trash. P-146 `Recharge Plug-In Q` prints `[Main] Place this card as 1 of
your non-white Digimon's bottom digivolution card`, so the used card goes to the
digivolution stack and never reaches the trash; the file also never imported
`../P/P-146.js`, so the module registered nothing. The predicate had always been
false: it only started failing once `bfa5aba87 test(testkit): make settle throw
when its predicate never holds` turned a silently-satisfied `settle` into an
error. Fix: import the P-146 module and settle on the card leaving hand, then
assert the real end state (instance in `perm("base").stack`, absent from trash,
memory 2, black `[Plug-In]` still in hand, no pending decision). No engine
regression and no IR defect; the end-state assertions were strengthened, not
weakened.

Test hygiene fixed: two numeric `security: <n>` fixtures replaced with explicit
inert main-deck Digimon (`BT1-012`); an unused `optionId` binding turned into a
real trash-zone assertion; endpoint assertions (memory, hand, trash, stack, no
pending decision) added to the two pre-existing behavioural tests.

(memory 6 -> 2: digivolve 3 + P-146 cost 3 reduced to 1) | effect 1, `UseOptionWithoutCost`, `from: ["hand"]`, `payCost`, `reduceCostBy: 2`, `or: [name Plug-In, colors Yellow]` |

| 3 | "resets the inherited once-per-turn use on the next own turn" (real turn loop, gate resets); "uses the reduced-cost Option from a Sakuyamon host when attacking" (real attack, memory unchanged because the reduced cost is 0); "does not offer the inherited use when the host has no Sakuyamon in its name" (BT17-032 host, Option stays in hand); "uses the inherited attack effect only once per turn" (two real attacks in one turn, second Option stays in hand) | effect 2, `isInherited`, `WhenAttacking`, `frequency: OncePerTurn`, `condition: selfHasNameContaining ["Sakuyamon"]` |

### BT17-036 — Boutmon

Lane 42. Card-only. Module IR unchanged; only the test and this report were edited.

#### Card summary (printed clauses)

Boutmon, Digimon, Lv5, Yellow/Green, DP 7000, play cost 7. EvoCosts Lv4 Yellow/Green
cost 4.

1. `[Digivolve]Lv.4 w/[Pulsemon] in its text: Cost 3` — alternate reduced route from any
   Lv4 whose text union holds "Pulsemon".
2. `[All Turns] [Once Per Turn]` When Boutmon would leave the battle area by an opponent's
   effect, trash the top security card to prevent it.
3. `[All Turns] [Once Per Turn]` When a card is trashed from your security stack by an
   effect, Boutmon with `[Leon Alexander]` in its digivolution cards may digivolve into a
   `[Pulsemon]`-text Digimon in hand without paying the cost.
4. Inherited `[End of Attack] [Once Per Turn]` If this Digimon has `[Pulsemon]` in its
   text, trash the top security card to unsuspend this Digimon.

Catalog carries U+00A0 before "in its text", "in its digivolution cards", "in the hand".

#### Q&A ids covered

- Q2786 ("what does 'would leave the battle area' mean"): the answer enumerates trash,
  return to hand/deck, place in security, move to breeding, place under another card.
  Covered by the clause-2 behavioural tests, which exercise an effect-driven deletion
  (one such leave cause) and prove the prevention fires. The clause is modeled with
  `event: "wouldLeavePlay"` + `leaveCause: "byOpponentEffect"`, which is the single
  replacement hook every leave cause routes through, so the ruling is honoured for all
  listed causes; deletion is the representative case tested.

#### Clause -> test -> IR mapping

| Clause | Test | IR |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Printed text, evoCosts, alt route | "matches the catalog printed text..." | `digivolutionRequirement [{level:4, texts:["Pulsemon"], cost:3, isAlternate:true}]` |
| 1 alternate route | "digivolves from a Lv4 Pulsemon-text source for 3 ... and draws one" | same requirement, `texts` gate (cardData.ts) |
| 1 printed Lv4 route | "digivolves from a plain Yellow Lv4 ... for 4" | `evoCosts` |
| 1 negatives | "refuses the alternate route from a source that lacks Pulsemon ... or the level" | `texts` + `level` gates |
| 2 prevention | "trashes its top security card to prevent an opponent-effect deletion" | `Replacement wouldLeavePlay / byOpponentEffect / trashSecurityTop` |
| 2 OPT + reset | "prevents only once per turn and resets on the next turn"; "prevents again on a later opponent turn" | `frequency: OncePerTurn` |
| 3 free digivolve | "digivolves for free into a Pulsemon-text Digimon when an effect trashes its security with Leon underneath" | `SubTrigger whenEffectTrashesFromSecurity -> Digivolve payCost:false` |
| 3 Leon gate | "does not free-digivolve when a security card is trashed but no Leon is underneath" | `digivolutionStackNameOrTrait [Leon Alexander]` |
| 4 inherited unsuspend | "unsuspends the attacker at end of attack when its top card has Pulsemon in text" | `EndOfAttack Unsuspend / selfTopHasText / trashSecurityTop` |
| 4 gate negative | "does not unsuspend when Pulsemon is only on an underneath card" | `selfTopHasText` (correct per §4-23-2) |

Also kept: three structural `toMatchObject` clause->IR shape checks.

#### Defects / retained red

None. No retained red remains in this file.

The former `it.fails` "should still unsuspend when Pulsemon is only on an underneath card
(whole-Digimon text)" was an authoring error, not an engine gap. `data/kb/rules/comprehensive.md`
§4-23-2 — "When an effect is gained, that doesn't mean that text is gained", with this exact
shape as its worked example — says a Digimon does not gain a digivolution card's text. So
"If this Digimon has [Pulsemon] in its text" reads the top card only, `selfTopMatchesText` in
`apps/api/src/engine/effects/interpreter/matching/permanent.ts` is correct, and Boutmon under a
non-Pulsemon top card is correctly gated off.

The test is now a plain `it` asserting the correct endpoint: with `BT1-009` (no Pulsemon text)
on top of Boutmon, the attacker is still suspended after the attack settles, security still holds
1 card, `state.pendingDecision` is undefined and `state.memory` is unchanged. The positive case
(Pulsemon `BT16-047` on top, unsuspend fires and security is spent) sits directly beside it.

The whole-stack reading was tried by the engine lane before the rule was found and made the gate
unconditional, breaking `BT17-034.test.ts:471`; it was reverted. Detail:
`WHOLE-DIGIMON-TEXT-MECHANISM.md`.

No local defects fixed (module IR was already correct).

- Clause 2 tested through effect-driven deletion only; other Q2786 leave causes (return,
  to-security, to-breeding, place-under) share the same `wouldLeavePlay` hook and are not
  separately exercised.
- Clause 3 positive is proven through Dynasmon's `[When Attacking]` security trash and the
  Leon negative through the `trashFromSecurity` verb; a same-card comparative peer case
  was not added beyond the Leon-present vs Leon-absent pair in the same fixture.

None. Catalog text matches the printed card. `texts`-gated alternate route (not `names`)
is correct for "w/[Pulsemon] in its text".

### BT17-037 — RizeGreymon

Digimon, Lv5, Yellow, Vaccine, Cyborg, 7000 DP, play 7, digivolve Yellow Lv4 cost 3.

#### Printed clauses

1. `[Your Turn]` While you have a suspended Tamer, this Digimon gets +3000 DP and gains ＜Piercing＞.
2. `[When Digivolving]` `[When Attacking]` By suspending 1 of your yellow Tamers, 1 of your
   opponent's Digimon gets -3000 DP for the turn.
3. Inherited `[All Turns]` `[Once Per Turn]` When one of your yellow or red Tamers is deleted,
   place 1 `[Marcus Damon]` from your trash on top of your security stack.

Catalog text matches the official list. No discrepancy.

#### Q&A covered

- **Q2787** — a deleted `[Marcus Damon]` may itself be placed on security.
  Test: "places the deleted Marcus Damon itself on top of security" — the Tamer permanent is
  deleted, lands in the trash, and the same instance id is found at `security[0]` face down.

#### Clause → test → IR

| Clause | Test | IR |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 1 (DP + Piercing) | "grants +3000 DP and Piercing only on your turn while a Tamer is suspended" | `YourTurn` → two `Aura` actions with `while: youHave { suspended, kind: Tamer }` |
| 1 (structure) | "gains DP and Piercing with a suspended Tamer" | same |
| 2 (When Digivolving) | "suspends a yellow Tamer on digivolution, reduces DP, and enables its aura" | `WhenDigivolving` → `ModifyDP -3000`, `cost.suspend` yellow Tamer |
| 2 (When Attacking) | "suspends a yellow Tamer when attacking and leaves a non-yellow Tamer alone" | `WhenAttacking` → same shape |
| 2 (structure) | "suspends a yellow Tamer to reduce one opposing Digimon by 3000 when digivolving or attacking" | both triggers |
| 3 (natural trigger) | "naturally reacts when an Option effect deletes a red or yellow Tamer" | inherited `SubTrigger onDeletionOf` → `SecurityManipulation placeAsSecurity` |
| 3 (Q2787) | "places the deleted Marcus Damon itself on top of security" | same |
| 3 (name match + OPT + reset) | "ignores Marcus Damon & Agumon and fires only once per turn" | `nameOrTrait [{ tokens: ["Marcus Damon"], match: "nameExact" }]`, `frequency: "OncePerTurn"` |

#### Behavioural endpoints asserted

- Aura: `currentDP` 10000 with the Tamer suspended, 7000 unsuspended, 10000 re-suspended,
  7000 on the opponent's turn; `observe(...).hasPierce` toggles with it; the opposing Lv5
  keeps 6000 DP and no Pierce (peer case, self-only aura).
- When Attacking: only the yellow Tamer suspends (a red-only Tamer, BT1-085, is left
  unsuspended — comparative cost-filter case); the attacked Digimon drops 20000 → 17000.
- Inherited: security length 1 → 2 with the exact `[Marcus Damon]` on top, a second Tamer
  deletion in the same turn leaves it at 2, and after `advance(...).runTurn(1)` a third
  deletion places the next Marcus Damon (BT13-095), taking security to 3. AD1-021
  "Marcus Damon & Agumon" stays in the trash throughout.

#### Defects fixed locally

1. **Set-wide name defect.** `BT17-037.ts` compiled `[Marcus Damon]` as
   `match: "name"` (substring), which would also match AD1-021 "Marcus Damon & Agumon" and
   ST24-13 "Marcus Damon & Thomas H. Norstein". Changed to `match: "nameExact"`. **IR changed —
   `BT17-catalog-sync.test.ts` needs the coordinator's set sync.**
2. **Digi-Egg in security.** Two fixtures seeded `security: ["BT1-001"]` (a Digi-Egg).
   Replaced with the inert main-deck `BT1-009`.

### BT17-038 — Sakuyamon

#### Card summary

BT17-038 Sakuyamon (Digimon, Lv6, Yellow, 11000 DP, Shaman/Data/Mega). Printed clauses:

1. `[Digivolve][Sakuyamon: Maid Mode]: Cost 1` — alternate named digivolution route (no
   "in name").
2. `＜Barrier＞` — static keyword.
3. `[When Digivolving] 1 of your opponent's Digimon gets -6000 DP for the turn. Then, you
   may use 1 Option card with [Plug-In] in its name ... or 1 yellow Option card with a cost
   of 5 or less from your hand without paying the cost.`
4. `[Your Turn] [Once Per Turn] When you use an Option card with a cost of 2 or more, this
   Digimon can't be returned to hand or deck by your opponent's effects until the end of
   their turn.`

No `[Security]` clause on this card (catalog carries none).

#### Catalog discrepancy

`effectText` prints the Plug-In branch as "...with [Plug-In] in its name **in its name** or
1 yellow Option...". The phrase "in its name" is duplicated — a catalog typo versus the
official text (single "in its name"). Reported only; catalog not edited. The IR filter is
correct (single `nameOrTrait` name-match token), so behaviour is unaffected.

#### Q&A coverage (Q2788, Q2789, Q2790, Q5457, Q5458)

- **Q5458** (unpaid effect-driven use of a cost-2+ Option still triggers the watcher):
  covered behaviourally — "reduces DP, uses a free cost-2 yellow Option, and locks itself
  against opponent return". The `[When Digivolving]` free-use of BT1-102 (Yellow, cost 2)
  fires the `[Your Turn]` watcher and applies the `beReturned` restriction.
- **Q5457** (payment-only cost reduction keeps original use cost → triggers): covered by the
  paid cost-2 use test (BT1-102 used at its printed cost of 2, watcher fires). The engine
  reads `usedOptionCost` = use cost after card-level changes, before payment-only reductions
  (`conditions.ts` `triggerOptionCostAtLeast`, `primitives.ts` `useOptionFromHand`
  `projectedHandUseCost`), so a payment-only discount does not change the gate.
- **Q2790** (card-level cost reduction to ≤1 → does not trigger) and **Q2788** (the watcher
  activates after the used Option's `[Main]` resolves): covered by engine mechanism, not by a
  dedicated fixture. The fire path resolves the Option's `[Main]`, trashes it, then fires
  `whenOptionUsed` with the card-level use cost; a card-level reduction below 2 would set
  `usedOptionCost` < 2 and skip. Building a card-level-reduction fixture needs an unrelated
  reducer card (e.g. BT2-099/BT8-097) — out of this lane's allowed files; documented rather
  than tested. The cost-1 negative test ("does not lock ... less than 2") exercises the same
  `< 2` gate directly.
- **Q2789** (Option activated by `[Security]`/`＜Delay＞` does not trigger): covered by engine
  design — `fireOptionUsed`/`whenOptionUsed` fire only on a genuine "use" (playCard or the
  use-option-without-cost verb), never on a `[Security]`/`＜Delay＞` activation (BT19-040 design
  note). No dedicated fixture (needs a security/delay Option path from another card).

#### Clause → IR → test mapping

| Clause | IR | Test |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `[Sakuyamon: Maid Mode]: Cost 1` | `digivolutionRequirement[0] = {namesExact, cost:1, isAlternate}` | "digivolves through the printed Sakuyamon: Maid Mode route for 1"; negative "refuses a near-name Sakuyamon base ..." |
| `＜Barrier＞` | `effects[0]` Static keyword Barrier | asserted via `observe.hasKeyword(base,"Barrier")` in both digivolve tests |
| `-6000 DP for the turn` | `WhenDigivolving.actions[0]` ModifyDP -6000 forTheTurn, opponent Digimon x1 | "reduces DP, uses a free cost-2 yellow Option ..." (9000 → 3000) |
| free Option use | `WhenDigivolving.actions[1]` UseOptionWithoutCost (Plug-In name OR yellow ≤5) | yellow (BT1-102), red Plug-In (EX2-066), negative (BT5-102 + color-illegal EX2-066) |
| `[Your Turn]` return lock | `YourTurn` OncePerTurn SubTrigger whenOptionUsed ≥2 → Restrict beReturned untilOpponentTurnEnd byOpponentEffectsOnly isSelfRef | free cost-2 (Q5458) and paid cost-2 tests assert `isRestricted(beReturned)`; cost-1 negative asserts absent |

#### Evolution routes

- Normal Lv5-Yellow evoCost (memory 3): exercised by the DP/free-option test digivolving from
  BT17-035 Taomon (memory 3 → 0), with source-stack + Barrier asserted.
- Alternate named route (`useAlternateCost: true`, cost 1): digivolve from BT10-041
  "Sakuyamon: Maid Mode"; memory 3 → 2 asserted, source-stack identity `[BT10-041]`, bonus
  draw landed. Not a bare `{ok:true}`.
- Illegal-source negative: BT5-044 "Sakuyamon" (Lv6) refused on both the exact route and the
  Lv5 evoCost.

#### Defects fixed locally

- **IR change (route name):** `digivolutionRequirement` changed from `names`
  (substring) to `namesExact`, per the coordinator route-name decision — the printed
  `[Digivolve][X]: Cost N` has no "in name" so the base name must equal, not contain, the
  route name. Reported below as an IR change.
- Replaced the injected-timing return-lock test (`advance().fireSubTrigger`, no behavioural
  credit) with two real-use tests (free via `[When Digivolving]`, and paid via `playCard`),
  plus a cost-1 negative. Added a real alternate-route positive and a near-name negative, and
  a catalog/IR contract test.

#### Retained reds / seams

None retained red inside this test file (8/8 green). One expected cross-file red: see below.

#### Remaining gaps

- **Once-per-turn reset** is not independently observable: the restriction is a binary self
  `beReturned` lock, so a second same-turn cost-2 use and a next-own-turn reset produce no
  distinct visible state. The `OncePerTurn` frequency mechanic is proven on an observable
  debuff by the near-matching sibling BT17-032 (same printed clause shape).
- **`byOpponentEffectsOnly` endpoint** (an actual opponent return effect being prevented while
  an own return still works) is asserted via the `isRestricted(beReturned)` proxy, not by
  driving an opponent return card (would need a non-allowed card in the fixture).
- Q2788/Q2789/Q2790 covered by mechanism, not dedicated fixtures (see Q&A section).

### BT17-039 — ShineGreymon

Digimon, Lv6, Yellow, Vaccine, Light Dragon, 11000 DP, play 11, digivolve Yellow Lv5 cost 3.

   opponent's effect, by returning 1 of your yellow Tamers to the hand, prevent it from leaving.

  security, move to breeding and placement under another card.
  Covered for two of those modes: deletion to trash ("returns a yellow Tamer to prevent an
  opponent-effect deletion", "prevents only once per turn and resets on the next turn") and a
  natural return-to-hand from an opponent Option ("returns a yellow Tamer to prevent a natural
  opponent Option return", BT3-104). The remaining modes (security, breeding, placed under
  another card) share the same `wouldLeavePlay` replacement seam and no BT17-legal fixture adds
  behavioural information beyond the two proven paths; listed as a gap rather than claimed.

#### Clause → test → IR

| Clause | Test | IR |

| 2 (deletion) | "returns a yellow Tamer to prevent an opponent-effect deletion" | same |
| 2 (return to hand, natural) | "returns a yellow Tamer to prevent a natural opponent Option return" | same |
| 2 (once per turn) | "prevents only once per turn and resets on the next turn" | `frequency: "OncePerTurn"` |
| 2 (reset) | "resets the prevention on a later turn" | same |

- Clause 2: the ShineGreymon permanent id is still in the battle area after an opponent-effect

- Once per turn: the second opponent-effect deletion in the same turn removes ShineGreymon

- Reset: after `advance(...).runTurn(1)` a further opponent-effect deletion is prevented again

### BT17-040 — Kazuchimon

Lane 44. Digimon, Lv6, Yellow/Green, DP 12000, types Shaman / Abadin Electronics.

#### Printed clauses

1. `[Digivolve]Lv.5 w/[Pulsemon]` (U+00A0) `in its text: Cost 3` — alternate reduced-cost route.
2. `[When Digivolving]` Suspend 1 opponent Digimon. Then, if [Leon Alexander] is in this
   Digimon's digivolution cards, all opponent Digimon gain ＜Security Attack -1＞ until end of
   their turn.
3. `[End of Your Turn] [Once Per Turn]` If ≥3 security → 1 opponent Digimon -6000 DP for the
   turn. If ≤3 security → ＜Recovery +1 (Deck)＞. Then, 1 of your Digimon may attack an
   opponent's Digimon.
4. Inherited `[All Turns] [Once Per Turn]` When a card is removed from your security stack, if
   this Digimon has [Fenriloogamon] in its name, 1 opponent Digimon -8000 DP for the turn.

No `[Security]` clause is printed or omitted. Catalog text matches the official list.

#### Defect found and fixed (IR change)

The alternate digivolve route (clause 1) was entirely absent from the module: no
`digivolutionRequirement`, no `data.ts` override, no generated override, no `effects.json`
entry. The reduced cost-3 path was therefore unplayable — the player could only ever pay the
catalog cost 4. Sibling cards encode this same kind of route as a module
`digivolutionRequirement` (BT17-007, BT17-015), and `digivolutionRequirementsFor` falls back to
`compiledEffects[id].digivolutionRequirement`.

Fix (in my allowed file `BT17-040.ts`):

```ts
digivolutionRequirement: [
  { level: 5, texts: ["Pulsemon"], cost: 3, isAlternate: true },
],
```

"in its text" is the source's full card-information union (`cardData.ts` `req.texts` gate:
names + types + forms + attributes + every effect field), so `texts` is correct, not `names`.
Verified: BT17-036 Boutmon (Lv5 Yellow/Green, "Pulsemon" in its effectText) is accepted for
cost 3; BT1-057 Sirenmon (Lv5 Yellow, no Pulsemon) is rejected by the alternate gate and falls
back to catalog cost 4.

This is an IR change. `BT17-catalog-sync.test.ts` will go red until the coordinator runs the
BT17 set sync (effects.json carries `null` for this field today). Not touched, per brief.

#### Q&A coverage

- Q2792 (later-entrant opponent Digimon also get ＜Security A. -1＞): covered — test plays a
  Digimon after resolution and asserts it reads -1 (`includeLaterEntrants: true`).
- Q2793 (exactly 3 security → both the -6000 and Recovery branches fire): covered — the
  exact-three tests assert the target dies and the recovered card lands in security.
- Q2794 (the post-"then" attack happens regardless of the security count, even if Recovery
  raised it to 4): covered — the natural end-of-turn test recovers then still attacks and
  removes the target; the Attack action carries no security condition in the IR.

#### Clause → action → test

| Clause | IR action | Test |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 1 alt route | `digivolutionRequirement[texts:Pulsemon,cost:3]` | alternate-route (cost 3), same-base-no-flag (cost 4), Sirenmon fallback (cost 4), off-color refusal, catalog-match |
| 2 suspend + keyword | `Suspend` + `GainKeyword`/`selfDigivolutionStackHasTrait` | "suspends one opponent and gives all opponents Security Attack -1 with Leon underneath" |
| 3 DP-loss branch | `ModifyDP -6000` / `securityAtLeast 3` | exact-three (injected + natural); ">3 does not recover" |
| 3 recovery branch | `SecurityManipulation addTop` / `zoneCount lte 3` | exact-three natural (recovered card in security); ">3 does not recover" (security stays 4) |
| 3 attack | `Attack optional` | exact-three natural (target removed after recovery) — Q2794 |
| 4 inherited | `SubTrigger whenSecurityRemoved` + `ModifyDP -8000`/`selfHasNameContaining` | injected + natural (host BT17-101 → 12000-8000=4000) |

#### Behavioural notes

- Real routes exercised through `applyIntent({type:"digivolve"})` with memory deltas (5→2 alt,
  5→1 catalog/fallback) and the digivolve bonus draw (deck top → hand), plus stack identity.
- Natural turn-loop coverage via `runOneTurn`/`endPhase` for the end-of-turn effect and via a
  real attack for the inherited security-removal trigger.
- The two injected-timing tests (`advance().fire`, `fireSubTrigger`) are structural duplicates
  of natural tests that already exist; kept, but they earn no behavioural credit.
- All fixtures avoid the flagged smells: explicit inert main-deck Digimon in security
  (BT1-009/010/012/013), no numeric `security:<n>`, no Digi-Egg in security. The two
  pre-existing `security: 3` fixtures were converted to three inert cards per coordinator note.

#### Peer/stack

Comparative cases present: two opponent Digimon plus a later entrant for the "all opponent
Digimon" clause (Q2792); the alternate vs. catalog cost compared on the *same* base
(BT17-036); real evolution stacks (Leon under the base for clause 2; BT17-040 under a
Fenriloogamon host BT17-101 for clause 4), with stack identity asserted.

  behaviour is). Minor.
- `BT17-catalog-sync.test.ts` red until set sync (see above) — coordinator action.

None. Printed text, evoCosts (Yellow/Green Lv5 cost 4), colors, level, DP all match.

### BT17-041 — ShineGreymon: Burst Mode

#### Card summary

Digimon, Lv7, Yellow, Vaccine, Light Dragon, DP 15000, play cost 8, ACE
(overflow -5). Catalog `evoCosts`: Yellow Lv6 for 5.

Printed clauses:

1. `[Digivolve][ShineGreymon]: Cost 4` — alternate route from an exactly named
   `ShineGreymon`.
2. `[Hand] [Counter] ＜Blast Digivolve＞`.
3. `[On Play] [When Digivolving]` — may play 1 Tamer from hand without paying
   the cost; then 1 opponent Digimon gets -5000 DP for the turn for each of
   your Tamers.
4. `[When Attacking]` — by suspending up to 2 of your yellow Tamers, gain
   ＜Security Attack +1＞ for the turn for every Tamer suspended.

No inherited effect.

#### Q&A ids covered

KB-INDEX lists none for BT17-041; `node tools/kb/query.mjs card BT17-041`
returns no rulings. Nothing to cover.

#### Defect fixed

`digivolutionRequirement` compiled the printed `[ShineGreymon]` bracket as
`names: ["ShineGreymon"]`, the substring gate
(`matchingAlternateDigivolutionRequirement` in
`apps/api/src/engine/cards/cardData.ts` uses `name.includes(token)` for
`names`, `name === token` for `namesExact`). The catalog holds
"ShineGreymon: Ruin Mode" (EX4-074) and two other "ShineGreymon: Burst Mode"
prints, so the substring form wrongly opened the cost-4 route from those Lv7
bases. Changed to `namesExact: ["ShineGreymon"]`. This is the known set-wide
`[Name]` defect. **IR changed — `BT17-catalog-sync.test.ts` will be red until
the coordinator runs the set sync.**

#### Engine seam closed

`it("gains only one Security Attack when a single yellow Tamer is available")`
— green; the assertion is unchanged.

- File: `apps/api/src/engine/effects/interpreter/costs.ts`, `canPayCost`,
  the `cost.kind === "suspend"` branch.
- Was: it returned `required > 0 && candidates.length >= required` and never
  read `cost.target.upTo`, unlike every neighbouring cost kind (`deleteOwn`,
  `trash`-from-security, `return`). With one legal yellow Tamer the "up to 2"
  cost was judged unpayable, so no decision was raised, nothing suspended and
  Security Attack stayed 0.
- Now: `if (cost.target?.upTo === true) return candidates.length > 0;` — the
  player chooses how many to suspend and `usePaidCount` scaling grants
  ＜Security Attack +1＞ for the one Tamer paid. Zero candidates stays
  unpayable (Comprehensive Rules 15-8-4-4-1, unperformable optional process),
  so "still attacks with no Security Attack bonus when no yellow Tamer can be
  suspended" still asserts no pending decision.
- Red-then-green: `Tests 1 failed | 11 passed (12)` before, `Tests 12 passed
  (12)` after. Full write-up:
  `docs/audits/BT17-reaudit/SUSPEND-UPTO-COST-MECHANISM.md`.

The IR was always correct; the gap was the engine's payability precheck.

#### Clause → test → IR

| Clause | Test | IR |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Catalog identity, printed text, both routes | "matches the catalog identity, printed text and evolution routes" | `digivolutionRequirement: [{ namesExact: ["ShineGreymon"], cost: 4, isAlternate: true }]` |
| ＜Blast Digivolve＞ structure | "has Blast Digivolve and plays a Tamer…" | `trigger: "Counter"`, `isFromHand: true`, `keywords: [BlastDigivolve]` |
| On Play / When Digivolving structure | same test | two effects, `PlayWithoutCost` then `ModifyDP` with Tamer `scaling` |
| When Attacking structure | "gains Security Attack +1 per yellow Tamer suspended…" | `GainKeyword` with `cost.kind: "suspend"`, `upTo: true`, `scaling.usePaidCount` |
| On Play behaviour: play a Tamer, then -5000 × Tamers | "plays a Tamer from hand and scales the on-play DP reduction by the Tamer count" (20000 → 10000 with 2 Tamers, played Tamer on board, hand down to the spare, no pending decision) | `PlayWithoutCost` + `ModifyDP` scaling |
| Per-Tamer scaling is a real count | "reduces DP by only 5000 when a single Tamer is on the board" (20000 → 15000) | `scaling: { per: 1, filter: Tamer }` |
| Alternate route costs 4 and fires When Digivolving | "digivolves from an exact [ShineGreymon] for 4 and fires When Digivolving" (memory 8 → 4, stack `["BT1-009","BT17-039"]`, Tamer played, target 20000 → 15000) | `namesExact` route, `WhenDigivolving` effect |
| Catalog route costs 5 | "digivolves from a plain yellow Lv6 through the printed catalog route for 5" (ST3-10 base, memory 8 → 3) | `evoCosts` |
| Illegal source negative | "refuses a ShineGreymon: Ruin Mode base…" (`{ ok:false, reason:"invalid-evolution" }`, memory unchanged, card still in hand) | `namesExact` |
| ＜Blast Digivolve＞ behaviour | "Blast Digivolves from hand during the opponent's attack without paying memory" (real `counterWindowOpened` → `respondCounter`, memory stays 0, When Digivolving still plays the Tamer) | `Counter` effect |
| Security Attack +2 from two suspended yellow Tamers | "gains one Security Attack for each yellow Tamer suspended when attacking" (both Tamers suspended, 3 security checked and trashed, attacker suspended) | `usePaidCount` scaling |
| Colour gate on the cost | "gains only one Security Attack when a single yellow Tamer is available" — green | `cost.target.filter.colors: ["Yellow"]` |
| No yellow Tamer: attack still happens, no bonus | "still attacks with no Security Attack bonus when no yellow Tamer can be suspended" (red Tamer untouched, 1 security checked, attacker suspended, no pending decision) | `optional: true`, `abortOnDecline: true` |

#### Catalog discrepancies

None. `effectText` matches the official list, including the ＜Security
Attack +1＞ run-on with no space before "for the turn".

### BT17-042 — Argomon

#### Card summary

Digimon, Lv3, Green/Purple, DP 2000, play cost 3. Catalog `evoCosts`:
Green Lv2 for 1, Purple Lv2 for 1.

Printed clauses:

1. `[Digivolve]Lv.2 [Argomon]: Cost 0` — alternate route from a Lv2 card named
   exactly `Argomon`.
2. `[On Play] Reveal the top 3 cards of your deck. Add 1 [Argomon] and 1
   [Rhythm] among them to the hand. Return the rest to the bottom of the deck.`
3. Inherited: `[On Deletion] Gain 1 memory.`

#### Q&A ids covered

- **Q2795** — "if only one of [Argomon]/[Rhythm] is revealed, can I add just
  that card?" Yes. Covered by "adds the only applicable card when just one
  bracket is revealed (Q2795)": a board where the three revealed cards contain
  an Argomon and no Rhythm; the Argomon lands in hand and the other two go to
  the bottom in offered order.
- **Q2796** — "if both are revealed, may I add only one?" No; you must add as
  many as possible. Covered by "adds the revealed Argomon and Rhythm and
  bottoms the remainder (Q2796)": the run sets no `autoAcceptOptional`, so a
  mandatory add is the only way both cards can move, and the test asserts both
  in hand, hand size 3, deck size 2 and `pendingDecision` undefined.

#### Defect fixed

Three printed `[Name]` brackets compiled as substring gates:
`[Argomon]` and `[Rhythm]` in the On Play add as `match: "name"` (now
`match: "nameExact"`), and `[Argomon]` in the digivolution requirement as
`names` (now `namesExact`). This is the known set-wide `[Name]` defect.

Honest note on impact: the current catalog holds no card whose `nameEn`
strictly contains "Argomon" or "Rhythm" as a substring, so the two forms are
behaviourally indistinguishable today and no test can separate them. The fix
is correctness-of-intent, not an observed misbehaviour. **IR changed —
`BT17-catalog-sync.test.ts` will be red until the coordinator runs the set
sync.**

#### Clause → test → IR

| Clause | Test | IR |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Catalog identity, printed text, inherited text, route | "matches the catalog identity, printed text and evolution route" | `digivolutionRequirement: [{ namesExact: ["Argomon"], level: 2, cost: 0, isAlternate: true }]`, `coverage: "full"`, `residual: []` |
| Bracketed names are exact | "compiles the printed [Argomon]/[Rhythm] brackets as exact names, not substrings" | `nameOrTrait: [{ tokens: [...], match: "nameExact" }]` |
| Inherited On Deletion structure | "gains one memory on deletion as an inherited effect" | `isInherited: true`, `GainMemory` |
| Reveal 3, add both, bottom the rest (Q2796) | "adds the revealed Argomon and Rhythm and bottoms the remainder (Q2796)" | `RevealAdd` `revealCount: 3`, two `add` entries, `rest: "deckBottom"` |
| Add whatever matches (Q2795) | "adds the only applicable card when just one bracket is revealed (Q2795)" — deck order asserted exactly | same |
| Nothing matches | "bottoms all three when neither bracket is revealed" — hand keeps only the spare, deck order asserted exactly | `rest: "deckBottom"` |
| `count: 1` per bracket | "adds only one of two revealed [Argomon] cards, alongside the [Rhythm]" — 1 of 2 Argomon in hand, the other bottomed | `add[].count: 1` |
| Alternate Lv2 [Argomon] route for 0 | "publicly digivolves from a Lv2 [Argomon] Digi-Egg through the printed route for 0" (BT17-004 base, memory stays 0, stack identity asserted) | `digivolutionRequirement` |
| Catalog route for 1 | "publicly digivolves from a green Lv2 Digi-Egg through the normal route for 1" (BT1-007 Tanemon, memory 0 → -1) | `evoCosts` |
| Illegal alternate source | "refuses the cost-0 route from a Lv2 Digi-Egg that is not named Argomon" — `useAlternateCost: true` falls back, memory delta -1 is the proof, not `{ok:true}` | `namesExact` gate |
| Inherited On Deletion behaviour | "gives its controller 1 memory when the Digimon above it is deleted in battle" — real attack into a 20000-DP suspended wall, both cards in trash, memory 3 → 4 | `OnDeletion` inherited |
| Comparative negative for the same clause | "does not give memory when the Argomon under a survivor is untouched" — attacker survives, memory unchanged at 3 | same |

breeding, the legal zone for a Digi-Egg), no numeric `security: <n>` form,

20000-DP host trick used for the battle case, and no injected timing — the
only `advance` call is `verb.unsuspend` as plumbing after a real battle.

- No test of the inherited clause firing from security or from breeding
