#### Engine lane E2 resolution (session 3) — retained red CLOSED

The seam was real and is fixed. `combat/controller.ts` now captures
`defenderAtDeclaration` (the defender's digivolution-card count at attack declaration, the
sibling of the existing `attackerDPAtDeclaration`), and `attackTargetMatchesFilter` answers
the digivolution-stack half of its filter from that snapshot instead of from live state, so
a same-window inherited trash cannot retroactively arm the delete (Q2816).

`pnpm --filter @aegis/api exec vitest run src/cards/BT17/BT17-064.test.ts --maxWorkers=1 --no-file-parallelism`
red `Tests 7 passed | 1 expected fail (8)` -> green `Tests 8 passed (8)`.

One assertion in the reproducer was corrected, not weakened: it expected BT17-064 to be
deleted by the battle, but Pipismon carries ＜Armor Purge＞ and a BT16-016 source, so it
trashes its top card and promotes [Patamon]. The endpoint is now asserted precisely (one
permanent, top card BT16-016, empty stack).

### BT17-065 — DexDorugamon

Lane 50. Digimon, Lv4, Purple/Black, DP 6000, play cost 6.

#### Printed clauses

1. `[Digivolve][Dorugamon]: Cost 0` — alternate digivolution route. No "in name",
   so the route name is **exact** (coordinator decision): `namesExact: ["Dorugamon"]`.
2. `[Trash] [All Turns] When one of your [Dorugamon] would be deleted, by
   digivolving it into this card without paying the cost, prevent that deletion.`
   Replacement from trash: `event: wouldBeDeleted`, `digivolveFromTrash: true`,
   `sourceFilter {zone: trash, controller: mine}`, `abortOnDecline`, `leaveCause: any`.
3. `[When Digivolving] Trash 1 card in your hand. Then, ＜Draw 1＞. If [Dorugamon]
   is in this Digimon's digivolution cards or this digivolved from the trash,
   delete 1 of your opponent's Digimon with a play cost of 4 or less instead.`
   Mandatory hand Trash, then a `not(anyOf)` Draw vs an `anyOf` Delete on the same
   `selfHasInDigivolutionCards[Dorugamon] || digivolvedFromZone[trash]` condition.
4. Inherited `＜Reboot＞`.

#### Q&A coverage

- **Q2817** (when the [Trash] effect triggers): covered behaviourally — the
  trash-digivolve replacement fires while DexDorugamon sits in trash and a
  Dorugamon would be deleted ("digivolves from trash..." test).
- **Q2820** (delete replaces the Draw, not the hand trash): covered by IR shape
  (Trash unconditional; Draw `not(anyOf)`; Delete `anyOf`) and behaviourally —
  the [Dorugamon]-route case deletes and still trashes a hand card; the normal-route
  case draws and still trashes a hand card.
- **Q2818 / Q2819** (DP-reduced-to-0 deletion prevented by the digivolve, but the
  DP debuff carries over so deletion recurs, and [When Digivolving] then does not
  activate): **documented untestable**. The testkit deletes by `RemovalCause`
  (`verb.deletePermanent(..., "byEffect")`), not through a lingering DP-to-0
  modifier that re-evaluates the deletion after the replacement digivolves the
  base. Reproducing the recurrence and the [When Digivolving] suppression needs a
  persistent DP-reduction debuff the harness does not expose, so no honest
  behavioural test exists. Not a single named engine seam; left as a gap.

#### Clause -> test -> IR mapping

| Clause | Test | IR |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Route (exact name, cost 0) | "digivolves Dorugamon through the [Dorugamon] cost-0 route..."; "refuses ... near-name source" | `digivolutionRequirement[0].namesExact` |
| [Trash] replacement | "digivolves from trash to prevent..."; "allows ... when unavailable" | effect `isFromTrash` Replacement |
| [When Digivolving] delete branch | "[Dorugamon] cost-0 route... deletes"; "from trash... deletes instead of drawing" | Delete action, `condition anyOf` |
| [When Digivolving] draw branch | "non-Dorugamon Lv3... draws instead of deleting" | Draw action, `condition not(anyOf)` |
| Mandatory hand Trash | both branch tests assert the hand card lands in trash | Trash action (no condition) |
| Inherited Reboot | "grants inherited Reboot..." | inherited Static keyword |

Evolution routes proven: real `applyIntent(digivolve)` on the public API —
same-level [Dorugamon] cost-0 route (memory stays 0, Dorugamon in stack), normal
Lv3->Lv4 evo route (memory 3->0), near-name negative (`BT9-075 DexDorugamon`
refused on both the alternate and normal routes; DP/hand/stack unchanged). No
DNA/Tamer routes are printed.

   `Darcmon`, cheaper than the printed Lv4 routes.
2. `＜Blocker＞` (main).
3. `[When Digivolving] You may play 1 level 3 Purple or Yellow Digimon card from your hand
   without paying the cost.` — optional, hand only, level 3, Purple or Yellow.
4. Inherited: `＜Blocker＞.`

#### Q&A ids covered

`node tools/kb/query.mjs card BT17-066` returns `(no knowledge-base entries)`. The lane
assignment lists no Q&A ids. Nothing to cover.

#### Clause -> test -> IR mapping

| Clause | Test | IR |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Catalog identity + whole IR | `matches the catalog and the complete IR contract` | `compiled.effects` deep equality plus `digivolutionRequirement: [{ names: ["Darcmon"], cost: 3, isAlternate: true }]`, `coverage: "full"`, `residual: []` |
| `[Digivolve][Darcmon]: Cost 3` + free play | `uses the Darcmon route for 3 memory and plays a yellow level 3 from hand without cost` | `digivolutionRequirement`, `PlayWithoutCost` |
| Printed Lv4 route costs 4 | `uses the printed level 4 route for 4 memory when no alternate is named` | catalog `evoCosts` |
| Illegal source | `refuses a level 4 Red source that matches neither the printed nor the Darcmon route` | — |
| Optional refusal | `may decline the free play and leaves an ineligible hand card untouched` | `optional: true` |
| Colour/level filter on the free play | both free-play tests keep `BT1-010` (Red Lv3) in hand | `colors: ["Yellow","Purple"], levels: [3]` |
| Main ＜Blocker＞ | `intercepts a player attack with its printed Blocker and wins the battle` | `Static` + `keywords: [Blocker]` |
| Inherited ＜Blocker＞ | `grants inherited Blocker to its evolved host` | `isInherited: true` + `keywords: [Blocker]` |

The Darcmon-route test proves source-stack identity explicitly: the permanent id is
unchanged, the top card is the HippoGryphonmon instance, and `stack` holds exactly the
original Darcmon instance (`Permanent.stack` is the cards beneath the top card). It also
asserts the digivolve bonus draw (deck empties, `BT1-009` reaches hand) and that the freely
played card is a distinct battle-area permanent, with `state.memory` landing on 0 from 3 —
which is what separates the alternate route from the printed cost 4.

The ＜Blocker＞ test drives the real combat path: a player-directed attack, the
`blockWindowOpened` event, then a public `declareBlock` intent from seat 1. Endpoints:
attacker in trash, blocker alive and suspended, security untouched.

### BT17-067 — DexDoruGreymon

Digimon, Lv5, Purple/Black, DP 8000, play cost 8. Undead / X Antibody / SoC.

#### Printed clauses

1. `[Digivolve][DoruGreymon]: Cost 1` — named digivolution route, reduced cost 1.
2. `[Trash] [All Turns]` — when one of your [DoruGreymon] would be deleted, digivolve
   it into this card from the trash without paying the cost, preventing that deletion.
3. `[When Digivolving]` — trash 1 hand card, then ＜Draw 1＞; if [DoruGreymon] is in
   this Digimon's digivolution cards **or** this digivolved from the trash, delete 1
   opponent Digimon with play cost ≤ 6 **instead of the draw** (Q2824).
4. Inherited `[End of Attack] [Once Per Turn]` — you may choose 1 of your Digimon;
   delete that chosen Digimon and 1 opponent Digimon with level ≤ the chosen one's.

#### IR change made (report to coordinator)

Route `digivolutionRequirement` changed from `names: ["DoruGreymon"]` (substring) to
`namesExact: ["DoruGreymon"]`, per the coordinator route decision: printed
`[Digivolve][X]: Cost N` with no "in name" is exact. Under the old substring form a
source named **DexDoruGreymon** (BT9-078) satisfied the [DoruGreymon] route — wrong.
A near-name negative now guards the exact form.

Consequence: `effects.json` still holds `{"names":["DoruGreymon"],cost:1,isAlternate:true}`,
so `BT17-catalog-sync.test.ts` will be red until the coordinator re-runs the set sync.
`effects.json` was **not** edited.

- **Q2821** (Trash effect triggers while in trash when your DoruGreymon would be
  deleted): covered — "naturally replaces an effect deletion from the trash" and
  "allows declining the trash replacement".
- **Q2822** (prevent a DP-to-0 deletion by digivolving, but the DP-reduction carries
  over and re-deletes if DP is still 0): **not behaviourally tested**. Documented
  untestable through the Board Spec / intent surface: it needs a persistent
  DP-reducing effect whose carry-over re-triggers the rule DP check on the digivolved
  DexDoruGreymon. The prevent side of the replacement (`leaveCause: "any"`) is proven;
  the carry-over re-deletion is a rules-layer nuance beyond the fixture surface.
- **Q2823** (cannot activate [When Digivolving] after such a DP-0 digivolution because
  the Digimon is deleted first): **not tested**, same DP-reduction-carryover
  dependency as Q2822.
- **Q2824** (the "delete instead" replaces "Then, ＜Draw 1＞", not the hand trash):
  covered — structural test (Trash unconditional; Draw gated `not(anyOf)`; Delete gated
  `anyOf`) plus two behavioural cases: delete-instead (trash happened, target deleted,
  no draw) and draw branch (trash + draw happened, opponent survives).
- **Q2825** (choosing your own Digimon that can't be deleted by effects still deletes
  the opponent's): covered — the protected host is spared, the opponent Digimon dies.

### BT17-069 — Fenriloogamon

#### Card summary and printed clauses

Digimon, Lv6, Purple/Red, DP 11000, play cost 11. Restricted to 1 copy since
2025-03-28 (only BT17 banlist entry).

Catalog `effectText` / `inheritedEffectText` match the lane sheet exactly. No
`[Security]` clause is present, so none was omitted from the sheet.

Printed clauses:

1. `[Digivolve] Lv.5 w/[SoC] trait: Cost 3` — trait-based alternate route.
2. `[When Digivolving]` — if `[Eiji Nagasumi]` is in the digivolution cards, may
   play 1 `[Fenriloogamon]`/`[Kazuchimon]` from trash without paying the cost;
   at the next end of the opponent's turn, return that Digimon to the hand.
3. `[All Turns][Once Per Turn]` — when one of your Digimon or Tamers with the
   `[SoC]` trait or `[Pulsemon]` in its text is played, delete 1 opposing
   Digimon with 10000 DP or less.
4. Inherited `[Your Turn]` — while this has `[Fenriloogamon]` in its name, the
   turn-end condition becomes the opponent having 3 or more memory.

#### Clause -> test -> IR mapping

- Clause 1 -> `refuses the SoC digivolve route from a non-SoC level 5 source`
  (added) and the SoC-route digivolve inside `plays the matching Fenriloogamon
  from trash...` -> IR `digivolutionRequirement[0]` `{ level: 5, traits:
  ["SoC"], cost: 3, isAlternate: true }`. Route is trait-based, so `traits` is
  correct; the coordinator's `namesExact` rule applies only to name routes and
  does not apply here.
- Clause 2 -> `plays the matching Fenriloogamon from trash and returns it at the
  next opponent turn end` -> IR `WhenDigivolving` actions: `PlayWithoutCost`
  (from `["trash"]`, `bindResultAs: "playedFenriloogamon"`, condition
  `selfDigivolutionStackHasTrait` on `[Eiji Nagasumi]`) then `DelayedEffect`
  (`Return` of `boundRef` to hand, `trigger: nextEndOfOpponentTurn`).
- Clause 3 -> `deletes a 10000 DP opposing Digimon when an SoC Digimon is
  played`, `deletes a 10000 DP opposing Digimon on the Pulsemon-in-text branch
  with no SoC source` (added), `accepts Pulsemon text and enforces the 10000
  DP boundary once per turn`, and `deletes once per turn: refuses a second
  same-turn play, resets on the next own turn` (added) -> IR `AllTurns`
  `SubTrigger` (`whenPlayed`, `sourceFilter` `mine` Digimon/Tamer `nameOrTrait`
  SoC-trait or Pulsemon-text) with `Delete` `opponent` `dp lte 10000`,
  `frequency: OncePerTurn`.
- Clause 4 -> `keeps the active turn through opponent memory 1 or 2, then ends
  at 3` -> IR inherited `YourTurn` `SetTurnEndMemory` `minimum: 3` under
  `selfHasNameContaining ["Fenriloogamon"]`.

Structural shape tests (first three `it`s) assert the IR objects directly; they
carry no behavioural credit on their own.

#### Q&A coverage (Q2830-Q2834)

- Q2830 (played Digimon returned at opponent turn end; only top card returned,
  digivolution cards trashed): behaviourally covered — BT14-081 is played from
  trash and returns to hand at the next opponent turn end. Partial on the
  sub-nuance: the played card has no digivolution cards in the fixture, so the
  "trash the under-cards on return" branch is exercised only by the generic
  return path, not with a stacked returnee.
- Q2831 (turn continues at opponent memory 1-2, ends at 3+): behaviourally
  covered by the inherited-clause test (continues at -1, ends at -4).
- Q2832 (effect applies only on your turn): structurally covered — the clause is
  `trigger: "YourTurn"`, so it never installs on the opponent's turn. Not
  separately behaviourally tested (asserting a non-event).
- Q2833 (an `[End of Your Turn]` effect that lowers opponent memory back to 1-2
  lets your turn continue): NOT covered. This is a turn-end re-evaluation timing
  interaction in the engine, not a clause this card can assert in isolation.
- Q2834 (`<Blitz>` still needs opponent memory 1+ to attack): NOT covered —
  depends on the `<Blitz>` keyword engine, outside this card's effect surface.

    Earth Dragon) with memory 10 (so the failure is the trait check, not cost)
    is refused with `{ ok: false, reason: "invalid-evolution" }`.

- `BT17-069.ts`: unchanged. IR was already correct; no IR change, so
  `BT17-catalog-sync.test.ts` stays green.

### BT17-070 — Gulfmon

Lane 19. Digimon, Lv6, Purple, play cost 12, DP 11000, Mega, Virus, Dark Animal.

#### Printed clauses

| # | Clause |
|---|---|
| C1 | `[Digivolve]Lv.5 w/[Dark Masters] in its text: Cost 3` |
| C2 | `[On Play] [When Digivolving] By placing 1 level 5 card with [Dark Masters] in its text from your hand or trash as this Digimon's bottom digivolution card, delete 1 of your opponent's level 5 or lower Digimon.` |
| C3 | `[When Attacking] By returning 7 cards from your opponent's trash to the bottom of the deck, unsuspend this Digimon.` |

No inherited effect. Catalog `effectText` matches the official list; no discrepancy found.

#### Q&A coverage

| Id | Covered by | Result |
|---|---|---|
| Q2835 | `still pays the placement cost when no opposing level-5 target exists` | green — the placement resolves with no legal delete target (`allowCostWithoutTarget: true`) |
| Q2836 | `returns seven cards including a Digi-Egg to the correct deck and unsuspends after attacking` | green — BT1-001 lands in `eggDeck`, the other six in `deck` |

Q2835's wording says "in its traits" while the printed card and the catalog both say "in its text".
The IR follows the printed text (`match: "text"`); the ruling's point — that the cost is payable
without a delete target — is what the test asserts, and it is unaffected by that wording slip.

#### Clause → test → IR

| Clause | Test | IR |
|---|---|---|
| C1 | `resolves the same placement-and-delete effect after a legal digivolution`; `takes a blue level-5 base only when its text carries [Dark Masters]` | `digivolutionRequirement[0]` = `{ level: 5, texts: ["Dark Masters"], cost: 3, isAlternate: true }` |
| C2 | `uses the place-as-cost effect for both On Play and When Digivolving` (structural); `plays naturally, places a level-5 Dark Masters-text card, and deletes only a level-5 target`; `still pays the placement cost when no opposing level-5 target exists`; `resolves the same placement-and-delete effect after a legal digivolution` | `OnPlay` and `WhenDigivolving` → `Delete` with `cost { kind: "place", destination: "digivolutionStack", position: "bottom", host: "self", from: ["hand","trash"] }` |
| C3 | `returns exactly seven cards from the opponent's trash before unsuspending` (structural); `returns seven cards including a Digi-Egg to the correct deck and unsuspends after attacking`; `stays suspended when the opponent's trash holds fewer than seven cards` | `WhenAttacking` → `Unsuspend` with `cost { kind: "return", count: 7, filter { zone: "trash", controller: "opponent" } }` |

Behavioural endpoints asserted: the placed card present in the stack, the deleted permanent by
`permanentId` with the level-6 neighbour still standing, memory 3 → 0 on the alternate route, hand
size and memory unchanged on the illegal route, the emptied opponent trash split correctly between
`eggDeck` and `deck` (6 cards), and `isSuspended` in both the paid and unpaid cases. No injected
timing: play, digivolve and attack all go through `applyIntent`.

#### Added this run

- `takes a blue level-5 base only when its text carries [Dark Masters]` — a comparative pair on a
  BLUE level-5 base, which the Purple catalog route can never accept, so only the printed alternate
  can carry it. BT15-027 Scorpiomon (text names [Dark Masters]) is accepted at cost 3; BT1-038
  Monzaemon (blue level 5, no printed text at all) is refused with hand and memory untouched. This
  is the missing illegal-source negative and the peer comparison.
- `stays suspended when the opponent's trash holds fewer than seven cards` — six cards in the
  opponent's trash: the cost cannot be paid, nothing is returned, the deck stays empty and Gulfmon
  stays suspended.
- Removed an unused `advance` import flagged by oxlint.

#### Defects

None found. The set-wide `[Name]` substring defect does not apply here: C1 and C2 both print "in
its text", which is correctly a substring match, compiled as `texts: ["Dark Masters"]` and
`nameOrTrait: [{ tokens: ["Dark Masters"], match: "text" }]`.

**IR changed: no.** `BT17-070.ts` is untouched, so no catalog re-sync is needed for this card.

#### Remaining gaps

- "level 5 or lower" is proven at the level-5/level-6 boundary only; no level-4-or-lower target case
  was added.
- The `from: ["hand", "trash"]` disjunction is covered by two separate fixtures (hand for On Play,
  trash for When Digivolving), not by a single fixture offering both zones at once.

#### Card

Purple Lv6 Digimon, DP 12000, play cost 12, trait Demon Lord.

Printed clauses:

   digivolution cards, by deleting 1 of your other Digimon, you may play 1
   [Ornismon] from your trash without paying the cost.

   1 of your opponent's Digimon with level equal to or less than the deleted Digimon.

No inherited effect. Catalog text matches the assignment exactly; no discrepancy.

#### Q&A covered

- **Q2837** (may delete the cost Digimon and then decline to play [Ornismon]).
  Partially covered. The declined branch is tested behaviourally
  ("Q2837: declining ... deletes nothing"): the engine models the clause as one
  opt-in prompt, so declining costs nothing. The other legal line the ruling
  permits — pay the `by deleting 1` cost and *then* decline the play, in order to
  fire clause 3 — is not reachable: the interpreter asks the optional prompt
  before paying the action cost. Seam:
  `apps/api/src/engine/effects/interpreter/actions/play.ts` (PlayWithoutCost
  optional/cost ordering). No red test is retained because "declined" is itself a
  legal outcome; the gap is the missing second choice, not a wrong result.

#### Clause -> test -> IR

| Clause | Test | IR |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 1 | "limits the Darcmon evolution route…", "plays Ornismon from trash after the legal Darcmon/HippoGryphonmon evolution route" (Lv4 base, `alternateRequirementIndex: 0`, memory 4 -> 0), "rejects the Darcmon route when HippoGryphonmon is not in the digivolution cards" | `digivolutionRequirement[0]` |
| 2 | "requires both stack names…" (structure), "plays Ornismon from trash…" (positive), "does not play Ornismon when only Darcmon is in the digivolution cards" (negative, normal route from HippoGryphonmon), "Q2837: declining…" | `effects[0]` `PlayWithoutCost` + `allOf(selfDigivolutionStackHasTrait x2)` + `deleteOwn` cost |
| 3 | "deletes an opposing Digimon no higher than the Digimon deleted by the trigger" (structure), "deletes an opposing Digimon after a natural battle deletion, once per turn and within the level bound" (fires once, level bound respected, same-turn refusal), "re-arms the once-per-turn deletion trigger on the next turn" (real `advance(...).runTurn(0)` boundary) | `effects[1]` `SubTrigger onDeletionOf` + `Delete` with `levelComparison lte lastDeleted`, `frequency: "OncePerTurn"` |

  the printed `[Ornismon]`, `[Darcmon]` and `[HippoGryphonmon]` references

### BT17-072 — Ornismon

#### Card summary

Catalog: Digimon, Lv6, Purple, play cost 13, DP 13000, Mega, Virus, type Ancient Bird.
Digivolution requirements: Purple Lv5 cost 5, Yellow Lv5 cost 5. No inherited effect.

Printed clauses:

1. `[On Play] [When Digivolving] Delete 1 of your opponent's unsuspended Digimon.` — one
   mandatory deletion on two triggers.
2. `[All Turns] While you have another level 6 Digimon, this Digimon gets +2000 DP and
   gains ＜Security Attack +1＞.` — two conditional continuous grants on one condition.

#### Q&A ids covered

`node tools/kb/query.mjs card BT17-072` returns `(no knowledge-base entries)`. The lane
assignment lists no Q&A ids. Nothing to cover.

#### Clause -> test -> IR mapping

| Clause | Test | IR |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Catalog identity + whole IR | `matches the catalog and the complete IR contract` | `compiled.effects` deep equality, `coverage: "full"`, `residual: []`, and `inheritedEffectText` asserted undefined |
| `[On Play]` deletes an unsuspended Digimon, not a suspended one | `deletes only the unsuspended target when played` | `OnPlay` -> `Delete` with `unsuspended: true` |
| `[On Play]` with no legal target | `deletes nothing on play when every opposing Digimon is suspended` | same |
| `[When Digivolving]` deletes | `digivolves from a level 5 Purple/Yellow source for 5 memory and deletes on the way in` (`it.each`) | `WhenDigivolving` -> `Delete` |
| Digivolution requirements | same `it.each` (memory 5 -> 0 on both colours) | catalog `evoCosts` |
| Illegal source | `refuses a level 5 Red source that matches neither digivolution requirement` | — |
| `+2000 DP` and `＜Security Attack +1＞` while another Lv6 is present, and the drop when it leaves | `grants +2000 DP and Security Attack +1 while another own level 6 is present, and drops both when it leaves` | two `Aura` actions sharing `while: { kind: "youHave", filter: { levels: [6], excludeSelf: true } }` |
| No other Lv6 -> no bonus | `does not grant the continuous bonuses without another level-6 Digimon` | `excludeSelf: true` |
| Opposing Lv6 does not count | `does not count an opposing level 6 Digimon` | `controllerDefault: "mine"` |
| ＜Security Attack +1＞ behaviourally | `checks two security cards with the granted Security Attack +1` | keyword aura |

The `it.each` digivolve test proves source-stack identity (same permanent id, Ornismon on
top, exactly the original source instance beneath), the digivolve bonus draw, memory
5 -> 0, and the opponent's deleted Digimon reaching trash. The Security Attack test drives
a real player attack and asserts that two security cards leave (three seeded, one left),
rather than reading the grant only.

The "drops both when it leaves" step removes the other level 6 from the battle area
and calls `s.engine.recomputeContinuousEffects()`, then re-asserts DP 13000 and no
＜Security Attack＞ — proving the aura is conditional, not one-shot.

#### Peer/stack comparative case (session 3)

New test: `counts only the top card's level: a level 6 in another Digimon's digivolution cards
is not another level 6`. One fixture holds Ornismon beside a near-miss peer — a BT2-075 (Lv5)
permanent seeded with `under: ["BT17-071"]`, so a level 6 card is physically present on the
board but only inside the peer's digivolution cards. The aura stays off (13000 DP, no
＜Security Attack＞). The same fixture then gains a real BT17-071 permanent through
`putOnBoard`, and both grants switch on (15000 DP, keyword amount 1). This proves the
`youHave levels: [6]` filter reads top cards only, with the matching and non-matching cases
side by side.

#### Commands and results

```
pnpm --filter @aegis/api exec vitest run src/cards/BT17/BT17-072.test.ts --maxWorkers=1 --no-file-parallelism
### 11 tests passed (session 3 re-run)

pnpm exec oxfmt --check apps/api/src/cards/BT17/BT17-072.test.ts # clean
git diff --check                                                 # clean
```

#### Defects found and fixed locally

The pre-existing suite had no evolution proof, no illegal source, no empty-target case for
`[On Play]`, no opposing-level-6 negative, no proof that the aura drops when the condition
stops holding, and only a boolean `hasKeyword` read for ＜Security Attack +1＞ — which does
not prove the amount is 1 or that it reaches combat. All of that is now covered, and the
partial IR `toMatchObject`/`objectContaining` assertions were replaced with a catalog match
plus full deep equality.

One fixture bug was found while writing the tests: `BT17-071` (Murmukusmon) is level 6, so
it is not a legal digivolution source for this card. The Purple route now uses `BT2-075`
(Myotismon, Lv5 Purple) and the Yellow route `BT1-057` (Sirenmon, Lv5 Yellow).

No module (`BT17-072.ts`) change was needed. The IR was already correct.

#### Retained reds

None.

#### Remaining gaps

- The `[All Turns]` timing word is not proven across a turn boundary; the aura is only
  observed under the turn seat `setupEngine` establishes. The IR carries `trigger:
  "AllTurns"` and the aura has no `duration:` string to expire, so nothing suggests a
  your-turn-only restriction, but the crossing is not asserted.
- ＜Security Attack +1＞ is proven at two checks with one other Lv6 present. Stacking with
  another source of the keyword is not exercised.
- Ornismon has no inherited effect, so there is no inherited-source proof to give.

#### Catalog discrepancies

None. The catalog `effectText` matches the official text in the lane assignment, and
`inheritedEffectText` is correctly absent.

3. `[When Digivolving]` — `＜De-Digivolve3＞` 1 opponent Digimon; then, if `[Dorugoramon]`

   delete all of the opponent's lowest-level Digimon.
4. `[All Turns] [Once Per Turn]` — when another Digimon is deleted, you may unsuspend this
   Digimon.

Inherited: none. No `[Security]` clause. Catalog `effectText` carries no U+00A0.

#### Q&A covered

- **Q2838** — the `[Trash]` effect triggers while this card sits in the trash and your
  Dorugoramon would be deleted. Proven by `digivolves from trash to prevent a natural
  Dorugoramon deletion`: BT17-073 in trash, BT16-064 Dorugoramon under attack, replacement
  digivolves it out of the trash and cancels the deletion.
- **Q2839** — prevention of a DP-becoming-0 deletion. The prevention path is proven (same
  test); the extra nuance (the DP reduction carries over and re-deletes if DexDorugoramon is
  still at 0 DP after digivolving) is **not** separately tested — see Remaining gaps.

#### Clause -> test -> IR

| Clause | IR | Behavioural test |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Exact-name route | `digivolutionRequirement:[{namesExact:["Dorugoramon"],cost:2,isAlternate}]` | `digivolves from a Dorugoramon base for 2 ...` (memory 2->0, BT16-064 beneath, bonus draw); negative `refuses a near-name DexDorugoramon base ...` |
| `[Trash]` replacement | `Replacement wouldBeDeleted, digivolveFromTrash, target nameExact Dorugoramon` | `digivolves from trash to prevent ...` |
| `[When Digivolving]` de-digivolve | `DeDigivolve amount 3 stopAtLevel 3` | `digivolves from a Dorugoramon base ...` (deTarget L6->L5) and the unmet-condition test |
| conditional delete | `Delete superlative lowestLevel count all, condition anyOf(selfHasInDigivolutionCards nameExact Dorugoramon, digivolvedFromZone trash)` | met branch deletes L3 (Dorugoramon route); unmet branch keeps it (BT10-081 Lv5 route) |
| OPT unsuspend | `AllTurns OncePerTurn SubTrigger onDeletionOf excludeSelf -> Unsuspend isSelf` | `unsuspends after an opponent's Digimon is deleted ...`; same-turn refusal in `does not unsuspend a second time ...` |

The met/unmet delete pair uses an identical opponent board (AD1-004 over AD1-002 plus a
BT1-009 L3), differing only in the digivolution source, which isolates the condition as the
cause of the deletion — the comparative case for peer/stack credit.

#### Defects fixed locally (IR changed: YES)

Three name references were substring (`match:"name"` / `names`) but the printed text brackets
each name with no "in name", so all are exact:

1. `digivolutionRequirement` `names` -> `namesExact` (coordinator rule: `[Digivolve][X]: Cost N`
   with no "in name" is exact). Under substring `names`, a base named "DexDorugoramon" (contains
   "Dorugoramon") would wrongly pass the cost-2 route; the near-name negative test is the
   fails-when-reverted guard.
2. Replacement target `nameOrTrait match:"name"` -> `"nameExact"` — "one of your `[Dorugoramon]`"
   must not also protect DexDorugoramon.
3. `selfHasInDigivolutionCards nameOrTrait match:"name"` -> `"nameExact"` — "`[Dorugoramon]` is in
   this Digimon's digivolution cards".

These are IR changes, so **`BT17-catalog-sync.test.ts` will be red until the coordinator runs the
set sync**. I did not touch `effects.json`.

#### Remaining gaps

- **OPT next-own-turn reset** not behaviourally re-proven. Same-turn refusal is proven through
  two real battles; the reset is the engine-generic per-turn tracker (`tracker.count(key,
  "subtrigger")`) and the structural `frequency:"OncePerTurn"`. Crossing a real turn boundary to
  re-fire was skipped to respect the one-test-run discipline.
- **Q2839 DP-carryover re-deletion** untested. The trash replacement is exercised against a
  battle deletion, not a DP-to-0 deletion; modelling the carried-over DP reduction re-checking
  after the digivolve was out of scope for this pass.

No engine seams were hit; no `it.fails` retained.

2. `[When Digivolving]` If it's your turn, you may play 1 white Tamer card with a
   play cost of 4 or less **or** 1 level 5 or lower [Eosmon] from your hand for a
   cost of 2. If you do, your opponent may play 1 Tamer card from their hand
   without paying the cost.
3. Inherited: `[Opponent's Turn] [Once Per Turn]` When an opponent's Digimon
   attacks, you may switch the attack target to 1 of your [Eosmon].

Catalog text matches the assignment exactly; no discrepancy.

- **Q2841** (the inherited effect may switch the target to an *unsuspended*
  [Eosmon]): covered by "redirects an opponent attack only to an unsuspended
  Eosmon" and "redirects only one opponent attack per turn".

#### Clause -> test -> IR

| Clause | Test | IR |

| 1 | both play tests digivolve onto `BT17-044` Morphomon; "rejects a digivolve from a base that is not [Morphomon]" (Lv3 `BT1-009` refused) | `digivolutionRequirement[0]` (`names: ["Morphomon"], cost: 2, isAlternate`) |
| 2 first branch | "plays a white cost-4-or-less Tamer for 2 memory on your turn" (memory 5 -> 1, opponent's Tamer played) | `effects[0].actions[0]` `PlayWithoutCost` + `payMemory 2` + `isYourTurn` |
| 2 second branch | "plays a level-5-or-lower Eosmon from hand for 2 memory" (BT17-075 played, memory 4 -> 0) | `orFilters[0]` (`levelComparison lte 5`, `nameExact` Eosmon) |
| 2 "If you do" | "gives the opponent no Tamer play when the controller declines the play" (declined: both Tamers stay in hand, opponent board empty, memory 5 -> 3) | `effects[0].actions[1]` + `condition: ifThisEffectActed` |
| 3 | "redirects an opponent attack only to an unsuspended Eosmon"; "redirects only one opponent attack per turn" (first attack redirected onto the Eosmon, second resolves to security) | `effects[1]` `SubTrigger whenOpponentAttacks` -> `RedirectAttack`, `isInherited`, `frequency: "OncePerTurn"` |

#### Defects fixed

### BT17-076 — Eosmon

Digimon, Lv6, White, play cost 12, 12000 DP, Unknown trait. SR.
Module: `apps/api/src/cards/BT17/BT17-076.ts`.
Test: `apps/api/src/cards/BT17/BT17-076.test.ts`.

#### Printed clauses

Catalog `effectText` (verified against `packages/shared/src/cards/data/cards.json`):

1. `[Digivolve]Lv.5 [Eosmon]: Cost 4` — digivolve route, no "in name".
2. `[When Digivolving] [When Attacking] [Once Per Turn]` — may play 1 level 5 or
   lower `[Eosmon]` from hand without paying. Two timings share one once-per-turn key.
3. `[All Turns] [Once Per Turn]` — when one of your `[Eosmon]` is played, delete 1
   opponent Digimon with DP ≤ the played Eosmon's DP.
4. `[Your Turn] For each Tamer, all of your [Eosmon] get +1000 DP.`

Inherited: none.

#### Defects found and fixed (both are IR changes)

##### 1. `[Your Turn]` DP aura wrongly gated on controlling a Tamer yourself

The aura carried a `while` gate `{ kind: "youHave", filter: { controllerDefault:
"mine", kind: ["Tamer"] } }`. `runStaticAction` (`apps/api/src/engine/effects/
interpreter/actions/statics.ts:24`) treats `while` as a hard gate: if false the whole
aura returns without applying. But the aura's own `scaling.filter` is `controller:
"any"` (counts both players' Tamers), matching the printed bare "For each Tamer" and
the predecessor BT6-086 "For each Tamer in play". So the module was self-contradictory:
it scaled by every Tamer in play yet suppressed the entire bonus whenever you did not
control a Tamer. On your turn with an opponent-only Tamer, your Eosmon received +0
instead of +1000.

The prior audit (`internal-docs/audits/BT17/BT17-071-080.md`) corrected the *scaling*
filter to `any` but left this `while` gate at `controllerDefault: "mine"` — an
overlooked residual defect.

Fix: removed the `while` gate. The printed text carries no "if you have a Tamer"
condition; with 0 Tamers the scaling already yields +0, so no gate is needed. The
battle-area guard stays implicit (no source permanent → no candidates).

Proof: `boosts your Eosmon by an opponent-only Tamer on your turn` — seat 0's turn,
seat 0 has only the Eosmon, seat 1 has one Tamer (BT17-088). `currentDP` is 13000.
Pre-fix this asserted 12000.

##### 2. Digivolve route must be `namesExact`, not `names`

Printed `[Digivolve]Lv.5 [Eosmon]: Cost 4` has no "in name", so per the coordinator
route-name decision the requirement must use `namesExact` (exact) rather than `names`
(substring). Changed `digivolutionRequirement[0].names: ["Eosmon"]` →
`namesExact: ["Eosmon"]`. `cardData.ts:487` reads `namesExact` as an equality gate.

Note: the brief also asks for a near-name substring negative (a card whose name
contains "Eosmon" but is not exactly "Eosmon", refused). No such card exists — all
seven Eosmon-named cards (BT6-083/085/086, BT7-084, BT17-074/075/076) are named
exactly "Eosmon". So `namesExact` vs `names` is not behaviorally observable in the
current pool; the change is correctness-only. The name gate is still guarded by a
Lv5 non-Eosmon negative (BT1-024).

#### Q&A coverage (Q2844, Q2845, Q2846)

- **Q2844** (this card's own play triggers its `[All Turns]` deletion): covered by
  `triggers its own [All Turns] deletion when this Eosmon is played (Q2844)`. Hard-play
  BT17-076 (itself an Eosmon) → its `whenPlayed` subtrigger fires on itself and deletes
  an opponent Digimon (BT17-063 dp 6000 ≤ 12000). No engine seam here; the subtrigger
  catches the play that placed the source.
- **Q2845** (played Eosmon's DP changes before this effect processes → threshold uses
  the processing-time DP) and **Q2846** (played Eosmon removed before this effect
  processes → no deletion): documented untestable in the current harness. Both require
  a *second* simultaneous trigger ordered ahead of this deletion (one that raises, or
  removes, the played Eosmon) in a minimal fixture; no such co-triggering card is
  available to seed cleanly. The IR mechanism is consistent with both rulings: the
  deletion binds the played Eosmon via `SelectBind` (`bindAs: "playedEosmon"`) and the
  `Delete` filter reads `relativeTo: { attr: "dp", op: "lte", selectionRef:
  "playedEosmon" }` live at resolution — so a DP change resolves against the live value
  (Q2845) and a removed reference yields no comparable DP and no legal target (Q2846).

#### Clause → test → IR mapping

| Clause | Test | IR |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Digivolve Lv5 [Eosmon] cost 4 | `digivolves from a Lv5 [Eosmon]...`; `refuses a wrong-level Eosmon and a right-level non-Eosmon source` | `digivolutionRequirement[0]` (`namesExact`, level 5, cost 4, alternate) |
| Free-play on digivolve/attack, shared OPT | `plays Eosmon from hand on a natural digivolution...`; `plays Eosmon from hand when attacking and shares its once-per-turn limit` (existing) | `effects[0]`/`effects[1]` `PlayWithoutCost`, `sharedUseKey: "ir-shared-0"` |
| Delete opponent Digimon ≤ played Eosmon DP | `deletes by the played Eosmon's DP...` (existing); `triggers its own [All Turns] deletion...` | `effects[2]` `SubTrigger whenPlayed` → `SelectBind` + `Delete relativeTo` |
| Delete is once per turn | `deletes only one opponent Digimon per turn across two Eosmon plays` | `effects[2].frequency: "OncePerTurn"` |
| +1000 DP per Tamer (any controller) | `deletes ... and counts both players' Tamers for DP` (existing, own+opp Tamer → 14000); `boosts your Eosmon by an opponent-only Tamer on your turn` | `effects[3]` `Aura modifyDP` `scaling.filter.controller: "any"`, no `while` |

### BT17-077 — Imperialdramon: Paladin Mode

Digimon, Lv7, White/Blue/Green, DP 16000, play cost 9. ACE (overflow 5).

#### Printed clauses

1. `[Digivolve]Lv.6 w/[Imperialdramon] in its name: Cost 5` — alternate reduced route.
   "in its name" is substring, so `digivolutionRequirement` uses `names` (not
   `namesExact`). Correct per the coordinator route-name rule; no IR change.
2. `[Hand] [Counter] ＜Blast Digivolve＞` — keyword-only effect, no actions.
3. `[On Play] [When Digivolving]` — trash all opponent digivolution cards; then
   return all cards from your OR your opponent's trash to the deck bottom (modal
   choice); if the return moved a white level 7 card, gain 3 memory.
4. `[When Attacking]` — optional: return 1 opponent Digimon with no digivolution
   cards to the deck bottom to unsuspend this Digimon.
5. `[Rule] Trait: Has the [Free] attribute` — reflected in catalog `types`
   (`["Ancient Holy Warrior", "Free"]`); static, asserted in the catalog test.

#### Q&A coverage

- Q4711 (who returns trash): modal `chooseOption`. Behavioral both branches —
  own trash (index 0) and opponent trash (`preferOptionIndex: 1`).
- Q2848 (multicolor white L7 counts): BT12-112 (White/Red, L7) returned → +3.
  Engine `definitionMatches` uses `colors.some`, so a card that merely contains
  White matches `colors: ["White"]`. Behaviorally verified.
- Q2849 (two white L7 return still only +3): two white L7 in trash → memory 7
  (9 − 5 cost + 3), not 10. `GainMemory` is a flat +3, fires once. Verified.
- Q2847 (Digi-Egg in trash → bottom of the Digi-Egg deck): NOT tested.
  Reason: no engine support and a fixture prohibition. The interpreter has no
  egg-deck concept at all — `rg -n "eggDeck|digiEgg|isEgg"` over
  `apps/api/src/engine` returns nothing — so `Return { to: "deckBottom" }`
  (`actions/removal.ts`) routes every card to the main deck. Testing it also
  requires a Digi-Egg in trash, which the fixture rules forbid. Documented as an
  engine seam rather than blessed with a wrong result.

2. `＜Raid＞` — engine keyword (§16-23).
3. `＜Blocker＞` — engine keyword.
4. `[On Play] [When Digivolving] If DNA Digivolving, choose 1 opponent's Digimon and return the
   chosen Digimon and all of your opponent's Digimon with the same level to the bottom of the
   deck. Then, delete 1 of your opponent's Digimon.`

No inherited effect.

#### Q&A coverage (Q2369, Q2851, Q6006)

- **Q2851** (delete resolves even when the "if DNA Digivolving" branch does not): covered by
  `deletes one opposing Digimon after a natural non-DNA play and returns nothing`. Hard-played
  Omnimon deletes exactly one opponent Digimon; nothing is returned; deck stays empty.
- **Q6006** (Omnimon removed mid-return; the "Then delete" still resolves): **documented
  untestable in-lane.** Reproducing it needs an opponent card carrying a "when [your Digimon]
  would leave" immediate effect that removes Omnimon during the first (return) step; that is a
  fixture involving a second unaudited card and the trigger-ordering seam, out of scope for a
  single-card lane. The IR already models the guarantee: the `Delete` action (effect[3]/[4]
  actions[2]) is a sibling of the `Return`, unconditional, so it runs regardless of the source's
  fate — asserted structurally by `keeps the post-return deletion independent of the DNA
  condition`.
- **Q2369** (＜Overflow＞ processed when a card carrying Omnimon ACE in its digivolution cards is
  trashed by this effect): **documented untestable in-lane.** ＜Overflow＞ is an engine ACE
  keyword driven by `overflowMemory`, not by this card's compiled IR, and the scenario places
  Omnimon under a third card. It belongs to an Overflow/ACE engine test, not this module.

#### Clause -> test -> IR

| Clause | Test | IR |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Blast DNA keyword shape | `keeps ... separate keyword clauses` | effect[0] Counter/isFromHand/BlastDNADigivolve |
| Raid / Blocker shape | same | effect[1]/[2] Static keyword |
| DNA return + delete shape | `during DNA Digivolve returns ...` / `keeps the post-return deletion ...` | effect[3]/[4] SelectBind+Return(isDnaDigivolving) + unconditional Delete |
| Blast DNA route (behaviour) | `Blast DNA Digivolves from a Counter window ...` | effect[0] + effect[3] resolution |
| On Play/When Digivolving return+delete (behaviour) | same | effect[3] SelectBind/Return/Delete |
| Q2851 delete-without-DNA | `deletes one opposing Digimon after a natural non-DNA play ...` | effect[3] Delete only |
| Recipe gate | `refuses DNA materials that miss the printed recipe` | shared DNA requirement |
| ＜Blocker＞ block (behaviour) + ＜Raid＞ conferral | `blocks an attack with ＜Blocker＞ and survives ...` | effect[1]/[2] |

The Blast DNA behavioural test asserts full endpoints: Omnimon on the battle area with
`stack` carrying both material instance ids, hand equal to the single drawn card, memory
unchanged at 3 (cost 0), both same-level SaberLeomon at the deck bottom, exactly one opponent
Digimon in trash, no BT1-043 left in the battle area.

#### Defect fixed in the shared package (lane S)

`rejects a near-name (BlackWarGreymon) that only substring-matches the recipe` — now **green**
(the `it.fails` was flipped to `it`).

- File/function: `packages/shared/src/effects/data.ts`,
  `DNA_DIGIVOLUTION_REQUIREMENT_OVERRIDES["BT17-078"]`, consumed by `matchingDnaDigivolveCost`
  (`apps/api/src/engine/effects/primitives.ts` -> `dnaMaterialSpecMatches`).
- Was: the override stored the printed brackets as substring `names`, so BlackWarGreymon
  (BT2-112) and the X-Antibody variants were wrongly admitted as Blast DNA materials at cost 0.
- Now: `materials: [{ namesExact: ["WarGreymon"] }, { namesExact: ["MetalGarurumon"] }]`,
  mirroring the BT13-059 precedent in the same file. BlackWarGreymon + MetalGarurumon is
  refused with `invalid-evolution`.
- Red then green: see `docs/audits/BT17-reaudit/SHARED-NAME-ALIAS-MECHANISM.md`.

#### Secondary observation (not a retained red)

BT17-078 prints no Main-phase `[DNA Digivolve]` header; its DNA is `[Hand][Counter]` Blast only
(§16-31). Because the shared override registers a plain `dnaDigivolveRequirement`, the
Main-phase `dnaDigivolve` verb also accepts the WarGreymon+MetalGarurumon pair outside a Counter
window. This may be an over-admission of route/timing. I did not encode it as a red assertion —
I have no ruling that explicitly forbids the Main-phase declaration, and the behavioural route
coverage here uses the unambiguous Counter path instead. Flagging for the coordinator.

#### Remaining gaps

- ＜Raid＞ combat redirect (§16-23) is proven only as keyword conferral here; its behaviour is
  covered by `apps/api/src/engine/combat/advancedKeywords.test.ts`.
- Q6006 and Q2369 documented untestable in-lane (above).

#### Catalog discrepancies

- Catalog text matches the official list. The shared DNA override's substring names were the
  only data issue; lane S fixed it in `packages/shared`, not the catalog.

### BT17-079 — Takuya Kanbara

#### Card

Tamer, Lv-, Red, play cost 3. Catalog `packages/shared/src/cards/data/cards.json`.

Printed clauses:

1. `[Security] Play this card without paying the cost.`
2. `[Start of Your Main Phase] If your opponent has a Digimon, gain 1 memory.`
3. Inherited `[Your Turn] This Digimon gets +2000 DP.`
4. Inherited `While this Digimon has 10000 DP or more, it gains ＜Piercing＞.`

Catalog note: the `[Security]` clause lives inside `effectText` for this card, while the
sibling BT17-080 carries it in a separate `securityEffectText` field. Reported, not edited.

#### Q&A covered

- **Q2852** — <Piercing> keeps its second security check even if the checked card's
  `[Security]` effect drops the Digimon below 10000 DP after <Piercing> activated.
  Covered structurally only: the IR grants <Piercing> through an `Aura` whose `while`
  gate is `selfDpAtLeast: 10000`, which is the correct continuous spelling; the ruling is
  about the engine's <Piercing> activation window (a keyword already activated is not
  revoked mid-resolution), not about this card's IR. No card-level endpoint exists here,
  so no behavioural test was added for the ruling itself. The threshold either side of
  10000 is asserted behaviourally (see below).

#### Clause → test → IR

| Clause | Test | IR |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 1 | "naturally plays from Security and gains memory at main-phase start" (real attack, real security check, tamer lands in seat 1's battle area, no `payCost` memory event, card left security) | `effects[0]` `Security` / `PlayWithoutCost` `payCost:false` |
| 2 positive | same test, `advance().runTurn(0)` raises `memoryChanged` `gainMemory` 0→1 | `effects[1]` `StartOfYourMainPhase` / `GainMemory` `condition.opponentHas` |
| 2 negative (Tamer only) | "gains no memory when the opponent has no Digimon in the battle area" | same |
| 2 negative (breeding) | "does not count an opponent Digimon that sits in the breeding area" | `zone: "battleArea"` on the condition filter |
| 3 | "naturally grants the inherited host DP and Piercing only during its turn" — BT16-025 host 8000 → 10000 on your turn; BT17-063 host stays 5000 on the opponent's turn | `effects[2]` `YourTurn` `isInherited` / `ModifyDP` +2000 |
| 4 | same test (Piercing at 10000) plus "withholds Piercing from a boosted host that still sits below 10000 DP" (BT17-063 5000 + 2000 = 7000, no Piercing on your own turn) | `Aura` keyword Piercing, `while.selfDpAtLeast 10000` |

#### Defect fixed

`effects[1].actions[0].condition.filter` had no `zone`. An `opponentHas` counting filter
also counts the breeding area, so an opponent whose only Digimon was in breeding gave
memory. Comprehensive Rules §3-4-5-8 forbids referencing breeding-area card information.
Added `zone: "battleArea"`; the new breeding test is the witness.

**IR changed: yes.** `BT17-catalog-sync.test.ts` will be red until the coordinator runs
the set sync. `packages/shared/src/effects/effects.json` was not touched.

#### Peer/stack and behaviour additions (session 3)

- `boosts only the host it sits under, not a peer stack carrying an inert digivolution card`:
  one fixture with three BT16-025 permanents — one `under: ["BT17-079"]` (10000 DP, ＜Piercing＞),
  one `under: ["BT1-009"]` (inert digivolution card, 8000 DP, no ＜Piercing＞) and one bare
  (8000 DP, no ＜Piercing＞). The comparative peer proves the inherited grant is scoped to its
  own stack.
- `checks security with the inherited Piercing after winning a battle`: the granted ＜Piercing＞
  is now proven through a real attack — the 10000 DP host deletes a suspended BT1-009 and the
  excess damage checks exactly one security card. Q2852's own activation-window question stays
  engine-owned (no card-level endpoint), but ＜Piercing＞ is no longer proven by `observe` alone.

#### Clause → test → IR

| Clause | Test | IR |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 1 positive | "naturally plays from Security and gains memory at main-phase start" (BT17-008 Guilmon on board, real turn loop, `gainMemory` 0→1) | `effects[0]` `StartOfYourMainPhase` / `GainMemory` `condition.youHave` |
| 1 negative (breeding) | "does not count a Guilmon that sits in the breeding area" — real turn loop with the interactive Breeding window skipped by the turn player's own `endPhase` | `zone: "battleArea"` on the condition filter |

| 2 accept | "naturally places the required Trash cards and evolves Guilmon into Gallantmon" — Guilmon becomes BT17-016, stack holds BT17-008/080/010/013, Gallantmon left hand, Takato left the battle area | `effects[1]` `Digivolve` + `cost` + two `additionalCosts` |

| 2 self binding | "places only the copy of [Takato Matsuki] that owns the trigger, not a second copy" — two BT17-080 on the board, exactly one lands in the stack, exactly one remains | `cost.target.filter.isSelfRef: true` |
| 3 | "naturally plays from Security and gains memory at main-phase start" (real attack, real security check) | `effects[2]` `Security` `isSecurity` / `PlayWithoutCost` `payCost:false` |

#### Defects fixed

1. **Set-wide `[Name]` substring defect.** Five printed exact bracket references compiled
   as `match: "name"` (substring): the `[Guilmon]` host (both `target.filter` and
   `cost.underFilter`), `[Gallantmon]` in `into`, `[Growlmon]` and `[WarGrowlmon]` in the
   additional costs, and `[Takato Matsuki]` in the cost target. All moved to `nameExact`.
   This was live, not cosmetic: `Growlmon` as a substring matches `WarGrowlmon`,
   `BlackGrowlmon` and `Growlmon (X Antibody)`, so the printed 2-card trash cost could be
   paid with a single WarGrowlmon plus a BlackGrowlmon; `Guilmon` as a substring accepted
   `Guilmon (X Antibody)` as a host. The `[Guilmon]/[Growlmon]/[Gallantmon] in its name`
   condition on clause 1 is correctly left as substring.
2. **"this Tamer" was any Takato.** The cost target matched by name only, so a second copy
   of BT17-080 on the board could be placed instead of the trigger's own source. Added
   `isSelfRef: true`.
3. **`youHave` counted the breeding area.** Added `zone: "battleArea"` (Comprehensive Rules
   §3-4-5-8).

**IR changed: yes.** `BT17-catalog-sync.test.ts` will be red until the coordinator runs
the set sync. `packages/shared/src/effects/effects.json` was not touched.

#### Retained red (engine seam)

`it.fails("Q2853: places the cost cards even when the digivolve itself is declined")`.

- Ruling: placing this Tamer, 1 `[Growlmon]` and 1 `[WarGrowlmon]` and then declining the
  digivolve is legal, because the "may" sits on the digivolve, not on the placement.
- IR/engine: the clause is one optional `Digivolve` action carrying the placement as its
  `cost`. `apps/api/src/engine/effects/interpreter/actions/runAction.ts`
  (`runActionInner`, the optional prompt ahead of `payableActionCost`) asks a single
  yes/no covering cost **and** effect, so a decline skips both. There is no IR spelling
  for "pay, then optionally do" — `DigivolveViaPlacement`
  (`packages/shared/src/effects/ir/actions/digivolve.ts:106`) is a different shape (the
  effect source itself digivolves onto the host) and does not fit.
- Expected: BT17-080, BT17-010 and BT17-013 sit in the `[Guilmon]`'s stack, the Digimon is
  still BT17-008, and BT17-016 is still in hand. Actual: nothing moves.
- Not an engine edit (out of lane scope). The existing green test
  "may decline the natural end-of-turn evolution without moving its required cards" pins
  the current behaviour so the seam's closure is visible.

#### Engine lane E2 resolution (session 3) — retained red CLOSED

`BT17-080.ts` sets `payCostBeforeOptional: true` on the `Digivolve`. `runActionInner` skips
the leading optional prompt for that flag and raises the payload prompt AFTER the
additional-cost loop, so the whole printed "By placing this Tamer and 1 [Growlmon] and 1
[WarGrowlmon]" cost is paid before the digivolve is offered (Q2853). `it.fails` flipped
to `it`.

red `Tests 10 passed | 1 expected fail (11)` -> green `Tests 10 passed (10)`.

required cards" was removed: same fixture and decision path as the Q2853 test, asserting the
opposite endpoint. A comment in the file names the ruling.

#### Q&A coverage (KB, all 2024-03-28)

  and target filter `suspended:false`.

  mid-turn): COVERED — new test drives a main-phase player-attack (Omnimon
  suspends, opponent security 2->1), `verb.unsuspend` (sanctioned plumbing for
  the Blitz/When-Digivolving self-unsuspend), then the EoT effect fires and
  attacks again (security 1->0).
- Q2856 (played Digimon self-deleted before activation; effect activates but

  subtrigger declines rather than "activating for zero gain". Game-state outcome
  matches the ruling (no memory gained); the only divergence is paying the
  optional suspend cost for nothing, which a player would never choose. Not an
  engine defect; documented as a benign modeling difference.
- Q2859 (two copies, two Omnimon: only one attack resolves): NOT TESTED — needs
  two Tamer copies plus the engine's "no new attack declaration during an
  attack" semantics; out of scope for a single-card fixture and not exercisable
  through the public intents here. Documented.

  assert the `[Your Turn]` clause fires off it — Minami suspends and a blue
  Digimon gains ＜Rush＞. The negative counterpart ("does not trigger when a
  matching Digimon is played from hand") pins the "from digivolution cards"
  restriction the ruling relies on.

| --- | --- | --- |
| 1 On Play, optional, hand or digivolution cards, no cost | "naturally plays Labramon from hand without paying its cost"; "naturally plays Labramon from a stack…"; "honors declining the optional On Play stack recovery" | `effects[0]` `PlayWithoutCost`, `from: ["hand","digivolutionCards"]`, `payCost: false`, `optional: true`, `nameOrTrait` tokens `Labramon`/`Seasarmon` with `match: "name"` |
| 2 Your Turn watcher, suspend cost, 1 blue Digimon, ＜Rush＞ for the turn | "naturally plays Labramon from a stack, suspends only this Minami, and grants blue Digimon Rush"; "grants Rush to a blue Digimon and never to a non-blue peer in the same fixture"; "does not trigger when a matching Digimon is played from hand" | `effects[1]` `SubTrigger whenPlayed` with `sourceFilter.fromDigivolution: true`, `GainKeyword Rush`, `duration: "forTheTurn"`, `cost.kind: "suspend"` on `isSelfRef`, `optional`/`abortOnDecline` |
| 3 Security play without cost | "naturally plays itself from security without paying its cost" | `effects[2]` `trigger: "Security"`, `isSecurity: true`, `PlayWithoutCost` on self |

`[Name]` handling is exact (`match: "name"` over `["Labramon","Seasarmon"]`), which
matches the printed bracketed names. No digivolution route is printed on this card,
so the `names`/`namesExact` route decision does not apply.

### BT17-083 — Koji Minamoto

#### Card summary

Blue Tamer, Lv-, play cost 4.

Printed clauses:

1. `[Security]` Play this card without paying the cost.
2. `[Start of Your Turn]` If you have 2 memory or less, set your memory to 3.
3. Inherited: `[Your Turn] [Once Per Turn]` When an effect adds cards to your hand,
   gain 1 memory. Then, this Digimon gains ＜Jamming＞ for the turn.

#### Q&A covered

- **Q2861** — the inherited effect activates even when a single effect trashes the
  same number of cards it added (net-zero hand change). Covered by "fires for a
  Tamer effect whose net hand size is unchanged, once per turn only (Q2861)",
  which plays BT7-091 Koichi Kimura (`[On Play] ＜Draw 1＞. Then, trash 1 card in
  your hand.`) and asserts memory +1 and ＜Jamming＞ on the host.
- **Q2862** — the effect does **not** activate on the digivolution-bonus hand
  increase. Covered by the existing "does not trigger from the digivolution bonus
  hand addition" test: a real `digivolve` intent draws the bonus card, memory shows
  no watcher gain and the host has no ＜Jamming＞.

#### Defect found and fixed: over-narrowed inherited watcher

`BT17-083.ts` carried

```ts
fireCondition: {
  kind: "triggerByYourDigimonEffect",
  raw: "one of your Digimon's effects adds cards to your hand",
},
```

The `raw` string is not this card's printed text. It is BT15-083's
("When **one of your Digimon's effects** adds cards to your hand"), and the gate
was copied with it. BT17-083 prints "When **an effect** adds cards to your hand",
the same wording as BT9-002, which compiles the same `whenEffectAddsToHand`
SubTrigger with **no** `fireCondition`. The engine agrees: the
`effectAddsToHandGate` comment in
`apps/api/src/engine/effects/interpreter/actions/subTrigger.ts:551` names
BT9-002 / BT15-083 / BT17-083 as the "an effect adds cards to YOUR hand" family and
notes the event already scopes the add to the watcher's own hand
(`effectAddedToHandSeat === source.ownerSeat`), so no extra gate is needed.

Effect of the bug: a Tamer's or Option's effect adding cards to your hand — the
Q2861 case among them, since the ruling says "an effect such as ＜Draw 1＞. Then,
trash 1" without restricting it to Digimon — silently failed to fire.

Fix: the `fireCondition` is removed and replaced with a comment naming the printed
wording and the BT15-002/BT15-083 contrast. **This changes the compiled IR**, so
`BT17-catalog-sync.test.ts` will be red until the coordinator runs the set sync.
`effects.json` was not touched.

Regression proof: the new test's first leg would not fire before the change
(verified — the watcher never granted ＜Jamming＞ with the gate in place).

#### Clause → test → IR

| Clause | Test | IR |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 1 Security play without cost | "naturally plays itself from security without paying its cost" | `effects[0]` `trigger: "Security"`, `isSecurity: true`, `PlayWithoutCost` on self |
| 2 Start of Your Turn, memory ≤ 2 → set 3 | "sets memory to 3 at the natural start of your turn only from 2 or less" (both branches, through `advance().runTurn(0)`) | `effects[1]` `SetMemory value 3` with `condition memoryAtMost 2` |
| 3a inherited watcher fires on any effect hand-add | "naturally gains memory and grants its host Jamming when a Digimon effect draws"; "fires for a Tamer effect whose net hand size is unchanged, once per turn only (Q2861)"; "does not narrow the printed \"an effect\" wording to your Digimon's effects" | `effects[2]` `SubTrigger whenEffectAddsToHand`, no `fireCondition` |
| 3b gain 1 memory, then ＜Jamming＞ for the turn | same tests, asserting exact memory values and `hasKeyword(host, "Jamming")` | `GainMemory 1` + `GainKeyword Jamming duration "forTheTurn"` on `isSelfRef` |
| 3c `[Once Per Turn]` | "fires for a Tamer effect … once per turn only (Q2861)": second same-turn add grants no memory (7 → 4 on a 3-cost play), then the limit resets and the third play on the next own turn gains again | `effects[2].frequency: "OncePerTurn"` |
| 3d not the digivolution bonus | "does not trigger from the digivolution bonus hand addition" | Q2862; the bonus draw never routes through the `whenEffectAddsToHand` fire sites |

The once-per-turn reset runs through the real turn loop: opponent's turn via
`advance().runTurn(1)`, then this seat's own turn via `engine.runOneTurn()` +
`waitForMainPhase(0)` + `endMainPhaseIfOpen(0)`. No injected timing is used for
any behavioural claim. `observeStartTurn` wraps `fireTiming` but calls through to
the production implementation and only records memory — it observes, it does not
fire.

```
  -> 1 file passed, 8 tests passed
pnpm --filter @aegis/api exec tsc --noEmit -p tsconfig.json   -> clean
pnpm exec oxlint <files>; pnpm exec oxfmt --check <files>; git diff --check  -> clean
```

- The test file imports `../BT7/BT7-091.js` to register the Tamer draw-then-trash
  effect used by the Q2861 case; the BT17 index alone does not register it.
- Not covered: an **opponent's** effect adding cards to your hand. The
  `whenEffectAddsToHand` seat gate is asserted in the engine's own suite, and no
  BT17 fixture card puts cards into the opponent's hand cheaply; the clause's
  breadth is proven with a non-Digimon (Tamer) effect instead.
- No retained red on this card, no engine seam raised.

### BT17-084 — Davis Motomiya & Ken Ichijoji

Tamer, Lv-, Blue/Green, play cost 5. Re-audited independently; the 2026-09-02
10/10 claim was not treated as evidence.

#### Printed clauses (catalog `cards.json`)

`effectText` carries three clauses; `securityEffectText` carries a fourth that
the lane sheet omitted. All four are compiled and behaviourally proven.

1. `[Start of Your Turn]` If you have 2 memory or less, set your memory to 3.
2. `[All Turns]` When one of your level 5+ `[Free]` Digimon would be deleted in
   battle, by suspending this Tamer, you may play 1 level 4 or lower Digimon
   card from that Digimon's digivolution cards without paying the cost.
3. `[End of Your Turn]` 1 of your `[Free]` Digimon may attack an opponent's
   Digimon.
4. `[Security]` Play this card without paying the cost.

#### Clause -> test -> IR mapping

| Clause | IR | Test |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Start (set memory 3) | `StartOfYourTurn` -> `SetMemory{value:3, condition memoryAtMost 2}` | "sets memory to 3 ... only from 2 or less" (2->3 fires; 4 does not) |
| All Turns replacement | `AllTurns` -> `Replacement{event wouldBeDeleted, leaveCause byBattle, sourceFilter L>=5 + Free trait}` -> `CostGatedBlock{suspend self}` -> `PlayWithoutCost{from digivolutionCards, level<=4, hostFilter triggerSubject}` | "naturally suspends itself and plays a card from the battled Digimon's stack" |
| End attack | `EndOfYourTurn` -> `Attack{attacker Free trait, target opponent Digimon, optional}` | "naturally attacks an opponent's Digimon at end of turn" |
| Security | `Security` -> `PlayWithoutCost{isSelf, payCost:false}` | "naturally plays itself from security without paying its cost" |

#### Q&A coverage (KB-INDEX: Q2863, Q2864, Q2865, Q2866)

- **Q2863** (can activate with no eligible stack card): "can pay the
  battle-deletion replacement cost even with no eligible stack card" — tamer
  suspends, attacker still trashed, nothing played.
- **Q2864** (played card's inherited `<Retaliation>` cannot delete the battled
  opponent): NEW "does not fire the played card's inherited Retaliation against
  the battled opponent" — BT1-083 (Free L6) over BT2-074 Devimon (`<Retaliation>`
  inherited) is deleted attacking suspended BT5-086; Devimon is played, BT1-083
  trashed, and BT5-086 survives. Engine handles it correctly.
- **Q2865** (suspended Free Digimon cannot attack): "does not attack with a
  suspended or non-Free Digimon".
- **Q2866** (two copies -> one attack): NEW "performs only one attack when two
  copies trigger with two Free Digimon" — 2 Tamers + 2 unsuspended Free Digimon
  vs 2 suspended opponents; exactly one Free suspends and exactly one opponent is
  deleted. Note: the assertion proves the observable ruling outcome (one attack);
  it does not, on its own, distinguish "both triggered, one attacked" from "only
  one triggered". Engine result matches the ruling.

#### Trait / filter verification

`[Free]` is an attribute-trait (`cardData.staticTraitsOf` = forms ∪ attributes ∪
types). Confirmed the fixtures are honest, not passing by a disabled filter:
BT8-038 Magnamon and BT1-083 GranKuwagamon carry attribute `Free`; the
negative NON_FREE fixture BT1-032 Frigimon is `Vaccine`. Level and Free-trait
source gates are genuinely enforced.

stack) plus a comparative permanent in the same fixture (BT1-009 over BT4-056)
that is NOT a level-5+ Free source and is left untouched.

#### Fixtures

No Digi-Egg in security or deck; no numeric `security:<n>` form. Hosts that must
survive a deletion check use inert main-deck Digimon or explicit `dp`. Attack
targets use `{kind:"permanent"|"player"}`.

#### Card

kinds ["Tamer"].

Printed clauses:

1. `[Start of Your Main Phase]` If your opponent has a Digimon, gain 1 memory.
2. `[Main]` By placing this Tamer and 1 [Kyubimon] and 1 [Taomon] from your trash
   in any order as the bottom digivolution cards of one of your [Renamon], that
   Digimon may digivolve into [Sakuyamon] in your hand for a digivolution cost of
   4, ignoring its digivolution requirements.
3. Rider on clause 2: if digivolved by this effect, you may return 1 Option card
   from your trash to the hand.
4. `[Security]` Play this card without paying the cost.

Inherited effect: none. No digivolution routes (Tamer), so the coordinator's
`names` / `namesExact` route decision does not apply to this card.

#### Q&A coverage

| Q&A | Ruling | Covered by |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Q2867 | "may digivolve" — the player may place all three cards and still decline the evolution | `honors declining the optional Sakuyamon evolution after paying and placing all materials` (green) |
| Q2868 | EX4-030 [Kuzuhamon] is treated as having [Sakuyamon] **in its name** only, never as having the name [Sakuyamon]; the route must refuse it | `refuses EX4-030 Kuzuhamon, which only carries [Sakuyamon] in its name (Q2868)` — **retained red (`it.fails`)** — name gate now correct, red moved to the CostGatedBlock activation seam below |

#### Clause -> test -> IR

| Clause | Test | IR |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 1 | `naturally gains memory at the start of the main phase when the opponent has a Digimon` (real `runTurn(0)`, asserts the `memoryChanged 0 -> 1` event) | `effects[0]` `StartOfYourMainPhase` -> `GainMemory{amount:1, condition:opponentHas kind:["Digimon"]}` |
| 2 | `naturally places this Tamer and both Trash materials under one Renamon, then optionally evolves` — asserts the Renamon top card becomes BT17-038, the stack holds BT17-085/BT17-032/BT17-035, and memory 4 -> 0 (the fixed cost of 4) | `effects[1]` `Main` -> `CostGatedBlock` with a compound `place` cost: self Tamer bound as `rikaTarget` under a `[Renamon]` host, then Kyubimon and Taomon from trash onto the same bound host; then `Digivolve{fromSelectionRef:"rikaTarget", into nameExact "Sakuyamon", from:["hand"], costOverride:4, ignoreRequirements:true, optional:true}` |
| 2 (decline) | `honors declining ...` — after accepting the activation and declining the evolution, the Renamon top card is still BT17-031, all three materials are in the stack, memory is unchanged at 4, and BT17-038 is still in hand | `optional: true` on the inner `Digivolve` |
| 2 (name gate) | `does not treat an unrelated Digimon as Sakuyamon for the named evolution` (BT17-016 in hand: nothing placed, Rika still in the battle area, materials still in trash) and the Q2868 EX4-030 case | `into.nameOrTrait [{tokens:["Sakuyamon"], match:"nameExact"}]` |
| 3 | asserted inside the main behavioural test: the BT1-097 Option card ends in hand | `effects[1].actions[1]` `Return{to:"hand", condition ifThisEffectDigivolved, optional:true}` |
| 4 | `naturally plays itself from security without paying its cost` — a real attack trashes security, the instance leaves security and appears in the battle area | `effects[2]` `Security` / `isSecurity:true` -> `PlayWithoutCost{payCost:false, isSelf:true}` |

Registration: exactly one `registerIrCard("BT17-085", compiled)`, no `registerCard`.
`coverage: "full"`, `residual: []` — honest against the printed text.

#### Q2868 name gate — fixed in shared (lane S); a second, unrelated seam keeps the test red

The **naming** half of Q2868 is closed.

- File: `packages/shared/src/cards/effectiveNames.ts`, `parsedStaticNameAliases`.
- Was: the parser harvested every `[X]` in an "also treated ..." phrase as a **full name
  alias**, so `effectiveStaticNames("EX4-030")` yielded `["Kuzuhamon", "Sakuyamon"]` and
  `match: "nameExact"` accepted Kuzuhamon as `[Sakuyamon]`.
- Now: aliases are split into two channels. "treated as **having** [X]" / "[X] **in its
  name**" feeds `effectiveSubstringOnlyNames` only; a bare "also treated as [X]" /
  "(Rule) Name: Also treated as [X]" feeds `effectiveExactNames`.
  `effectiveExactNames("EX4-030")` is `["Kuzuhamon"]`.
- Readers: `apps/api/src/engine/effects/interpreter/matching/definition.ts`
  (`match: "nameExact"`) and `apps/api/src/engine/cards/cardData.ts`
  (`matchGatedRequirement`'s `namesExact` gate) now read the exact channel.
  Behaviourally, Rika's route no longer digivolves Renamon into EX4-030.

#### Retained red — activation gating (engine lane), not naming

`it.fails("refuses EX4-030 Kuzuhamon ... (Q2868)")` stays red on a **different** seam.

- File/function: `apps/api/src/engine/effects/interpreter/effect.ts`, `canActivateEffect` ->
  `intrinsicPossible`. A `CostGatedBlock` is not in the gated-action set, so its inner
  `Digivolve` is never preflighted.
- Expected: with no legal `[Sakuyamon]` in hand the `[Main]` effect cannot be activated;
  Rika, `[Kyubimon]` and `[Taomon]` stay put (`stack` length 0, hand `["EX4-030"]`, memory 4).
- Actual: the effect activates, the compound place cost is paid, and the digivolve then
  fizzles — `stack` is `["BT17-035", "BT17-032", "BT17-085"]`, hand `["EX4-030"]`, memory 4.
- Same family as the Q2803/Q2804 pay-then-may seam (REVIEW-NOTES priority 4). Engine lane owns
  `engine/effects/**`; not editable by lane S.

The card IR itself is correct: `nameExact` is the right encoding for printed
`[Sakuyamon]` with no "in name" qualifier.

#### Cross-file test-hygiene defect found by lane S (BT17-085.test.ts:179)

`it("does not treat an unrelated Digimon as Sakuyamon for the named evolution")` activates
Rika's `[Main]` effect and asserts **synchronously** — no automation flags on `setupEngine`, no
`settle`, no `respondDecision`. The activation raises an `optional` decision nobody answers, so
the test ends with an engine resolution still in flight. That orphaned resolution churns
microtasks in the same worker and starves the next file's `advance.waitForMainPhase`, which
polls on microtasks. This is the cause of the 15 s timeout on
`BT17-019.test.ts > treats [Matt Ishida] as a substring of the Tamer name` in
`logs/restart-checkpoint-collection.log`; BT17-019 itself is clean.

Fix (card lane / coordinator; lane S may only flip `it.fails` in this file): settle the
activation before asserting — `{ autoDeclineOptional: true }` plus a `settle`, or an explicit
declining `respondDecision`, then `assertNoLoudGap(s)`. Isolation evidence is in
`SHARED-NAME-ALIAS-MECHANISM.md` section 6.

```
pnpm --filter @aegis/api exec vitest run src/cards/BT17/BT17-085.test.ts --maxWorkers=1 --no-file-parallelism
  -> 7 passed | 1 expected fail (8)
pnpm --filter @aegis/api exec tsc --noEmit -p tsconfig.json   -> clean
pnpm exec oxlint / oxfmt --check / git diff --check on the changed test file -> clean
```

#### Defects fixed / gaps

- No IR change. `effects.json` untouched; `BT17-catalog-sync.test.ts` is unaffected.
- Fixtures use no Digi-Eggs in security or deck and no numeric `security: <n>` form.
- Remaining gap: the "in any order" wording of the placement is not exercised as
  an ordering choice — the harness resolves the compound `place` cost in IR order
  and the engine exposes no order decision for it. The endpoint (all three cards
  at the bottom of the same stack) is asserted, which is what the rules text
  constrains; the ordering itself is player choice with no observable difference
  here.
- No catalog discrepancies. `effectText` matches the official list, including the
  double space before `[Main]`.

| --- | --- | --- |
| Catalog/rules | 2 | Identity, colors, kinds, play cost, effect text and security text asserted against the catalog; both Q&A ids traced to the ruling text. |
| IR trace | 2 | Every trigger, action, bound reference, cost override and name-match mode asserted structurally. |
| Behavioural | 2 | All four clauses driven through public intents and a real turn loop with exact endpoints (zones, stack contents, memory, hand). |

| Delivery gates | 0 | Worker lane. |

Worker total: 8/10.

#### Engine lane E2 resolution (session 3) — retained red CLOSED

The activation seam is fixed. `intrinsicPossible` in `interpreter/effect.ts` had no
`CostGatedBlock` case, so Rika's [Main] activated with no legal [Sakuyamon], paid its
compound place cost and fizzled. It now recurses into the block's inner actions, preflighting
each one whose target names a selection ref the block's place costs bind against that cost's
destination filter — the substitution `runActionInner` already makes for a `Digivolve` whose
own place cost produces its target. `it.fails` flipped to `it`.

`pnpm --filter @aegis/api exec vitest run src/cards/BT17/BT17-085.test.ts --maxWorkers=1 --no-file-parallelism`
red `Tests 7 passed | 1 expected fail (8)` -> green `Tests 8 passed (8)`.

Both "no legal [Sakuyamon]" tests asserted `applyIntent(...).ok === true` and then that
nothing moved. The activation is now REFUSED, so each asserts `ok === false` plus an added
`pendingDecision` check — strictly stronger, and it removes the unanswered optional decision
that starved BT17-019. The pair now runs clean together:
`vitest run src/cards/BT17/BT17-085.test.ts src/cards/BT17/BT17-019.test.ts` ->
`Tests 18 passed (18)` on three consecutive runs.

Detail: `PAY-THEN-MAY-MECHANISM.md`, "As landed". The retained-red and test-hygiene sections
above are superseded.

### BT17-087 — Marcus Damon

Lane 62. Tamer, Lv-, Yellow/Red, play cost 4, DP 0. Files touched:
`apps/api/src/cards/BT17/BT17-087.ts`, `.test.ts`, this report.

#### Printed clauses (catalog is source of truth)

Catalog `effectText` + `securityEffectText` (`packages/shared/src/cards/data/cards.json`):

1. `[On Play]` 1 of your `[Marcus Damon]` is also treated as a 3000 DP Digimon
   that can't digivolve and gains `＜Blocker＞` until the end of your opponent's turn.
2. `[All Turns]` When this Tamer becomes suspended, 1 of your Digimon gets +3000 DP
   for the turn. If you have a Digimon with `[Agumon]`/`[Greymon]` in its name, gain 1 memory.
3. `[Security]` Play this card without paying the cost. (Omitted from the lane sheet;
   present in the catalog `securityEffectText` and audited here.)

No inherited effect. No once-per-turn clause. No evolution routes (Tamer, `evoCosts: []`).

#### Defect found and fixed (IR change)

**On Play name gate was substring, must be exact.** The module compiled
`[Marcus Damon]` as `nameOrTrait: [{ tokens: ["Marcus Damon"], match: "name" }]`
(substring). Comprehensive rule 2-3-1-2 (which uses `[Marcus Damon]` as its own
worked example) and 2-3-1-3 make a bracket-only `[X]` an **exact** name match and
reserve substring for "with `[X]` in its name". KB Q6101 confirms AD1-021
"Marcus Damon & Agumon" only carries `[Agumon]` as a substring and is not treated
specially. Under the old substring mode the On Play would wrongly select the
combined-name cards AD1-021 "Marcus Damon & Agumon" (not in the CardNames alias
table `packages/shared/src/cards/effectiveNames.ts`).

Fix: `match: "name"` -> `match: "nameExact"` on the On Play `SelectBind` filter only.
The `[Agumon]`/`[Greymon]` memory condition is genuinely "in its name" (2-3-1-3) and
correctly keeps `match: "name"` (substring), verified against `matchNameOrTrait`
(`apps/api/src/engine/effects/interpreter/matching/definition.ts`).

Result after fix (proven in `matchNameOrTrait` test): BT17-087 and ST24-13
("Marcus Damon & Thomas H. Norstein", aliased to "Marcus Damon" in the CardNames
table) both accepted; AD1-021 refused by `nameExact`, accepted by `name` — the
near-name negative required by the brief.

**IR changed: YES.** `effects.json` still holds the old `match: "name"`, so
`BT17-catalog-sync.test.ts` (coordinator-owned) goes red until the coordinator runs
the BT17 set sync. Not touched here per brief.

#### Clause -> action -> test mapping

| Clause | IR action(s) (`BT17-087.ts`) | Test |
| --- | --- | --- |
| On Play select `[Marcus Damon]` | `SelectBind` bindAs `marcusTarget`, filter `nameExact` | structural + `matchNameOrTrait` near-name negative |
| treated as 3000 DP Digimon | `GrantStatic kinds ["Digimon"]` + `SetBaseDP 3000`, dur `untilOpponentTurnEnd` | "naturally applies all temporary On Play grants" (currentDP 3000) |
| can't digivolve | `Restrict digivolve`, dur `untilOpponentTurnEnd` | same (isRestricted digivolve true) |
| gains `＜Blocker＞` | `GainKeyword Blocker`, dur `untilOpponentTurnEnd` | same (hasKeyword Blocker true) |
| suspend -> 1 Digimon +3000 for the turn | `SubTrigger whenSuspended` (self) -> `ModifyDP +3000 forTheTurn` | suspend test (agumon 1000->4000); source negative (otherTamer suspend = no-op); memory-negative test (biyomon 2000->5000) |
| suspend -> +1 memory if Agumon/Greymon Digimon | `GainMemory 1` cond `youHave` Digimon name Agumon/Greymon | positive: suspend test (memory 0->1); negative: no Agumon/Greymon -> memory stays 0 |
| `[Security]` play without cost | `PlayWithoutCost payCost:false isSelf` | Security-check test (placed to battle area, not trashed) |

Durations `untilOpponentTurnEnd` and `forTheTurn` are valid `EffectDurationRef`
values (`packages/shared/src/effects/ir/durations.ts`); no silent-expiry risk.
Registration: single `registerIrCard("BT17-087", compiled)`, `coverage: "full"`,
`residual: []` — honest.

#### Q&A coverage (13 ids)

- **Q2870** ("treated as a Digimon with X000 DP" semantics): On Play `SetBaseDP 3000`
  + `GrantStatic kinds Digimon` proven (DP 3000, Blocker). The engine recognizes the
  granted kind as a battle-area Digimon via `isBattleAreaDigimon` HARD-01
  (`apps/api/src/engine/state/access.ts`). The "can't attack the same turn it is
  treated" and "gains inherited effects from its stack" nuances are engine-level and
  not part of this card's IR; not exercised in-lane.
- **Q2911 / Q2991 / Q6011** (gain memory while opponent restricts non-Tamer memory,
  because a treated-as card is both a Digimon and a Tamer effect): the memory gain
  itself is tested (positive + negative). The "still gains under opponent memory
  restriction" branch needs the opponent restriction card + combined-kind effect
  classification; engine/ruling-level, untestable within this card's file.
- **Q6007 / Q6008** (treated as both Digimon and Tamer; its effect counts as both):
  same engine treat-as semantics; the card only emits `GrantStatic kinds` + effects.
  Covered by ruling; not separately testable here.
- **Q6009** (DP set to 0 -> deleted at rule check): engine rule-check behaviour, not
  this card's IR. Untestable in-lane.
- **Q6010** (layering a second "treated as X000 DP" overwrites cost/level/DP): needs a
  second treat-as source; out of scope for this single card. Untestable in-lane.
- **Q6012** (opponent card with "isn't affected by Digimon effects" chosen for a
  treat-as Tamer effect): N/A to BT17-087 — its On Play selects "1 of **your**
  `[Marcus Damon]`", never an opponent. Generic ruling only.
- **Q2114 / Q4997 / Q5147 / Q5165** (placing BT12-038 GeoGreymon under a
  treated-as-Digimon Marcus via another card's `[When Digivolving]`, gaining the
  inherited effect): these test BT17-087 as the placement **target**; BT17-087 has no
  placement/`[When Digivolving]` effect of its own. Ruling: it gains the inherited
  effect while treated as a Digimon and loses it once no longer treated. Requires
  other cards; untestable within this lane's allowed files.

   until the end of your opponent's turn. (One clause, two triggers.)
2. `[Your Turn]` When one of your Digimon with [Terriermon]/[Lopmon] in its name
   is played, by suspending this Tamer, 1 of your Digimon with
   [Terriermon]/[Lopmon] in its name may digivolve into a Digimon card in the
   hand with the digivolution cost reduced by 2.
3. `[Security]` Play this card without paying the cost.

Inherited effect: none. No digivolution routes (Tamer), so the coordinator's
`names` / `namesExact` route decision does not apply to this card.
