| 1 (Start of Main) | `boosts one green Digimon at the natural start of the owner's main phase` — real `runTurn(0)`, DP 3000 | `effects[1]` `StartOfYourMainPhase` -> same `ModifyDP` |
| 1 (duration) | `expires the +2000 DP at the end of the opponent's turn, not at the end of its own` — after `runTurn(0)` DP is 3000; it survives seat 0's own turn end and drops back to 1000 only after `runTurn(1)` completes | `duration: "untilOpponentTurnEnd"` is a real `EffectDurationRef`, proven by the expiry point rather than by the string |

| 2 (cost reduction) | same test: BT17-046 Gargomon costs 2 off a Lv.3 green base, so "reduced by 2" makes it free and memory does not move after the played Terriermon's own cost (3 -> 0 -> 0) | `reduceCost: 2` |

#### Defects fixed locally (IR changed — yes)

1. **Name references were substring, not exact.** Both refs compiled as `match: "name"`, which
   is `includes()` (`engine/effects/interpreter/matching/definition.ts:349`). The printed text
   carries no "in name"/"in its name" wording, so per the coordinator's naming rule these are
   exact names. Under the old IR a Gargomon satisfied `[Argomon]` (`"gargomon".includes("argomon")`)
   and a yellow ClearAgumon satisfied `[Agumon]`, both wrongly granting ＜Draw 1＞. Now
   `match: "nameExact"`, with two near-name negatives in the test.
2. **The second condition branch was dead.** The IR expressed the printed OR as
   `filter.orFilters`, but `youHave` counts through `countMatching` →
   `permanentMatchesFilter` (`engine/effects/interpreter/matching/permanent.ts`), which never
   reads `filter.orFilters`. A yellow Agumon alone therefore never drew. Rewritten card-side as
   the `anyOf` condition combinator (`conditions.ts:759`), which is supported; no engine edit.
   This is an engine seam left in place and documented in the module comment: `youHave`/
   `opponentHas` silently ignore `filter.orFilters`.

#### Retained reds

None.

#### Remaining gaps

- The `you may` on clause 1 is asserted structurally (`optional: true`) but not behaviourally
  declined. `autoDeclineOptional` is a whole-run flag and would also decline the KoDokugumon
  `[On Play]` that produces the suspension, making the assertion vacuous; a per-decision
  decline would need a manual `respondDecision` sequence that the harness does not expose per
  effect key.
- `sourceFilter` is `kind: ["Digimon"]`, so an effect suspending an own *Tamer* correctly does
  not trigger — not covered by a dedicated test.

#### Catalog discrepancies

The catalog effect text reads `a yellow Digimon with [Agumon]/[Greymon]` with no "in its name"
tail. digimoncard.io renders the same wording (`with [Agumon] or [Greymon]`), so the catalog is
consistent with the public listing; the slash-vs-"or" separator differs. Not edited.

| 2 — target must hold a Tamer in its digivolution cards | `target.filter.digivolutionStackKind:["Tamer"]` | `refuses a Digimon with no Tamer card in its digivolution cards` |
| 2 — digivolve into a trash `[Dex]`/`[DeathX]` card, cost waived | `Digivolve{from:["trash"], payCost:false, optional:true, into:{nameOrTrait:[{tokens:["Dex","DeathX"], match:"name"}]}}` | positive digivolve test asserts BT17-065 leaves trash, becomes topCard, and BT17-062 + BT17-088 stay in the stack |
| 2 — requirements NOT waived | `payCost:false` only; the engine still gates on the digivolution requirement | `Q2873: refuses a trash [Dex] card whose digivolution requirement the host does not meet` |
| 2 — `[Once Per Turn]` | `frequency: "OncePerTurn"` | structural assertion only (see gaps) |
| 3 — `[Security]` play self | `effects[2]`: `Security` + `PlayWithoutCost`, `isSecurity:true` | `[Security] plays this Tamer to the battle area when hit as a security card` |

`match: "name"` (substring) is correct for clause 2 — the printed text says "in its name".

#### Commands and results

```
pnpm --filter @aegis/api exec vitest run src/cards/BT17/BT17-090.test.ts --maxWorkers=1 --no-file-parallelism
  -> Test Files 1 passed (1) | Tests 10 passed (10)
pnpm --filter @aegis/api exec tsc --noEmit -p tsconfig.json
  -> 0 errors in BT17-089/BT17-090; 11 errors remain in other lanes'
     in-flight files (BT17-008.test.ts, BT17-011.test.ts, BT17-092.test.ts)
pnpm exec oxlint <the four lane files>                        -> clean
pnpm exec oxfmt --check <the four lane files>                 -> clean
git diff --check -- <the four lane files>                     -> clean
```

The compiled IR changed, so `BT17-catalog-sync.test.ts` is expected to be red until the
coordinator re-runs the BT17 set sync. `effects.json` was not touched.

#### Defects fixed locally (IR changed — yes)

**"by suspending this Tamer" was compiled as an ordered action, not a cost.** The IR ran
`{kind:"Suspend", …}` followed by `{kind:"GainMemory", amount:1}`. When Tomonori was already
suspended the Suspend was a silent no-op and the memory was still gained — 1 free memory per
Tamer placement for the rest of the turn. Recompiled as
`CostGatedBlock{cost:{kind:"suspend", …}}` wrapping the `GainMemory`, matching the cost path in
`engine/effects/interpreter/costs.ts:646`, which refuses payment from an already-suspended
permanent. The new negative test failed against the old IR (`expected 1 to be +0`) and passes
against the new one. Mandatory (no "you may"), so no `optional` flag on the block.

#### Retained reds

None.

#### Remaining gaps

- `[Once Per Turn]` on clause 2 is structural only. The trigger fires at most once per
  opponent turn by construction, so a same-turn refusal has no natural second window; proving
  the reset would need two consecutive opponent turns through the real loop, which the turn
  helper does not arrange without injected timing.
- The `[Your Turn]` gate on clause 1 is untested behaviourally: the only available Tamer-placing
  effect in the fixture set (BT17-086 Leon) is an own-turn Main activation, so there is no
  natural opponent-turn placement to attempt.
- The module keeps its existing comment that the public Mind Link activation does not carry the
  optional `byEffect` provenance bit into the `onAddDigivolutionCards` payload, so the "by an
  effect" qualifier is not enforced. Left as-is; a rules-based (digivolve-driven) placement of a
  Tamer card is not otherwise reachable.

#### Catalog discrepancies

None. `effectText` and `securityEffectText` match the official wording, including
`[Dex]/[DeathX] in its name`.

| Behavioural proof | 2 | Real `activateEffect`, real `runTurn` through the opponent's end phase, real security check; endpoints asserted for memory, suspension, stack contents and trash. |
| Peer/stack proof | 2 | Real evolution-stack cases throughout: BT17-062 + BT17-088 under DexDorugamon, plus comparative hosts (Pulsemon with a Tamer but the wrong colour requirement, Dorumon with an empty stack). |
| Delivery gates | 0 | Worker lane. |

Total 8/10.

### BT17-091 — Cracker Fang

Lane 63. Tamer, Lv-, Purple. Also treated as [Eiji Nagasumi].

#### Printed clauses

1. `[Security]` Play this card without paying the cost.
2. `[Start of Your Turn]` If you have 2 memory or less, set your memory to 3.
3. `[Main] ＜Mind Link＞` (place as bottom digivolution card if no Tamer in
   its digivolution cards) 1 of your Digimon with the [Dark Animal]/[SoC] trait.
4. `[Rule] Name:` Also treated as [Eiji Nagasumi].
5. Inherited `[All Turns]` While this Digimon has the [Dark Animal]/[SoC] trait,
   it gains ＜Alliance＞ and ＜Blocker＞.
6. Inherited `[End of All Turns]` You may play 1 [Eiji Nagasumi] from this
   Digimon's digivolution cards without paying the cost.

#### Clause -> test -> IR mapping

| Clause | IR effect | Behavioural test |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Security play | `effects[0]` PlayWithoutCost isSelf | "naturally plays itself from Security during an opponent attack" (real attack intent, security -> battle area) |
| Start-of-turn memory floor | `effects[1]` SetMemory value 3, cond memoryAtMost 2 | "raises start-of-turn memory to 3 only when at 2 or less..." + "never lowers start-of-turn memory that is already above 2" (real `runOneTurn` + `waitForMainPhase`, with/without Tamer control, 2->3, 2->2, 4->4) |
| Mind Link place | `effects[2]` MindLink trait Dark Animal/SoC | "naturally Mind Links under a Dark Animal/SoC Digimon..." (real playCard + activateEffect; asserts stack membership and top-card removal) |
| Mind Link Tamer-in-stack guard | same | "does not Mind Link to a matching Digimon that already has a Tamer in its stack" (host with BT14-087 -> zero activatable effects) |
| Rule name alias | `effects[3]` GrantStatic name Eiji Nagasumi | Exercised through the engine by the self-play case below (alias resolves via shared `effectiveStaticNames` + `nameExact`) |
| Inherited Alliance/Blocker | `effects[4]` two Auras, while selfHasTrait | "naturally Mind Links..." (host gains both) + "withholds the inherited keywords while the host lacks the Dark Animal/SoC trait" (comparative: BT17-069 host grants, BT1-009 host does not) |
| Inherited End-of-All-Turns play | `effects[5]` PlayWithoutCost from digivolutionCards, optional | "naturally plays itself from the host stack at End of All Turns" (real turn loop; Q2874) |

#### Q&A coverage

- **Q2874** (can the inherited End-of-All-Turns effect play *this* card from a
  Digimon's digivolution cards?): covered by "naturally plays itself from the
  host stack at End of All Turns". With the IR fix below this now passes through
  the real name-alias path, not a hardcoded card name.

#### Defect fixed locally (IR change)

`effects[5]` targeted `{ tokens: ["Eiji Nagasumi", "Cracker Fang"], match: "name" }`.

- `match: "name"` is substring (`definitionMatchesRef` in
  `apps/api/src/engine/effects/interpreter/matching/definition.ts`), but the
  printed reference `[Eiji Nagasumi]` is an exact-name reference.
- The literal `"Cracker Fang"` token was a workaround for the self-play (Q2874)
  case. It is unnecessary: shared `effectiveStaticNames`
  (`packages/shared/src/cards/effectiveNames.ts`) already parses the
  `[Rule] Name: Also treated as [Eiji Nagasumi]` text and adds "Eiji Nagasumi"
  to Cracker Fang's effective names, which `nameExact` reads.

Changed to `{ tokens: ["Eiji Nagasumi"], match: "nameExact" }`. This is faithful
to the printed exact-name reference, still plays a genuine [Eiji Nagasumi]
(BT14-087 / BT20-089) from the stack, and makes the Q2874 self-play prove the
Rule alias through the real engine path. Test still green.

**This is an IR change.** `BT17-catalog-sync.test.ts` will go red until the
coordinator runs the set sync. `effects.json` not touched. Module still has one
`registerIrCard`, `coverage: "full"`, `residual: []`.

#### Remaining gaps

- The Rule alias is exercised indirectly (through the End-of-All-Turns self-play)
  rather than by an independent name-gate probe; no separate card in the fixture
  requires `[Eiji Nagasumi]` by name.

#### Catalog / shared observations (not edited; out of scope)

- `effectiveStaticNames` also emits a spurious `"Rule"` alias for any card with
  `[Rule] Name:` text, because its bracket scan runs over the whole matched
  phrase including the leading `[Rule]`. Harmless (no card is named "Rule"), but
  a real shared-parser bug. `packages/shared/src/cards/effectiveNames.ts`,
  `parsedStaticNameAliases`.
- Catalog `BT17-091.traits` is `["-"]` while `types` is `["SoC"]`; the printed
  Mind Link / inherited clauses key off the [Dark Animal]/[SoC] traits of the
  *host*, not this Tamer, so this does not affect behaviour.

### BT17-092 — Menoa Bellucci

Tamer, Lv-, White, play cost 3. Catalog `packages/shared/src/cards/data/cards.json`.

#### Printed clauses

1. `[On Play] By trashing 1 [Morphomon]/[Eosmon] in your hand, ＜Draw 2＞`
2. `[All Turns] While you have [Eosmon], all of your opponent's Tamers [On Play] effects don't activate.`
3. `[Opponent's Turn] [Once Per Turn] When one of your [Eosmon] would leave the battle area by an opponent's effect, by deleting 1 of your other [Eosmon], prevent it.`
4. `[Security] Play this card without paying the cost.`

No inherited effect (Tamer).

#### Clause -> test -> IR

| Clause | Test (`apps/api/src/cards/BT17/BT17-092.test.ts`) | IR |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 1 | "naturally trashes a Morphomon and draws two when played" (behavioural: Morphomon in trash, both named deck cards in hand) + IR shape test | `effects[0]` OnPlay Draw 2 with `cost.kind: "trash"`, zone hand |
| 2 | "naturally suppresses an opponent Tamer's On Play effect while Eosmon is present"; "naturally masks only the On Play window of an opponent Tamer with two timings (Q2875)" | `effects[1]` AllTurns + `youHave` condition, `DisableTimingEffect` `timings: ["onPlay"]`, `duration: "permanent"` |
| 3 | "naturally uses the once-per-turn replacement only for an opponent-effect departure"; "cannot prevent the departure when no other Eosmon can pay the deletion cost"; "leaves a non-Eosmon departure untouched and keeps the Eosmon fuel in play" | `effects[2]` OpponentsTurn / OncePerTurn Replacement on `wouldLeavePlay`, `leaveCause: "byOpponentEffect"`, Prevent with `deleteOwn` cost (`excludeSelf`, `excludeLeavingSubject`) |
| 4 | "naturally plays itself from Security without paying its cost" | `effects[3]` Security PlayWithoutCost |

#### Q&A coverage

- **Q2875** (an effect with `[On Play] [Main]` still activates at Main): covered behaviourally.
  Opponent plays BT4-093 Thomas H. Norstein while Menoa + BT17-074 Eosmon are out; its `[On Play]`
  ＜Draw 1＞ never fires (the named deck card stays in the deck, hand size unchanged) while the mask
  is scoped to `onPlay` in the IR. `observe(...).timingEffectDisabled` only exposes
  `onPlay`/`whenDigivolving`/`whenAttacking`, so the Main window is asserted through the IR
  `timings: ["onPlay"]` list, not an observable.
- **Q2876** ("would leave" also covers return to hand/deck, security, breeding and being placed
  under a card): now covered behaviourally. `Q2876: prevents an opponent bounce by deleting
  another Eosmon` has seat 1 play BT15-090 Fox Fire against two BT17-074; the bounce is replaced,
  seat 0's hand stays empty, one Eosmon stays on the field and the other is the deletion cost.
  The previous session's claim that Fox Fire "does nothing in this harness" was a fixture bug, not
  an engine gap: seat 1's play was set up with a memory value that left it unaffordable
  (`maxAffordable` is turn-player-relative). With `state.memory = 10` on seat 1's turn the Option
  resolves and the return happens. No engine change was involved.

#### Changes made

- `apps/api/src/cards/BT17/BT17-092.ts`: **IR changed**. All four `nameOrTrait` refs
  (`[Morphomon]`/`[Eosmon]` trash cost, the `youHave [Eosmon]` condition, the replacement
  `sourceFilter` and the `deleteOwn` cost) moved from `match: "name"` (substring) to
  `match: "nameExact"`. Printed text has no "in name" wording, so the brief's exact-name rule
  applies. `BT17-catalog-sync.test.ts` will be red until the coordinator runs the set sync.
  No behavioural negative is constructible: no printed card name contains "Eosmon" or "Morphomon"
  as a proper substring, so exact and substring agree on today's catalog.
- `apps/api/src/cards/BT17/BT17-092.test.ts`: IR-shape assertions updated to `nameExact`; three new
  behavioural tests (Q2875 mask scope; unpayable-cost negative; non-Eosmon departure negative).

### BT17-093 — Tai Kamiya & Kari Kamiya

Tamer, Lv-, White, play cost 3. Catalog `packages/shared/src/cards/data/cards.json`.

#### Printed clauses

1. `[All Turns] When you hatch in the breeding area, by suspending this Tamer, gain 1 memory.`
2. `[End of Your Turn] By returning this Tamer to the bottom of the deck, ＜Draw 1＞. Then, you may
   play 1 Tamer card with [Tai Kamiya]/[Kari Kamiya] in its name from your hand without paying the cost.`
3. `[Security] Play this card without paying the cost.`

No inherited effect (Tamer).

#### Clause -> test -> IR

| Clause | Test (`apps/api/src/cards/BT17/BT17-093.test.ts`) | IR |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 1 | "suspends this Tamer and gains 1 memory when its owner hatches" (real `hatchEgg` intent, memory 0 -> 1, Tamer suspended, egg in breeding); "gains no memory when the Tamer is already suspended and cannot pay the cost" | `effects[0]` AllTurns SubTrigger `whenHatch` -> GainMemory 1 with `cost.kind: "suspend"` on self |
| 2 | "naturally returns itself to deck bottom, draws, and plays a Tai/Kari Tamer at end of turn" (real `runTurn`); "plays the name-matching Tamer and leaves a non-matching Tamer in hand"; "does not re-run the End of Your Turn effect on the copy it just played (Q2878)" | `effects[1]` EndOfYourTurn Draw 1 with `cost.kind: "return"` to `deckBottom`, then PlayWithoutCost from hand, `nameOrTrait` `["Tai Kamiya","Kari Kamiya"]` `match: "name"` (substring, matching "in its name") |
| 3 | "naturally plays itself from Security without paying its cost" | `effects[2]` Security PlayWithoutCost |

#### Q&A coverage

- **Q2877** (playing into an empty breeding space without paying the cost is not hatching):
  carried by the IR event name — the SubTrigger listens to `whenHatch`, which the engine raises
  only from the `hatchEgg` intent; no play-into-breeding action raises it. **Residual gap**: no
  behavioural negative. Every printed card that plays a Digimon into the breeding area does so from
  an `[End of Your Turn]` effect, which races this card's own end-of-turn clause and would prove
  nothing cleanly.
- **Q2878** (a copy played by this effect cannot itself use `[End of Your Turn]`): covered
  behaviourally. After a real `runTurn(0)`, the played copy stays in the battle area, is not in the
  deck, and the third deck card is never drawn (no second ＜Draw 1＞).

#### Defect found and fixed

`apps/api/src/cards/BT17/BT17-093.ts` modelled "by suspending this Tamer" as a plain `Suspend`
action followed by `GainMemory`, so an already-suspended Tamer still gained 1 memory on every
hatch — an unpayable cost being ignored. Rewritten as a real `cost: { kind: "suspend", ... }` on the
`GainMemory` action (`optional: true`, `abortOnDecline: true`). The engine's suspend cost gate
(`apps/api/src/engine/effects/interpreter/costs.ts:646-659`, `isSuspended === false`) then refuses
it, and the new negative test locks the behaviour in. **This is an IR change**:
`BT17-catalog-sync.test.ts` will be red until the coordinator runs the set sync.

#### Changes made

- `apps/api/src/cards/BT17/BT17-093.ts`: suspend modelled as a cost (above).
- `apps/api/src/cards/BT17/BT17-093.test.ts`: three new tests (already-suspended negative, the
  name-substring comparative case, Q2878); the two hatch fixtures now pass
  `{ autoAcceptOptional: true, autoSelectCards: true }` because the cost raises an optional prompt.

#### Peer/stack comparative case (session 3)

New test: `treats a parenthesised Tai Kamiya name as a match and a same-colour Tamer without the
name as a near miss`. The hand holds P-012 "Tai Kamiya (V-Tamer)" and BT10-089 "Akari Hinomoto".
The end-of-turn clause plays P-012 — the parenthesised suffix still contains [Tai Kamiya], which
is the substring boundary "in its name" demands — and leaves Akari Hinomoto in hand. Both
out-of-set modules are imported explicitly. Together with the existing BT16-084 / BT17-087 pair
this gives the matching and non-matching sides in one fixture.

```
  -> Test Files 1 passed, Tests 8 passed (session 3)
pnpm --filter @aegis/api exec tsc --noEmit -p tsconfig.json
  -> no error in BT17-093.*; pre-existing errors in BT17-011.test.ts (another lane)
pnpm exec oxlint <files>; pnpm exec oxfmt <files>; git diff --check -- <files>  -> clean
```

#### Remaining gaps

- Q2877 has no behavioural negative (see above).
- Clause 2's optionality ("you may play") is exercised only in the accept direction; no
  `autoDeclineOptional` run was added.
- Injected timing: the hatch tests call `advance(s.engine).recompute()` (state recomputation, not
  `fire`/`fireTiming`); the hatch itself and the end-of-turn clause run through real intents and
  `runTurn`. No Digi-Egg in security/deck, no numeric `security: <n>` form.

#### Catalog discrepancies

None.

#### Printed clauses (catalog, `packages/shared/src/cards/data/cards.json`)

1. `[Main]` You may play 1 [Agumon]/[Gabumon] from your hand or trash without
   paying the cost. Then, place this card in the battle area.
2. `[All Turns]` When one of your level 6 Digimon with [Greymon]/[Garurumon] in
   its name would leave the battle area outside of a battle, ＜Delay＞.
   ・That Digimon and a card in the hand may DNA digivolve into a Digimon card
   with [Omnimon] in its name in the hand.
3. `[Security]` You may play 1 card with [Tai Kamiya]/[Matt Ishida] in its name
   from your hand or trash without paying the cost. Then, add this card to the
   hand.

No inherited effect (Option).

Lane brief note: the lane assignment listed only clauses 1 and 2. The catalog
carries a third printed clause, `securityEffectText` (clause 3). It is
implemented and tested; it is not a catalog discrepancy, only a gap in the lane
sheet.

#### Clause → IR → test

| Clause | IR (`BT17-095.ts`) | Test |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 1 free play | `effects[0]` `trigger: "Main"`, `PlayWithoutCost` (`from: ["hand","trash"]`, `payCost: false`, `optional: true`, `nameOrTrait` tokens Agumon/Gabumon `match: "nameExact"`) | `naturally plays an Agumon or Gabumon from trash before entering the battle area`; `refuses a free play when only an inexact Agumon name sits in the trash` |
| 1 "Then, place this card" | `effects[0].actions[1]` `PlaceInBattleAreaSelf`, not optional | `places itself in the battle area when the optional Digimon play is declined`; structural test asserts the absence of `optional` |
| 2 Delay replacement | `effects[1]` `trigger: "AllTurns"`, `keywords: [Delay]`, `Replacement` `wouldLeavePlay` / `instead` / `leaveCause: "otherThanBattle"`, `sourceFilter` mine + Digimon + level 6 + Greymon/Garurumon substring | `arms the intrinsic Delay only for an owned level 6 Greymon or Garurumon leaving outside battle` (structural); `…deleted by a battle (Q2881)` (behavioural negative) |
| 2 DNA bullet | `DnaDigivolve` with `materials.includeRef: "triggerSubject"`, `looseMaterials` from hand, `into` Omnimon substring, `optional: true` | `uses the leaving Digimon as the first DNA material when intrinsic Delay activates`; `does not consume intrinsic Delay without a separate hand material for the DNA recipe`; Q2882 and Q2883 tests |
| 3 Security | `effects[2]` `trigger: "Security"`, `isSecurity: true`, `PlayWithoutCost` (Tai Kamiya / Matt Ishida substring) then `AddToHandSelf` | `naturally plays a Tai/Matt card from Security, then adds itself to hand`; structural test |

Name-reference semantics (`matchNameOrTrait`,
`apps/api/src/engine/effects/interpreter/matching/definition.ts:297`):
`match: "name"` is substring, `match: "nameExact"` is exact. Clause 2
("with [Greymon]/[Garurumon] in its name") and clause 3 ("with [Tai Kamiya]/
[Matt Ishida] in its name") are substring references and correctly use `"name"`.

#### Defect fixed locally

Clause 1's `[Agumon]/[Gabumon]` is a bracketed exact-name reference but the IR
used `match: "name"` (substring). That let the free play take ToyAgumon,
SnowAgumon, BushiAgumon, ClearAgumon, Agumon Expert, Fake Agumon Expert,
Agumon - Bond of Bravery, Agumon (X Antibody), Marcus Damon & Agumon and the
Gabumon equivalents — 30 catalog cards that the printed text does not name.
Changed to `match: "nameExact"`.

Regression proof: with the token reverted to `"name"`, the new test
`refuses a free play when only an inexact Agumon name sits in the trash` fails
(ToyAgumon BT2-055 is played for free) along with the structural test; with
`"nameExact"` both pass.

#### Fixtures

No Digi-Egg in security or deck, no numeric `security: <n>` form, no injected
timing helpers (`advance`, `fireSubTrigger`, `fireTiming`) anywhere in the file.
Every behavioural test drives `s.engine.applyIntent` and asserts after
`settle(...)`, including `state.pendingDecision === undefined` on the new cases.
The Q2881 battle test seeds the opponent wall as an inert `BT1-009` at
`dp: 15_000`, suspended so it is a legal attack target.

### BT17-097 — Return to the Primogenitor

#### Card summary (catalog `cards.json`)

Blue/Green Option, play cost 2.

1. `[Main]` 1 of your Digimon may digivolve into a level 5 or higher Digimon card with the [Free] trait in your hand with the digivolution cost reduced by 4. Then, place this card in the battle area.
2. `[All Turns]` When one of your Digimon with the [Free] trait would be deleted other than by one of your effects, ＜Delay＞ ・By digivolving that Digimon into a Digimon card with [Imperialdramon] in its name in your hand without paying the cost, prevent that deletion.
3. `[Security]` You may play 1 Tamer card with [Davis Motomiya]/[Ken Ichijoji] in its name from your hand or trash without paying the cost. Then, place this card in the battle area.

Catalog matches the official text. [Free] is the card's *attribute* field in `cards.json`; the engine's `match: "trait"` filter covers attributes, which the behavioural tests confirm (BT3-014 / BT3-017 / BT12-028 / BT12-030 are all `attributes: ["Free"]`).

#### Defects fixed in this pass

- Deleted three `PROBE …` diagnostic tests left behind by the interrupted session (they threw by design).
- The three red tests failed on `{ ok: false, reason: "color-requirement-unmet" }`: the fixtures had no blue or green Digimon in play, so the blue/green Option could not be played at all. Added `BT17-019` (blue) and `BT17-036` (green) colour sources; in the deletion scenarios they carry `dp: 20_000` so the opponent's AncientGreymon deletion cannot pick them. Test-only fix — the module IR was already correct.
- Added three tests: Q2890, Q2891, and the imports of `../BT8/BT8-097.js` and `../EX3/EX3-063.js` (cards outside the BT17 index must be registered explicitly, otherwise their effects silently do nothing).

No module edit was needed; `BT17-097.ts` is unchanged from HEAD.

#### Retained red (session 3)

`it.fails("Q2889: Partition from a BT16-025 in the digivolution cards still plays its cards")`.

- Seam: **inherited ＜Partition＞ specifier is read from the top card only.**
- File/function: `apps/api/src/engine/effects/primitives.ts`, the `partitionCandidates` block
  (~line 3715) inside the deletion primitive.
- Mechanism: the gate is `continuous.hasKeyword(permanentId, "Partition")` (which a BT16-025 in
  the digivolution cards does grant) followed by `partitionSpecOf(perm.topCard.cardId)`, which
  parses the specifier out of the **top card's** printed text. With BT12-030 on top the specifier
  is `undefined` and the reaction is skipped.
- Expected (Q2889): the specified blue Lv.4 and green Lv.4 cards are played from the digivolution
  cards. Actual: BT12-030 is trashed with its whole stack and nothing is played.
- Not a BT17-097 defect; the module is unchanged.

#### Engine lane E2 resolution (session 3) — retained red CLOSED

＜Partition＞ granted by a digivolution card carries its specifier on that card.

flipped to `it`.

#### Engine seam closed

`it("refuses the Delay when the only [Pulsemon]-text host has no digivolution
cards")` — flipped from `it.fails` to `it` by the engine lane (E1); the
assertion is unchanged.

- File/function: `apps/api/src/engine/effects/interpreter/costs.ts`, `payCost`,
  the routed place-as-cost branch (`cost.destination !== undefined` with
  `cost.targetIsPermanent === true`).
- Was: candidates came from `resolvePermanentTargets(ctx, cost.target)` with no
  requirement that `permanent.stack.length > 0` when
  `cost.detachPermanentTop === true`, so a bare permanent's only card went to
  the security stack and the permanent left the battle area.
- Now: the source target is constrained with `hasDigivolutionCards: true`
  whenever `detachPermanentTop` is set, so the bare host is never offered, the
  cost is unpayable, memory stays 0 and the permanent stays on board (Q2892).
- Red-then-green: `Tests 8 passed | 1 expected fail (9)` before, then
  `Error: Expect test to fail` on the same `it.fails`, then `Tests 9 passed (9)`
  after the flip. Full write-up:
  `docs/audits/BT17-reaudit/DETACH-PERMANENT-TOP-COST-MECHANISM.md`.

#### Remaining gaps

- No retained red remains on this card.
- The reveal is not tested for the "no [Pulsemon]-text card among the 3" case
  (all 3 to the bottom, nothing added). Low value relative to the covered path.

  once-per-turn clause exists on this card, so no reset test applies.

None. `effectText` and `securityEffectText` match the official list.

No. `BT17-098.ts` is untouched, so `BT17-catalog-sync.test.ts` is unaffected.

### BT17-099 — Awakening of the Sun

Option, Lv-, Yellow, play cost 3.

#### Printed clauses

1. `[Main] You may play 1 [Marcus Damon]/[Rhythm] from your hand or trash
   without paying the cost. Then, place this card in the battle area.`
2. `[All Turns] When one of your Tamers is deleted or returned to the hand,
   ＜Delay＞. ・1 of your Digimon may digivolve into a Digimon card with
   [ShineGreymon] in its name in the hand without paying the cost.`
3. `[Security] You may play 1 card with [Marcus Damon]/[Rhythm] from your hand
   or trash without paying the cost. Then, place this card in the battle area.`

Inherited: none.

#### Q&A covered

- **Q2894** (the Delay does not ignore digivolution requirements): new test
  `does not ignore the digivolution requirements of the [ShineGreymon] card`.
  The Delay is armed for real (opponent BT10-069 deletes the owned
  [Marcus Damon]), the Delay effect is present and activatable, and with only a
  purple Rookie (BT1-009) in the battle area BT17-039 (yellow Lv5 requirement)
  stays in hand and the Rookie's stack stays empty.
- **Q2895** (the [ShineGreymon] card comes from the hand): new test
  `does not let the Delay digivolve use a [ShineGreymon] card from the trash`.
  Same real arming path, a legal yellow Lv5 host (BT17-037 RizeGreymon) on
  board, BT17-039 in the trash — no digivolution occurs and the card stays in
  the trash. The positive hand case is the pre-existing
  `naturally arms and activates Delay after an opponent effect deletes an owned
  Tamer` test.

#### Clause -> test -> IR

| Clause | Test | IR |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 1 play [Marcus Damon] free from trash | `naturally plays Marcus Damon from trash before entering the battle area` | `effects[0].actions[0]` `PlayWithoutCost`, `from ["hand","trash"]`, `payCost: false`, `optional: true` |
| 1 play [Rhythm] free from hand | `plays [Rhythm] from hand and leaves a non-matching Tamer behind` (new) | same node, `nameOrTrait [{tokens:["Marcus Damon","Rhythm"], match:"nameExact"}]` |
| 1 exact-name gate (no "in its name") | `refuses a near-name Tamer and plays only the exact [Marcus Damon]`, `refuses the near-name Tamer outright when it is the only candidate`, `still accepts ST24-13, whose static name list contains [Marcus Damon]` (all new) | `match: "nameExact"` on `effects[0]` and `effects[3]` |
| 1 "you may" (declinable) | `places itself after the optional Main play is declined` | `optional: true` |
| 1 "then place this card" | all three Main tests | `effects[0].actions[1]` `PlaceInBattleAreaSelf` (no `optional`) |
| 2 arming on Tamer deletion | `naturally arms and activates Delay after an opponent effect deletes an owned Tamer` | `effects[2].actions[0]` `SubTrigger` `onDeletionOf`, `sourceFilter {controller:"mine", kind:["Tamer"]}` |
| 2 arming on Tamer returned to hand | `naturally arms Delay when an opponent effect returns an owned Tamer to hand` | `effects[2].actions[1]` `SubTrigger` `whenEffectAddsToHand` |
| 2 Delay digivolve, free, from hand | `naturally arms and activates Delay ...` (positive), plus both new negatives | `effects[1]` `Digivolve`, `from ["hand"]`, `payCost: false`, `into.nameOrTrait [{tokens:["ShineGreymon"], match:"name"}]`, `requiresDelayArmed: true` |
| 2 requirements not ignored (Q2894) | new test | no `ignoreRequirements` flag on the `Digivolve` node — proven behaviourally |
| 3 Security | `naturally plays Marcus Damon from Security, then places itself in the battle area` | `effects[3]` `trigger "Security"`, `isSecurity: true` |

Structural shape tests for all four effects were already present and were kept.

#### Peer/stack proof

The new [Rhythm] test is the comparative case: [Rhythm] (BT17-089) and a
non-matching Tamer ([Takato Matsuki], BT17-080) sit in the same hand; only
[Rhythm] is played, Takato is the sole card left in hand, and the memory delta
(3 -> 0) proves only the Option's own cost was paid while Rhythm's cost 4 was
waived. The two Delay negatives are real evolution-stack cases (a legal Lv5
yellow host and an illegal purple Rookie host under the same effect).

#### Commands and results

```

=> 14 passed (14)
```

(oxfmt reformatted one block in `BT17-098.test.ts`; re-checked clean).

`pnpm --filter @aegis/api exec tsc --noEmit -p tsconfig.json`: 11 errors, all in
other lanes' files (`BT17-008.test.ts` readonly `under` tuples,
`BT17-011.test.ts` `Permanent.dp`, `BT17-092.test.ts` `settle({maxTicks})`).
Zero errors in `BT17-098.test.ts` or `BT17-099.test.ts`. Log:
`docs/audits/BT17-reaudit/logs/BT17-098-099-tsc.log`.

#### Defects fixed / retained red

**IR defect fixed (coordinator correction).** The printed Main clause reads
"play 1 [Marcus Damon]/[Rhythm]" and the Security clause "play 1 card with
[Marcus Damon]/[Rhythm]" — neither carries "in its name", so per the set-wide
rule both filters must be exact-name. `effects[0].actions[0]` and
`effects[3].actions[0]` were `match: "name"` (substring) and are now
`match: "nameExact"`. The `[ShineGreymon] in its name` Delay filter keeps
`match: "name"` (substring), as printed.

The fix is proven discriminating: flipping the two refs back to `"name"` turns
both new near-name negatives red (2 failed | 12 passed), and restoring
`"nameExact"` returns 14/14.

##### Correction to the reported collision set

The coordinator named AD1-021 "Marcus Damon & Agumon" and ST24-13 "Marcus Damon
& Thomas H. Norstein" as the substring collisions. Only **AD1-021** is a
collision. **ST24-13 is a legitimate [Marcus Damon]**: it is listed in
`packages/shared/src/cards/effectiveNames.ts`
(`STATIC_NAME_ALIASES_BY_CARD_ID["ST24-13"] = ["Marcus Damon", "Thomas H.
Norstein"]`), and `effectiveStaticNames` feeds the `names` array that
`nameExact` compares against
(`apps/api/src/engine/effects/interpreter/matching/definition.ts`, ~line 330 and
line 355). So ST24-13 answers to the exact-name gate by the combined-name Tamer
rule and must stay playable. AD1-021 carries no alias entry and is correctly
refused.

The tests reflect this: the two negatives use AD1-021, and a third new test
asserts ST24-13 is still played for free (memory 3 -> 0). No engine change was
made and none is needed here.

No retained red on this card.

Note on method: an earlier draft of the two negatives armed the Delay with
`s.engine.grantKeyword?.(...)`, which does not exist on the engine; the optional
call silently no-opped and the negatives were vacuous. Both were rewritten to
arm the Delay through the real opponent-effect Tamer deletion and now assert
`expect(delay).toBeDefined()` before activating.

#### Remaining gaps

- The `[All Turns]` arming clause is only exercised through opponent effects.
  Self-inflicted Tamer deletion (own effect) is not covered; the `sourceFilter`
  has no `byOpponentEffectsOnly`, so the printed text ("when one of your Tamers
  is deleted") is correctly unrestricted, but that is argued from the IR rather
  than proven behaviourally.
- Playing [Rhythm] from the trash (as opposed to hand) is not separately tested;
  the trash route is proven with [Marcus Damon].

#### Catalog discrepancies

None blocking, one wording note: the Main clause reads "play 1
[Marcus Damon]/[Rhythm]" while `securityEffectText` reads "play 1 **card with**
[Marcus Damon]/[Rhythm]". Neither carries "in its name", so both are exact-name
clauses; the extra "card with" in the Security wording does not make it a
substring clause. Both IR refs are now `match: "nameExact"`. Reported, not
edited in the catalog.

`types: ["Option"]` on an Option card is unusual for the catalog but is not a
rules-affecting field.

#### IR changed

**Yes.** `effects[0].actions[0].target.filter.nameOrTrait[0].match` and
`effects[3].actions[0].target.filter.nameOrTrait[0].match` changed from `"name"`
to `"nameExact"` in `apps/api/src/cards/BT17/BT17-099.ts`.
`BT17-catalog-sync.test.ts` will be red until the coordinator runs the BT17 set
sync; `effects.json` was not touched.

### BT17-100 — Doomsday Clock

Lane 66. Worker report; delivery gates are scored 0 by rule.

#### Card summary

Catalog (`packages/shared/src/cards/data/cards.json`): Option, Black, play cost 3,
`maxCountInDeck` 4. No errata. Not on the banlist (see discrepancies).

Printed clauses:

| # | Clause |
| --- | --- |
| C1 | [Security] Add this card to the hand. |
| C2 | [Main] Play 1 [Diaboromon] Token without paying the cost (Digimon/Cost 14/Lv.6/White/Mega/Unknown/Unidentified/3000 DP). |
| C3 | [Main] Then, place this card as the bottom digivolution card of 1 of your [Diaboromon] without [Doomsday Clock] in its digivolution cards. |
| C4 | [Start of Your Turn] If 4 [Doomsday Clock]s are placed in your battle area, you win the game. |
| C5 | Inherited [All Turns] When this Digimon would leave the battle area by an opponent's effect, by deleting 1 of your other [Diaboromon], prevent it from leaving. |
| C6 | Inherited [End of Opponent's Turn] Place 1 [Doomsday Clock] from this Digimon's digivolution cards in the battle area. |

#### Q&A covered

| Q&A | Ruling | Where |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Q2896 | Cards can't be placed under tokens, so C3 can never target the token C2 just played. | `Q2896: never places itself under the Diaboromon Token it just played` — **defect found and fixed** (see below). |
| Q2897 | "You win the game" means the player who activated the effect wins. | `Q2897: the controller of the four placed clocks wins…` asserts `winnerSeat === 0`, plus a three-clock negative. |
| Q2898 | "Would leave the battle area" = trashed, returned to hand/deck, placed in security, moved to breeding, or placed under another card. | `Q2898: deletes another Diaboromon to prevent an opponent-effect deletion` covers the trash route; the non-deletion routes are a remaining gap (below). |
| Q2899 | The inherited [End of Opponent's Turn] effect may place this very card from the stack. | `Q2899: places this very card from the host's digivolution cards into the battle area`. |

#### Clause -> test -> IR

| Clause | Test | IR |
| --- | --- | --- |
| C1 | `moves the checked card to hand, leaving trash and battle area untouched` — real seat-1 attack into a two-card security stack; asserts hand +1 containing the exact instance, trash clear, no battle-area permanent, remaining security stack `[BT1-009]`, no pending decision. | `{ trigger: "Security", actions: [{ kind: "AddToHandSelf" }] }` |
| C2 | `plays a 3000 DP Diaboromon Token…` — real `playCard` intent; asserts exactly one `TOKEN-Diaboromon` permanent at 3000 DP and the shared token definition (Lv6/cost 14/White/Mega/Unknown/Unidentified/isToken). | `PlayToken { tokens: ["Diaboromon"], count: 1, payCost: false }` |
| C3 | same test — clock is the **bottom** stack entry of the clock-free Diaboromon, absent from the Diaboromon that already held a clock, gone from hand, not trashed, memory 10 -> 7 (cost 3 paid, token free). | `PlaceUnder { position: "bottom", underFilter }` |
| C3 / Q2896 | `Q2896: never places itself under the Diaboromon Token…` — only the freshly played token is on the field; asserts the token stack stays empty and the used Option lands in the trash. | `underFilter.excludeToken: true` |
| C4 | `Q2897: …wins at the start of their own turn` — four effect-placed clocks, seat 1 takes a real turn via `advance().runTurn(1)`, then seat 0's real `runOneTurn()` start-of-turn window; asserts `gameOver` and `winnerSeat === 0`. Negative: `does not win with only three clocks…` runs the same real window and asserts `gameOver === false`, `winnerSeat === -1`. | `WinGame { winner: "controller", condition: permanentCount >= 4 nameExact "Doomsday Clock" }` |
| C5 | `Q2898: deletes another Diaboromon to prevent an opponent-effect deletion` — opponent plays BT17-017 ([On Play] delete); asserts the host survives with its stack intact, the other Diaboromon is in the trash, the clock is not. Negative: `cannot prevent the leave when no other Diaboromon can pay the cost` — host leaves and the clock is trashed. | `Replacement { event: "wouldLeavePlay", Prevent cost deleteOwn (excludeSelf, name Diaboromon) }` |
| C6 | `Q2899: places this very card…` — full opponent turn via `advance().runTurn(1)`; asserts the clock is a battle-area permanent with `placedByEffect === true` (so §17-1-3-2-2 keeps the Option on the field), the host's stack is empty, the clock is not trashed. | `PlaceInBattleAreaSelf` on `EndOfOpponentsTurn`, `isInherited: true` |

No injected timing is used for any clause. The previous test file proved C4 with
`advance().fireGlobal(EffectTiming.OnStartTurn)` and proved C3's host filter with a
structural `toMatchObject` on the IR; both were replaced with behavioural proofs.

#### Defect found and fixed

**C3 could place the Option under a token (violates Q2896).** The `underFilter` matched
any permanent named "Diaboromon" under my control, and `TOKEN-Diaboromon` has `nameEn`
"Diaboromon". With no other Diaboromon on the field the engine placed the clock under the
token it had just played. Fix, in the card module only: add `excludeToken: true` to the
`PlaceUnder.underFilter` (the same idiom `placeUnder.ts` documents as "the destination
predicate (mine, Tamer, non-Token)"). The new Q2896 test fails against the pristine module
and passes after the change.

**IR changed: yes.** `BT17-catalog-sync.test.ts` will be red until the coordinator runs the
set sync. `effects.json` was not touched.

#### Session 3: exact-name defect and the comparative case

**C3/C5 matched [Diaboromon] as a substring.** Both `nameOrTrait` refs (the `PlaceUnder`
`underFilter` and the `Prevent` `deleteOwn` cost) used `match: "name"`. The printed text has no
"in name" wording, so `[Diaboromon]` is an exact name and BT24-065 "Diaboromon (X Antibody)" — a
different card — was wrongly a legal destination and a legal prevention cost. Fixed to
`match: "nameExact"` in the card module. **IR changed: yes** (in addition to the `excludeToken`
fix above); the set sync is still coordinator-owned.

New comparative test: `picks the real clock-free [Diaboromon] over the token and over a
Diaboromon (X Antibody) peer`. One fixture holds the near-miss BT24-065, a real clock-free
BT17-059, and the token this same clause plays. The clock lands under the real Diaboromon only:
the X Antibody peer's stack stays empty (exact name) and the token's stays empty (Q2896). The
test is a witness for the fix — it fails against `match: "name"` and passes after it.

#### Remaining gaps

- Q2898's non-deletion leave routes (return to hand/deck, placed in security, moved to
  breeding, placed under another card) are not covered. Attempted with an opponent bounce
  Option (BT6-098 and BT15-090): `playCard` returned `{ ok: true }` for seat 1 but the
  Option's [Main] body never resolved on a hand-laid board — no decision was raised and no
  Digimon moved, with or without `s.ready()`. That is a harness/opponent-card issue rather
  than a BT17-100 defect, so no red test is retained here; the deletion route plus the
  cost-negative are the behavioural evidence for C5.
- C4's "are placed in your battle area" is exercised with `placedByEffect: true` fixtures
  and by C6's live placement, but there is no test that an Option placed by a *normal* play
  fails to count (a normal play of an Option never leaves it on the field, so the case is
  unreachable through public intents).

#### Catalog discrepancies

- `effectText` has a missing space in "1 of your[Diaboromon]". Official text is "1 of your
  [Diaboromon]". Cosmetic; not edited, per the brief.
- `docs/audits/BT17-reaudit/RUN.md` states the KB has "1 banlist entry (BT17-100)".
  `data/kb/banlist.json` has 64 events and none of them names BT17-100
  (`grep -o 'BT17-1[0-9][0-9]'` finds nothing). `KB-INDEX.md` row for BT17-100 has an empty
  Banlist column, which matches the data. RUN.md's line looks wrong; coordinator-owned, not
  edited.

#### Commands and results

```
pnpm --filter @aegis/api exec vitest run src/cards/BT17/BT17-100.test.ts --maxWorkers=1 --no-file-parallelism
  -> 1 file, 9 tests passed (session 3)   (docs/audits/BT17-reaudit/logs/BT17-100-vitest.log)
pnpm --filter @aegis/api exec tsc --noEmit -p tsconfig.json
  -> clean                    (docs/audits/BT17-reaudit/logs/BT17-100-tsc.log)
pnpm exec oxlint  apps/api/src/cards/BT17/BT17-100.ts apps/api/src/cards/BT17/BT17-100.test.ts  -> clean
pnpm exec oxfmt --check <same two files>  -> clean
git diff --check -- <same two files>      -> clean
```

`pnpm --filter @aegis/api exec` (cwd is already `apps/api`); `-p tsconfig.json` is the
working form.

### BT17-101 — Fenriloogamon: Takemikazuchi

#### Card summary (catalog `cards.json`)

Purple/Yellow Lv.7 Digimon, DP 16000, play cost 16, digivolve from purple Lv.6 / yellow Lv.6 for 6. Types: Dark Animal, X Antibody, SoC. Attribute Virus.

1. `[Trash] [Your Turn]` When one of your level 6 Digimon with [Pulsemon] in its text is played, 2 of your Digimon may DNA digivolve into this card.
2. `[When Digivolving]` 1 of your opponent's Digimon gets −16000 DP for the turn. If DNA digivolving, you may set your opponent's memory to 3. Then, if this Digimon has a Tamer in its digivolution cards, gain 1 memory and ＜Recovery +1 (Deck)＞.
3. `[When Attacking]` By trashing the top card of your security stack, trash the top card of your opponent's security stack.

Catalog matches the official text (the printed "set your the opponent's memory" typo is in the catalog too; harmless).

#### Q&A coverage (KB-INDEX: Q2900, Q4712)

| Q&A | Ruling | Evidence |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Q2900 | the [Trash] effect triggers while the card is in the trash, when your level 6 Digimon with [Pulsemon] in its text is played | positive: "naturally DNA digivolves the Trash copy …"; negatives: "does not offer the Trash DNA digivolve for a played level 6 Digimon lacking [Pulsemon] in its text (Q2900)" and "stays in the trash when the opponent plays the same level 6 Pulsemon-text Digimon on their turn" |
| Q4712 | the Tamer branch grants 1 memory and ＜Recovery +1 (Deck)＞ even when the DNA "if" is not met | "[When Digivolving] on a normal digivolve applies −16000 DP and, with a Tamer in the stack, gains 1 memory and recovers 1 security (Q4712)" — memory 10 − 6 + 1 = 5 and the deck card reaches the security stack, with no DNA digivolution |

#### Clause → test → IR

| Clause | Test | IR |
| --- | --- | --- |
| [Trash] [Your Turn] watcher | positive DNA test: BT17-040 played → the trash copy becomes the battle-area top card over BT17-069 + BT17-040 as its digivolution cards, and leaves the trash | `effects[0]` `trigger: "YourTurn"`, `isFromTrash: true`, SubTrigger `whenPlayed`, `sourceFilter { controller: "mine", levels: [6], nameOrTrait [Pulsemon] text }` |
| … source filter, "your", "[Your Turn]" | Q2900 negative (BT1-080, Lv.6 without Pulsemon text) and the opponent-turn negative (opponent plays the very same BT17-040) | same `sourceFilter` plus the `YourTurn` trigger |
| "2 of your Digimon may DNA digivolve into this card" | positive DNA test asserts both materials end up in the stack | SubTrigger `actions[0]` DnaDigivolve, `materials.count: 2`, `into { zone: "trash", isSelfRef: true }`, `optional: true` |
| −16000 DP for the turn | Q4712 test: opponent's 20000 DP Digimon reads 4000 | `effects[1].actions[0]` ModifyDP −16000 `forTheTurn` |
| DNA-only "set your opponent's memory to 3" | positive DNA test: memory ends at −3 (seat 1 holds 3) | `effects[1].actions[1]` SetMemory `value: 3`, `controller: "opponent"`, `condition isDnaDigivolving`, `optional: true`; also asserted on the runtime-compiled card |
| Tamer branch: gain 1 memory + ＜Recovery +1 (Deck)＞ | Q4712 test | `effects[1].actions[2..3]`, both gated on `selfDigivolutionStackMatchesFilter { kind: ["Tamer"] }` |
| [When Attacking] security trade | "[When Attacking] trashes own top security to trash opponent's top security" (cost paid) and "[When Attacking] leaves the opponent's security alone when its security cost cannot be paid" (empty own security: only the attack's own security check consumes 1 of the opponent's 2 cards) | `effects[2]` SecurityManipulation `trashTop` on the opponent with a `trash` cost, `optional: true`, `abortOnDecline: true` |

Peer/stack proof: the DNA test builds a real BT17-069 + BT17-040 stack under the card and asserts both digivolution cards; the Q4712 test digivolves onto a purple Lv.6 whose stack already holds the Tamer BT1-085; the near-miss peers are BT1-080 (level 6, no Pulsemon text) and the opponent's own BT17-040 (identical card, wrong controller and wrong turn).

#### Defects fixed in this pass

None in the module — `BT17-101.ts` is unchanged from HEAD and every printed clause behaves as printed. Test file gained two coverage tests (opponent-turn/controller negative, unpayable [When Attacking] cost).
